# ProcureAccess — Codebase state

> **Status:** v1 (2026-11-16) — the detailed state of the code, task by task, moved out of `CLAUDE.md` (v3, as of S5-17b), because Claude Code warns about a `CLAUDE.md` above 40 000 characters (it was 206 000). §1–§4 are moved verbatim, only split into sections; nothing was dropped. `CLAUDE.md` keeps the rules, the short layout and the everyday commands and points here.
> **How to use it:** read the section of an area before working on it (search for the task ID, the file or the type name). A task that builds something adds its paragraph to §2 (a new section, or the section it changes) and its files to §3, and changes `CLAUDE.md` only when a rule, command or convention that applies across the codebase changes. Like every document in `docs/`, a new version keeps only the two newest (`CLAUDE.md` §2). This file describes what is built; on a conflict SPEC, ARCHITECTURE and TESTING win.

## 2. State of the code by area

The sections follow the order in which the tasks built them and name their task IDs.

### 2.0 What is built — summary

Moved verbatim from the paragraph „State of the code“ of `CLAUDE.md` §3 on 2026-11-24 (S6-09, `TECH_DEBT_v5.md` TD-78: `CLAUDE.md` was at its limit of 40 000 characters); its last sentence „Next: migration step 1 …“ was dropped, because the sprint plan and the roadmap say what comes next. A task that builds something adds its short entry here and its paragraph below.

migration step 0 is done (sprint 01), parts of steps 1–3 are built: the module projects under `src/` (Platform, Catalog, IdentityAccess and Configurator with their own contexts; the Configurator and Export endpoints), the catalog with releases, the configurator S10/S11 and the anonymous export, the cookie session with registration, password reset and lockout, organisations with members and invitations (S20, S21, the invitation page), the signed-in header states with the organisation switch; the legacy account screens are gone (S5-10; the settings page in S5-25b, replaced by S60 in S5-26; the profile in S5-28b, replaced by S61); the personal data export (S5-27); the persistence of saved lists with `IConfigurationReader` and the export source of saved lists (S5-19); saving, reading and listing them, with the draft check the anonymous export shares (S5-20a); on the client „Liste speichern“ with the draft kept across the sign-in, S12, S14 and a saved list read only (S5-20b); changing a decision or the selection, deleting a list, its copy to the active release and its export, on the server (S5-21a) and on the client with J1 complete (S5-21b); the weight range served by the API, the `no-store` default, S13 leading to a too-long note and „Noch nicht gespeichert“ (S5-22); share links of saved lists on the server: Platform's `ShareLink`, the management endpoints and the anonymous read `GET /api/v1/shared/{token}` (S5-23); the share dialog S15 with the expiry dates the server serves and the shared list S16 at `/geteilt/:token` without an account (S5-24); the user preferences with `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/account/preferences` (S5-25a), applied on the client by `withSettings` (S5-25b), and the settings page S60 at `/einstellungen` (S5-26); the account deletion on the server, `DELETE /api/v1/account` with the organisation rules (S5-28a); the account page S61 at `/konto` with the export download and the deletion dialog (S5-28b); the session hardening of step 4 (S5-29, rules below); the session warning and the completed login overlay, step 4 done (S5-30); the rating legend `pa-rating-legend` once above the list on S11, a saved list and S16 (S5-31); S10 and S11 matched to their Figma frames (S5-32); the users in the IdentityAccess module: the Identity tables in its context with their rows, the account, sign-in and organisation endpoints, `GET /api/me`, the `roles` command and the seeded users (S6-10a), and the foreign keys of the module's tables onto the users (S6-10b); of step 5, the Workspace module with its own context: projects (S6-11; `withWorkspace()`, S30, S31: S6-13; S32: S6-14), candidates with test status and evidences (S6-15; S33, S34 with K1: S6-16), a project's own copy of a list (S6-12a; S11 of the copy: S6-12b), assessments (S6-18), projects in account deletion and data export (S6-25), ETag and `If-Match` on projects, candidates, evidences and lists (S6-20a/b). The legacy projects `API`, `DAL`, `MODELS`, `SERVICES` and their screens remain until their step replaces them.

Not named in that paragraph, added with the move (S6-09): the CI smokes of the dev server and of the chunk cache header (S6-07, §2.85), the root store (S6-24, §2.86), the reload flows (S6-27, §2.88), the legal pages S71 (S6-28, §2.89), the return to the guarded page after the sign-in (S6-03, §2.92), the legacy proposals retired (S6-23, §2.94), the mail and token hygiene (S6-05, §2.95), the rate limits of sign-in and organisation creation (S6-26, §2.96), the evidence tab S34a (S6-17, §2.107), the assessment tab S34b (S6-19a/b, §2.108, §2.109), the conflict dialog S37 (S6-21a/b, §2.110, §2.111), removing a candidate and the Reader view (S6-22a, §2.112), J2 up to the assessment (S6-22b, §2.113), the nightly backup and the rehearsed restore (S6-29, §2.115).

### 2.1 Step 0, sprint 01 and the module skeleton (S1-04 – S2-06)

**Current state of the code (migration step 0 completed in sprint 01; parts of step 1 built ahead):** backend projects on .NET 10 / EF Core 10 / Npgsql 10 (S1-04) and the API Docker image on the 10.0 base images (S1-06), since S2-21 a Release build that runs as the base image's non-root user `app` and restores from the project files alone, so the restore layer stays cached until a project file changes; the Swagger UI and the OpenAPI documents (`/swagger`) are served only in `Development` (`ConfigureSwaggerExplorer()`, O-S3-2, #183; elsewhere, staging included, they answer 404, `SwaggerDocumentTests`), Angular 22 with TypeScript 6.0 on Node 24 LTS / npm 11 (S1-09; `.nvmrc` and the client `Dockerfile` carry the same Node version; `e2e/` uses the client's TypeScript version and Dependabot offers only its patches there, O-S3-1), zoneless with every component `OnPush` (S1-10), client Docker image serves the release build through nginx (S1-11; stable branch, i.e. an even minor, S3-01), which also gzips static files and forwards `/api/` to the API, so the release build calls the API on its own origin (`apiUrl` `/api`) and one image fits every environment; `npm start` reaches the API through the dev server's proxy (`proxy.conf.json`: `/api` and `/health` → `http://localhost:5000`), so `apiUrl` is `/api` in every environment (S5-08, #317), root solution `ProcureAccess.sln` with the four backend test projects under `tests/` on xUnit v3 and Microsoft.Testing.Platform (S1-12); integration fixture with Testcontainers PostgreSQL 17, Respawn, `WebApplicationFactory`, test authentication handler (`X-Test-User` header) and `FakeTimeProvider`; the API registers `TimeProvider.System`, read so far by the log retention and the usage-event writer (S1-13, S1-22, S1-23; `ClockTests` checks that the fixture's clock is the one the API resolves); tagged tests for ARC-06 (Unit) and OPS-03 (Integration) (S1-14; `active` since the gate, S1-21); no AutoMapper since S3-07 (ADR-015, #31): entity–DTO maps are hand-written extension methods and `Expression` projections in `DAL/MODELS.ProcureAccess/Entities/Mapping/` (`CatalogMappings`, `AccountMappings`; services map through `BaseService.ToDto`), and ARC-06 (TESTING_v27) checks with `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Mapping/` that every map sets every target member or names it in its ignore list in `MapCases` — a new map gets a case there; the root `Directory.Build.props` is empty but stays (imported by `src/`, copied by the API Dockerfile); no build warnings since S3-08 (#118): `src/Directory.Build.props` sets `TreatWarningsAsErrors` (same NuGet audit exception), so a module project fails on a warning also locally; the legacy projects are held at zero by CI only; legacy nullable fixes use `= null!` where the value is always set (entities, settings, DTOs: `string?` on a request DTO would drop MVC's implicit required check) and `?` only where the code already handles null; CAT-10 API characterisation of the criteria filter on the `AddSeedData` seed, expected sets in `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/Fixtures/cat-10-criteria-filter.json` (S1-15; since S4-13 only this API part remains, until the legacy filter goes (O-S4-5 (a)): the key compares each field with its JSON kind (#91), and `ExistingCatalogSeed` — `Api.ExistingCatalogSeed` in integration tests — copies every seeded value from the legacy context's design-time model instead of a hand-written list (#92)), frontend tests on Vitest (`@angular/build:unit-test`, jsdom) with Angular Testing Library; every `TestBed` gets zoneless change detection from `src/test-providers.ts` (`providersFile`); the generated Karma specs are deleted (S1-16); the first component test `delete-account-dialog.spec.ts` is the example of the convention in §9, with the real German texts from `src/app/testing/provide-test-translations.ts`, and `npm run test:coverage` writes a coverage report (`@vitest/coverage-v8`, text summary + lcov in `coverage/`, not gated; the `models/` folders under `src/app` (types, DTOs, enums, constant maps) are left out since S2-23; environment files lie outside `src/app` and were never counted) (S1-17); Playwright with `@axe-core/playwright` in `e2e/` (own `package.json`), axe helper `e2e/support/axe.ts` with the central suppression list `e2e/support/axe-suppressions.ts` (every entry links an issue), E2E stack = `docker-compose.yml` + `docker-compose.e2e.yml` (database in memory, API still `Development`, same host ports as the dev stack), smoke test and helper self-test (S1-18); journey J1 `e2e/journeys/j1-configurator.spec.ts`, characterising the legacy filter until S4-13 (S1-19; three axe findings fixed, #53–#55) — since S4-13 it runs on S10 and the test release `T.1` with the keyboard only (A11Y-02 `active` for J1 in TESTING_v45): it reaches every control with Tab through its `tabTo` helper (never `click`, `tap` or the mouse), checks an exclusion by each higher layer with its reason, axe in both appearances on every state, and at the hand-over the criteria request and answer of `T.1`; since S4-22 it goes on through S11 (K5 change of 99.1 to „Hinweis“, its rationale in the detail panel, a note, 99.7 left out, the „Geprüft nach …“ card) and S13, downloads the XLSX and opens it with `e2e/support/xlsx.ts` (Node's `zlib` only, no XLSX package, O-S4-76; self-test `xlsx-helper.spec.ts`) to check the file name, the header row and the changed rows; it reads the name of an S11 include checkbox through its `aria-labelledby` texts, because Playwright's name computation of it crashes Firefox once axe has run on S11 (TESTING_v52 V165); the axe suppression list `e2e/support/axe-suppressions.ts` is empty since S4-13 (#52 stays open for the legacy `/filters`, which no test checks with axe any more); CI in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs stages 1–8 of TESTING §8.2 on every pull request to `main` (one job per stage, actions pinned by SHA; `dotnet format` checks whitespace, style and, since S3-08, analyzers on the whole solution, and stage 2 builds it with `-warnaserror` (NuGet audit warnings `NU1901`–`NU1904` stay warnings, owner 2026-11-05; TESTING_v28 V90); ESLint with the Angular template rules incl. accessibility, TypeScript rules since S2-14; backend coverage with `coverlet.MTP`, merged with the Vitest lcov by ReportGenerator into the job summary; E2E on all five browser projects with a generated `.env` — since 2026-11-05 one parallel job per project, without waiting for the build, and the job `6. E2E + a11y` (the required check) is green only when all five passed; each job runs Playwright in the pinned image `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright` (`e2e/playwright-image/Dockerfile`: tag = the `@playwright/test` version of `e2e/package-lock.json`, plus digest; CI fails on a mismatch, so a Playwright update changes both in one pull request), on the host network next to the stack, so no job installs browsers or apt packages any more — a slow apt mirror failed the jobs even with the retry of S4-01 (S4-01b, O-S4-1 (1), #203); stage 7 and the Figma-link check were placeholders until S1-21; npm install scripts of `esbuild`, `lmdb`, `msgpackr-extract`, `@parcel/watcher` are denied in `allowScripts`) (S1-20); CodeQL (stage 9) as advanced setup in `.github/workflows/codeql.yml` on every pull request to `main` (Dependabot's included), every push to `main` and weekly — actions, C# without a build, JavaScript/TypeScript, `default` queries; GitHub's default setup is off, because it never ran on Dependabot PRs and cannot run next to the workflow; the job names `Analyze (actions)`, `Analyze (csharp)`, `Analyze (javascript-typescript)` are required checks of the ruleset, so rename them only together with it (S3-03, #144); the ruleset's code-scanning rule (added by the owner after S3-03) blocks a pull request that introduces a CodeQL alert of severity „Error“ or a security alert of „High“ or higher (TESTING_v40 V120); invariant gate `scripts/check-invariants.mjs` (reads the catalogue from the highest `docs/TESTING_v*.md`, CI stage 7; since S2-09 it fails on a catalogue table with a different header, reads only test files, counts a tag only in the `DisplayName` of a .NET `[Fact]`/`[Theory]` or the title of a `test`/`it`/`describe` that is not skipped, requires `[Trait("Invariant", "<ID>")]` on the same .NET method, and accepts a protocol only as a Markdown file in `docs/a11y-reports/`; since S3-04 its self-test `scripts/check-invariants.test.mjs` on `node:test` runs in CI stage 1: each case copies the unchanged gate into a small fake repository in a temp folder and runs it there, so a change to the gate's rules comes with a case there (#185, TESTING_v25 V86); TESTING_v17 §8.3) and Figma link check `scripts/check-figma-links.mjs` (CI stage 1, self-test `scripts/check-figma-links.test.mjs` on `node:test`; its `PENDING` list is empty since the shell header carries its tag, S2-15; since S2-22, TESTING_v23 V84: `COMPONENTS` needs every node of a built component as a `@figma` line, `PAGES` holds the pages of mapping §2.2 at their planned paths and needs the base frame as the first `@figma` line, a production file that declares a mapped component's selector at another path fails, and a `PENDING` file with a wrong node or a `PENDING` entry without its file fails); ARC-06, OPS-03, CAT-10 and A11Y-01 are `active` in TESTING_v11 §4 (S1-21); operations basics ahead of step 1 (S1-22): Serilog writes to the console and, through `Serilog.Sinks.Postgresql.Alternative`, to `logging."SeriLogs"` (the migrations own the table, the sink never creates it; settings in `AppLoggingSettings` of `appsettings.json`, so every environment starts, issue #38), `LogRetentionJob` deletes rows older than `AppLoggingSettings:Retention:RetentionDays` (default 90) once at startup and every `RunInterval` (`LogRetentionService`, on `TimeProvider`), `/health/live` (no checks) and `/health/ready` (database) (the legacy `/api/Health` is gone since S2-19: the E2E client healthcheck asks `http://api:8080/health/ready` over the stack network, `e2e/support/global-setup.ts` asks `E2E_API_URL` (API origin, default `http://localhost:5000`) + `/health/ready`; nginx still forwards only `/api/`, so the health endpoints are not on the client origin, #112), `RebuildDatabase` runs only in `Development` (`DatabaseRebuildExtensions`), `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT` of the compose stack comes from `.env` (`.env.example` sets `Development`; `Production` if unset); OPS-01, OPS-02 and SEC-06 are `active` in TESTING_v12 §4; usage events ahead of step 1 (S1-23): table `analytics."UsageEvents"` (date only, no personal reference) and `IUsageEventWriter` with one `void` method per event of SPEC §9.1 (UTC day from `TimeProvider`); since S3-23 (#115, ARCHITECTURE_v26 C111) it only puts the event into a bounded queue and returns, and the hosted service `UsageEventBackgroundWriter` stores the queue in batches through `UsageEventStore` (own context options without the retrying execution strategy): it retries after `UsageEvents:RetryDelay` only when the connection could not be opened, never repeats a sent insert (no event counted twice), drops events beyond `UsageEvents:QueueCapacity` and logs every loss once; tests wait for the rows (integration) or drive it on `FakeTimeProvider` (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Analytics/`); S11 calls `CriteriaListShown` since S4-18b, the export calls `ExportCreated` since S4-21a; PRV-05 is `active` in TESTING_v13 §4 and is the first test of the architecture project; icons self-hosted (S2-03): Material Symbols Outlined (Apache-2.0), default font set of every `mat-icon` (`app.config.ts`); since S3-25 (#135, O-S3-7) the client ships a subset with only the icons of `src/assets/fonts/material-symbols-icons.json` (24 kB instead of 4 MB; an icon outside it stays blank): `scripts/subset-material-symbols.sh` cuts it from the `material-symbols` package with fontTools (MIT, a development tool run in the `python:3.13-slim` container, never shipped; it removes the ligatures of the other icons and keeps the variable axes) and writes `material-symbols-outlined.woff2` and its manifest next to the list, both committed (`REUSE.toml`, `LICENSES/Apache-2.0.txt`); `styles/material-symbols.scss` includes it through the package's own mixin, so the build still hashes it into `media/`; the client `Dockerfile` appends its licence entry to the served `3rdpartylicenses.txt`, because the build lists only npm packages. A new icon goes into the list (a name computed in code under `computed` with its component, an icon Angular Material draws itself under `material`), then regenerate; `scripts/check-icons.mjs` (CI stage 1, TESTING_v38 V117) fails on a used icon missing from the list, an unused one, and a subset that no longer matches the list or the pinned package version — so a Dependabot update of `material-symbols` stays red until the subset is regenerated (owner 2a); Font Awesome and the classic Material Icons are gone, the EasyMDE toolbar gets its glyphs from `styles/main.scss`; the client image also serves the build's `3rdpartylicenses.txt`; J1 fails on any request, `<link>` or `<script>` to a third-party origin (`e2e/support/third-party-requests.ts`); module skeleton (S2-06): `src/ProcureAccess.Modules.<Name>` and `.Contracts` for the eight modules of ARCHITECTURE §4.3 with exactly the references of its table, modules use Platform only through `Platform.Contracts` (owner, 2026-10-30), `Add<Name>Module()` of each is called from `Program.cs`; all of them are still empty: the legacy code in `API`, `DAL`, `MODELS` and `SERVICES` moves into a module only with the migration step that replaces it (owner decision O-S2-1; the mapping is in `docs/sprints/sprint_02/S2-06_module_skeleton.md` §1), and `API` stays the host;

### 2.2 Platform module filled (S2-07)

Platform module filled (S2-07): `TimeProvider`, Serilog with the PostgreSQL sink, `LogRetentionJob`, `/health/live` and `/health/ready`, `UsageEventWriter` and both tables moved out of `SERVICES`, `MODELS` and `API` into `src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform` (all `internal`; `AddPlatformModule(configuration)` and `MapPlatformEndpoints()` are its only public members), `IUsageEventWriter` and `IModuleDatabaseMigrator` in `Platform.Contracts`; `PlatformDbContext` maps `logging."SeriLogs"` and `analytics."UsageEvents"` with its own history `platform."__EFMigrationsHistory"` (ADR-014): the legacy migration `HandOverPlatformTables` only removes them from the legacy model, the Platform baseline `InitialPlatformSchema` creates them only where missing; `Program.cs` migrates the legacy context first, then every `IModuleDatabaseMigrator`; the legacy `IAppLogging<T>` wrapper stays in `SERVICES` until its controllers go; architecture tests (S2-08): ARC-01 – ARC-03 are `active` in TESTING_v16 §4.10, in `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Architecture/Modules/`: the module table of ARCHITECTURE §4.3 lives once in `ModuleMap.cs` (change it only with the table), the project references are read from the `.csproj` files of `ProcureAccess.sln` and followed transitively, NetArchTest checks the types (rules name the forbidden types, not namespaces), reflection checks that a module project makes public only `<Name>Module` and controllers, and `RuleSelfTests` shows on probe types that each check can fail; a `Contracts` project references no other project until O-22 is decided, and only `API` and the test projects reference a module project (owner, 2026-10-30).

### 2.3 Problem Details (S2-10)

Problem Details (S2-10): Platform answers every error as RFC 9457 Problem Details — validation errors of controllers and minimal APIs (bad requests of minimal APIs throw and `BadRequestExceptionHandler` answers them), unhandled exceptions as 500 without the exception text, empty error responses (e.g. 404 for an unknown route) through `UseStatusCodePages`; `type` is `<base>/problems/<name>` with `ProblemTypes:BaseUri` from `appsettings.json` (🔧, startup fails if it is not an absolute http(s) URI) and the names of `ProblemTypes` in `Platform.Contracts` (SPEC §8.6 plus `email-unconfirmed` of ARCHITECTURE §7.1, owner, 2026-11-01; `email-delivery` since S5-12a): set the name as `ProblemDetails.Type`, Platform writes the URI; validation errors and 404 get `validation` and `not-found` by themselves; `errors` keys are field paths as the client sends them (`newEmail`, `items[0].note`; `$` for the body as a whole); `app.UsePlatformMiddleware()` runs first in the pipeline; `UICustomizationController` answers failures with 400 Problem Details and logs the exception (no `code`, no `message` any more, owner, 2026-11-01); `BaseCrudController` is read-only since S3-07: its generic `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE` accepted anonymous requests and are gone (O-S3-4 (c), #90; `GenericCrudWriteTests`), the catalog changes only through the CLI import (SD20) and proposals only through `Proposals/upsert`, `review`, `approve`; API-01 is `active` in TESTING_v18 §4.6 (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Api/`); the legacy actions still send `application/json` because of their `[Produces]` attribute.

### 2.4 Plural forms (S2-12)

Plural forms (S2-12, SPEC SD25): pipe `translatePlural` (`shared/pipes/translate-plural.pipe.ts`, also `pluralKey()` for code) picks `<key>.zero` (count 0, optional), `<key>.one` (by `Intl.PluralRules`) or the plural `<key>`; first example `errors.summary.title` / `title.one` in `de.json`.

### 2.5 Client mapping of Problem Details (S2-11)

Client mapping of Problem Details (S2-11): `core/errors/problem-details.ts` reads the problem type from `…/problems/<name>`, maps it to a UX key (`PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS`; so far only `email-unconfirmed` → `auth.confirm.required`, other types keep the generic snackbar text until their step; the key of every type is in SPEC §8.6 v2.8 and UX copy v17 §6.3, S4-02) and binds the `errors` of a `validation` problem to form controls (`applyServerErrors`: error `server` on the control → `mat-error` with `errors.field.server`; Material sets `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`); `pa-error-summary` (Handoff §6.4, on `pa-notice`, the first built Handoff component) takes the focus after every failed submit, which is the announcement; a request marked `FORM_SHOWS_FIELD_ERRORS` gets no snackbar for a validation problem; example form: the legacy product proposal (`features/products/proposal/`); spacing tokens `--pa-space-*` and `.cdk-visually-hidden` in `styles/main.scss`; API-02 is `active` in TESTING_v19 §4.6 (FE part; E2E with the first rebuilt form, V80). `de.json` from the UX copy (S2-13): `scripts/check-i18n.mjs` (CI stage 1, TESTING_v20 V81; self-test `scripts/check-i18n.test.mjs` on `node:test`) reads the tables of §5–§6 of the highest `ux_copy_procureaccess_v*.md` and checks that every key of the areas `common`, `shell`, `errors` and, since S2-15, `legal` (`AREAS` in the script; add a root with the step that builds its screens — since S4-10 an area can also be a deeper prefix such as `criteria.select`, when only part of a root has its screen) is in `de.json` with exactly its text, that every lowercase key of `de.json` is a UX copy key with the same text (uppercase roots are legacy), that every text is reachable under its own key, and that every literal key before `| translate` / `| translatePlural` in the client resolves (keys computed at runtime are printed as notices); `--write` copies the texts of the areas into `de.json` and rejects the key segments `__proto__`, `constructor` and `prototype` (S3-04, #182; checked on the whole key before any write and again on each segment inside the write loop, because CodeQL counts only the latter, S4-04, #233); a key that extends a key with text (#101: `shell.nav.menu` / `shell.nav.menu.close`) is stored as one property name with dots next to it (`"menu": "Menü", "menu.close": "Menü schließen"`), as SD25 does for `.one`; `src/app/testing/de-json.spec.ts` resolves every text of `de.json` with the real ngx-translate; `en/es/fr.json` stay untouched (owner, 2026-11-02).

### 2.6 TypeScript lint rules (S2-14)

TypeScript lint rules (S2-14, TESTING_v21 V82): `eslint.config.js` runs `typescript-eslint` `recommendedTypeChecked` (type-aware through `projectService`) and Angular's `tsRecommended` on every `.ts` file; legacy files that a migration step replaces, reworks or retires keep their remaining findings turned off in `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` (exact paths grouped by step M2/M3/M5 — the M1 entry went with the shell, S2-15 — tracking issue #158; the config fails on an entry for a missing file); all other code, including everything written since sprint 02, has no exception (owner, 2026-11-02).

### 2.7 ESLint for `e2e/` and `scripts/` (S3-26)

ESLint for `e2e/` and `scripts/` (S3-26, #161, TESTING_v39 V118): `e2e/eslint.config.mjs` with `eslint`, `@eslint/js` and `typescript-eslint` as dev dependencies of `e2e/` (no `eslint-plugin-playwright`, O-S3-8) lints `e2e/**/*.ts` with `recommendedTypeChecked` — so a Playwright `expect(...)` without `await` fails through `no-floating-promises` — and `scripts/*.mjs` with `@eslint/js` recommended plus `no-floating-promises` and `no-misused-promises` (type information from `e2e/tsconfig.scripts.json`; `test`/`describe`/`it`/`suite` of `node:test` are safe calls; `no-undef` knows only `console` and `process` — add a Node global there when a script needs one); `npm run lint` in `e2e/` starts ESLint in the repository root with that config, because ESLint lints nothing outside the folder it starts from and `scripts/` stays without a `package.json`; CI stage 1 runs it after the e2e type check; no exception list.

### 2.8 App shell (S2-15)

App shell (S2-15, R-SHL-01/02/06): `App` renders skip link `shell.skipLink` (first focusable element, focuses `main#main` without changing the URL) → `pa-header` → `<main id="main" tabindex="-1">` → `pa-footer` inside a `mat-sidenav-container`; `pa-header` (`layout/header/`, `@figma` 2017-650 and, for the compact layout, 2039-2131) switches at 600 px (`injectWideLayout()` in `layout/shell/wide-layout.ts`) between the full header (app name + claim, `pa-main-nav` layout `bar`) and the compact one (app name + „Menü“ with `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls="main-nav"`); the compact menu is the shell's `mat-sidenav` (`mode="over"`, opens on the first nav item through `cdkFocusInitial`, Esc and „Menü schließen“ return the focus to „Menü“); `pa-main-nav` (`layout/main-nav/`) holds the nav `shell.nav.label` with Kriterien → `/kriterien` (since S4-12; current also on `/filters`), Produkte → `/products`, Beitragen → legacy `/product-proposal/0` (owner, S2-15) and „Anmelden“, or for a signed-in legacy user „Konto“/„Einstellungen“/„Abmelden“ (interim until the SignedIn states of step 3, O-S2-4); `pa-footer` (`layout/footer/`) links only targets that exist: „Kriterium vorschlagen“ (since S3-21, see below), the legal pages `/impressum`, `/datenschutz`, `/barrierefreiheit` (`pages/legal/`; H1 + `legal.placeholder` until GP.3, since S6-28 from Markdown, §2.89) and the GitHub repository; the legacy login outlet still replaces the whole shell; shell test doubles in `src/app/testing/shell-test-doubles.ts`; E2E `e2e/shell/shell.spec.ts` (A11Y-01 on the shell, both header layouts in every browser project).

### 2.9 Route change (S2-16)

Route change (S2-16, R-SHL-04): `PageTitleStrategy` (`core/a11y/`, the router's `TitleStrategy`) builds `<title>` from `data[PAGE_TITLE_KEY]` of the deepest primary route through `shell.pageTitle` (the start page through `shell.pageTitle.home`, UX copy v15); a legacy route without a matching key keeps the app name of `index.html` until its screen is rebuilt; when the primary path changes (not on the first page load, the login outlet, query parameters or dialogs) it focuses the H1 in `main#main` (`main` itself on a page without one) and announces the title through `A11yAnnouncerService` (`core/a11y/`, wraps CDK `LiveAnnouncer`; `announce(key, params, { politeness })`, a per-key `throttleMs` since S4-10); `provideDocumentLanguage()` sets `<html lang>` on every ngx-translate language change (`index.html` starts with `lang="de"`; the legacy settings store no longer sets it); `pageTitle` is an `AREAS` root since S4-18a (M2.6d), so `--write` copies every variant of UX copy §5.16 into `de.json`; A11Y-03 and A11Y-05 are `active` in TESTING_v22 §4.11 (`core/a11y/*.spec.ts`, `e2e/shell/route-change.spec.ts`).

### 2.10 Start page, error pages and system states (S2-17)

Start page, error pages and system states (S2-17, R-SHL-05/08/09): S04 `pages/home/` at `/` (`/home` redirects there) with H1, intro, the two cards whose target exists (Kriterien → `/kriterien` since S4-12, Produkte → `/products`) and `home.disclaimer`; the cards for projects and contributions and the vendor link come with their steps (owner, 2026-11-04, 1A); `home` is an `AREAS` root. S80 `pages/error/` is one component for `notFound` (route `**`, in place, URL kept), `forbidden` (`/unauthorized`, the target of the legacy `PermissionGuard`) and `server` (route `error`); the page title is the H1. The error interceptor shows the `server` page in place (`skipLocationChange`) for every 5xx answer and turns the offline notice on for a request without answer (status 0); neither gets a snackbar, other errors keep it until their screen is rebuilt (owner, 3A/4); the snackbar's only button reads `common.actions.close` („Schließen“) through `TranslateService.get` (`core/services/snackbar.service.ts`, S3-22, O-S3-9, #160); `ConnectionStatus` (`core/errors/system-errors.ts`) also follows the browser's `offline`/`online` events and goes online with the next answer of the API: only a request under `API_URL` counts, so a static file such as `de.json` cannot hide the notice (S3-02, #171). At the top of `main`: `pa-loading-bar` (`layout/loading-bar/`, `mat-progress-bar` named `common.loading` while the `withLoading()` counter is above zero — every store method decrements it in a `finally`, also when the call fails (S3-02, #168); the counter methods read the current value inside `patchState` (S4-12); since S6-24 the root store adds `withLoading()` once and the slices declare it as their input (§2.86) — one `common.loading` announcement each time loading starts; no skeleton screens) and the offline notice (`pa-notice` attention, `live="polite"` → `role="status"`). `A11yAnnouncerService` queues its messages in call order, because CDK `LiveAnnouncer` drops a message that is not yet shown when the next one comes. Empty states (text + one action) and the button spinner of Handoff §6.2 come with their first screen. E2E `e2e/shell/start-and-error-pages.spec.ts` (A11Y-01 on every state; server errors, no connection and slow answers through `page.route`).

### 2.11 Reflow (S2-18)

Reflow (S2-18, R-SHL-10): `e2e/shell/reflow.spec.ts` loads every page of the new shell, the open compact menu and the offline notice at 320 × 256 CSS px (a 1280 × 1024 window at 400 % zoom) and fails when the page is wider than the viewport or a visible link or button lies outside it (compared with the set width, because mobile browsers widen the layout viewport); invariant A11Y-09, `active` in TESTING_v26 §4.11 (S3-05, #188), so the gate requires it; a rebuilt screen joins its list in its step (S10 and the S11 placeholder since S4-13). Since S5-06 (#312, TD-54) it also opens S11's card `99.1` with two procedure tabs on `T.1` (HR-Anwendung + „Mobile Anwendung (offen)“, which the procedure mapping ties to Software-Test and App-Test): both tab labels fit the header without pagination because `main.scss` cuts the tab padding to `--pa-space-md` below 600 px, and the test checks that each tab stays reachable and in view when selected. Legacy screens are checked by hand (protocol `docs/sprints/sprint_02/S2-18_zoom_and_e2e.md`); the compact shell tests start at `/`. The legal pages' H1 uses `headline-medium` and is hyphenated (`hyphens: auto`) so long compound words wrap.

### 2.12 Theme foundation (S3-09)

Theme foundation (S3-09, SPEC §7.5, #184, #192): `styles/_theme-colors.scss` is generated from the Handoff colours (`ng generate @angular/material:theme-color`, primary `#6750A4` …, with the high-contrast mixin; the two hand-added palettes for the legacy `.secondary`/`.error` classes are marked) and `styles/theme.scss` emits light and dark through `theme-type: color-scheme`; since S4-29 (O-S4-22) `theme.scss` pins the light value of every role of Handoff §1.2 (`$figma-light`, the Figma variables) through `mat.theme-overrides` as `light-dark(<Figma>, <generated dark>)` — the dark half comes from a dark `mat.define-theme` of the same palettes, so the dark values stay generated until O-S5; `background` too, which §1.2 lists since O-S4-23 because Material paints the page area with it; roles §1.2 does not list (e.g. `tertiary`, `surface-variant`) keep their generated values, and the legacy `.contrast-more` class still replaces the pinned ones; `e2e/shell/appearance.spec.ts` reads the §1.2 table of the highest Handoff and compares every role resolved in the light appearance, so a changed Figma value fails until `$figma-light` follows. `AppearanceService` (`core/a11y/appearance.ts`, `provideAppearance()`) sets `theme-light`/`theme-dark` on `<html>` from `prefers-color-scheme`, follows changes without reload and uses no browser storage; `setExplicit()` is the override for an explicit choice — until S60 only the legacy settings store calls it (dark-mode toggle, a signed-in user's saved setting; a value it stored in `localStorage` is not applied, owner D3). `provideAppearance()` also sets `MATERIAL_ANIMATIONS` (`animationsDisabled`) from `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` (read once at start); `@angular/animations` and `provideAnimationsAsync()` are gone. Focus: `mat.strong-focus-indicators` (3 px, `--mat-sys-secondary`) for Material components, and `main.scss` gives every other `a`, `button`, `[tabindex]` the same outline on `:focus-visible` (elements with a `.mat-focus-indicator` are left out, so no second ring). Font: Roboto 400/500 from `@fontsource/roboto` (OFL-1.1, `angular.json` styles, bundled into `media/`; the per-weight files carry the `unicode-range` of every subset); Poppins and `LICENSES/OFL-1.1.txt` are gone. H1 of S04 and S80: `headline-small` below 600 px, `headline-large` from 600 px. Test double for media queries: `src/app/testing/fake-media-matcher.ts`; E2E `e2e/shell/appearance.spec.ts`.

### 2.13 Dark appearance in axe (S3-10)

Dark appearance in axe (S3-10, A11Y-08 `active` in TESTING_v29 §4.11): `expectNoAxeViolations` (`e2e/support/axe.ts`) checks every state in both appearances — first the one the page shows, then it emulates the other `prefers-color-scheme`, waits for the `theme-*` class and the colour transitions, runs axe with the same suppressions and switches back; reports are `axe-<state>-<appearance>.json`, and a test that calls it carries `INV:A11Y-08`. The first run found no dark contrast violation (also not on `/filters`). The explicit setting and its persistence in `UserPreferences` follow with S60 in step 3; a journey whose saved setting pins the appearance makes the helper fail with „did not switch“. The legacy settings store no longer opens a snackbar when the system appearance changes (owner, S3-10).

### 2.14 Catalog core (S3-12)

Catalog core (S3-12, ARCHITECTURE_v18 C101, O-S3-5): `src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Catalog` holds `CatalogRelease`, `Criterion`, `CriterionRevision`, `CatalogReleaseCriterion`, `StandardReference` (`Domain/`, all `internal`) with `CatalogDbContext` (`Persistence/`, schema `catalog`, history `catalog."__EFMigrationsHistory"`, `CreatedAt` from `TimeProvider`), registered by `AddCatalogModule(configuration)`; `CriterionKind` is in `Catalog.Contracts`. A release is built in memory, sealed with `Publish` and only then stored (`PublishedAt` `NOT NULL`): after that `Include`, a second `Publish` and changes to its revisions throw `PublishedCatalogChangeException`, and anything EF loads is sealed; only `Activate`/`Deactivate` change it (CAT-05 `active` in TESTING_v30, import part with S3-16). The legacy criteria tables and endpoints stay untouched (O-S3-5 C) until S10.

### 2.15 Filter dimensions and rules (S3-13)

Filter dimensions and rules (S3-13, ARCHITECTURE_v19 C104): `UseCaseTemplate`, `AppType`, `ProductPart`, `TestType` (base `FilterValue` with its `FilterLayer`) are seeded by the catalog migration `AddFilterDimensions` from `Persistence/Seed/FilterValueSeed.cs` (O-S3-5 D1; fixed ids, no id generation, keys as in C104 — never change a key; its header and `REUSE.toml` mark the quoted BIT inklusiv explanations); `FilterExclusion` (typed source/target; `Create` refuses a target layer that is not lower, CHECK constraints too, CAT-13 `active` in TESTING_v31), `CatalogReleaseFilterExclusion` and `CatalogReleaseAppTypeProcedure` (added by `CatalogRelease.AddExclusion`/`MapProcedure`), `CriterionApplicability`, `PriorityRecommendation`, `CriterionProcedureText` (added by `CriterionRevision.AddApplicability`/`AddPriority`/`AddProcedureText`), all refused after `Publish` (CAT-05); `Criterion.TestTypeId` exactly for procedure criteria; `PriorityLevel` and the flags enum `Rating` are in `Catalog.Contracts`. Respawn keeps the four filter-value tables, so integration tests read the seeded rows and store a release through the context that loaded them. No resolution logic yet (S3-20).

### 2.16 MiniCatalog and catalog builders (S3-14)

MiniCatalog and catalog builders (S3-14, TESTING_v31 §6.2): `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Shared` references the Catalog module (it is in its `InternalsVisibleTo`, and its own catalog types are `internal`, visible to Unit and Integration); `CatalogData` (`Builders/Catalog/`) has `ACatalogRelease()` (published by default, version `T.0`), `ACriterion()` (`ProcedureFor(testType)` → `PROC-<key>`) and `ARevision()` (`AppliesTo`, `WithPriority`, `WithDefaultPriority`, `WithProcedureText`, `Citing`), next to the legacy `TestData.ACriterion()`; `Fixtures/MiniCatalog.Build(values)` builds the published, inactive releases `T.1` (12 criteria) and `T.2` (11; 7 revisions shared, 4 new: changed, added, renamed 1:1, two merged into one — `SupersededByKey` holds one key, so the non-1:1 case is a merge, owner S3-14) with priority rules on all four levels, several app types (level, weight, sort order), exclusions on all six pairs of layers, Hardware without criteria and procedures, and a release-pinned procedure mapping; keys in `MiniCatalog.Keys`. Its filter values are `MiniCatalogValues`: `InMemory()` for unit tests, `LoadAsync(db)` (the seeded rows) for integration tests, which store the releases through that same context; tests never add filter values (Respawn keeps those tables). `MiniCatalogTests` lists every case the fixture promises — a new case gets a line there.

### 2.17 Release folder format and validation (S3-15)

Release folder format and validation (S3-15, prepares CAT-23; format in `docs/sprints/sprint_03/S3-15_release_data_format.md` §2.1): `catalog/releases/<version>/` holds `release.json` (`version` = folder name), `exclusions.json`, `procedure-mapping.json` and one `criteria/<key>.json` per criterion with its revision content, applicability, priorities and procedure texts (owner 2A); filter values only by key (D1); long texts are arrays of Markdown lines; a criterion names the ones it replaces in `supersedes` (owner 3a, no split); keys are the EN 301 549 number as Kriterienkatalog Teil A/B writes it (`11.1.3.1c`), PDF/UA-1 clauses as `UA1-7.10`, or `PROC-<testTypeKey>` (owner 1a). `ReleaseFolderReader` (`Releases/`, `internal`, System.Text.Json only) reads a folder or a set of files, `ReleaseFolderValidator` checks it whole and returns either a `ReleaseDefinition` or every error with file and JSON path; it checks consistency only — completeness (priorities, a procedure criterion per test type) is for `catalog activate` (S3-17). The MiniCatalog keys follow the scheme in chapter 99 (`99.1` …); `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Shared/Fixtures/Releases/T.1`, `T.2` hold the MiniCatalog as release folders (`TestReleaseFolders`), and `ReleaseFolderReaderTests` checks that they equal it — change both together.

### 2.18 Catalog import (S3-16)

Catalog import (S3-16, ARCHITECTURE_v20 C105): `catalog import <version>` is a command mode of the API host — `Program.cs` hands `catalog …` arguments to `CatalogModule.RunCommandAsync` (`API/Extensions/CatalogCommandHost.cs` builds a host with only Platform and Catalog: no web server, no migration, no background job; it refuses while catalog migrations are pending); exit code 0 done, 1 refused (nothing written), 2 usage. It reads `Catalog:ReleasesPath/<version>/` (`appsettings.json`: `catalog/releases`, relative to the working directory; the API image copies `catalog/releases/` to `/app/catalog/releases`), validates the folder, checks it against the database (version not yet stored; `supersedes` keys are criteria of the previous release, the one stored last; a stored criterion keeps kind, test type and category) and stores the release published and inactive with one `SaveChanges` (`Releases/CatalogReleaseImporter.cs`). It reuses criteria by key, the previous release's revision when `RevisionContent.Matches` (CAT-07), standard references by standard, clause and URL, and exclusion rules by source, target and reason (unique indexes `NULLS NOT DISTINCT`, migration `AddImportUniqueIndexes`); `Criterion.MarkSupersededBy` sets a successor once. `CatalogImmutabilityInterceptor` (added in `CatalogDbContext.OnConfiguring`) refuses every change and delete of a stored catalog row except `IsActive` and a first `SupersededByKey`. CAT-23 (import part), CAT-07 and the import part of CAT-05 are `active` in TESTING_v32 (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/CatalogImportTests.cs` imports `T.1` and `T.2` and compares them with `MiniCatalog.Build` through `Fixtures/ReleaseDefinitions`).

### 2.19 Catalog activation and cache (S3-17)

Catalog activation and cache (S3-17, ARCHITECTURE_v21 C106): `catalog activate <version>` (`Releases/CatalogReleaseActivator.cs`, same exit codes) refuses an unknown version and an incomplete release — a criterion without catalog-default priority (a rule without app type and use case), procedure criteria included, or a seeded test type without its `PROC-` criterion — and names every gap; a stored release never changes, so the fix is a new version. It deactivates the previous release in the same transaction (two saves: the partial unique index `IX_CatalogReleases_SingleActive` on `IsActive` is checked per row); an older release can be activated again (rollback), the active one again is a no-op (exit 0), and there is no deactivate command. `CatalogReader` (`Reading/`, scoped; `AddMemoryCache()` in `AddCatalogModule`) caches a `CatalogReleaseSnapshot` per `CatalogReleaseId` without expiry and reads the active release id from the database on every call, because the command runs in another process — so never cache which release is active; the catalog endpoints (S3-19) read through it and extend the snapshot. The MiniCatalog has one procedure criterion per seeded test type (`PROC-pdfTest`, `PROC-bikWeb`, `PROC-bikApp` without mapping or texts; `T.1` 15, `T.2` 14 criteria), so both test releases can be activated. CAT-09 (read part; HTTP part with S3-19) and the activate part of CAT-23 are `active` in TESTING_v33 (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/CatalogActivationTests.cs`).

### 2.20 Legacy seed (S3-18)

Legacy seed (S3-18, ARCHITECTURE §16.3.2): HR-Anwendung no longer excludes Mobile Anwendung (offen) / (geschlossen); the legacy migration `RemoveHrMobileExclusions` deletes the two `CriteriaFilterExclusion` rows 17 → 12 and 17 → 13, and J1 checks that both stay selectable (`NOT_EXCLUDED_BY_PRODUCT_TYPE`); the CAT-10 sets are unchanged.

### 2.21 Catalog filter options (S3-19)

Catalog filter options (S3-19, ARCHITECTURE_v22 C107, O-S3-6): module endpoints are **minimal APIs**, `internal` in the module; the module's only public addition is `Map<Name>Endpoints(this IEndpointRouteBuilder)` on its registration class (ARC-03), and `Program.cs` maps it on the versioned group `app.NewVersionedApi().MapGroup("/api/v{version:apiVersion}").HasApiVersion(1, 0)`. Every `Map…` call stands before `app.ConfigureSwaggerExplorer()`, which lists the documents through `app.DescribeApiVersions()` (#176; `SwaggerDocumentTests` fails for a versioned minimal API mapped later). Only the new module endpoints live under `/api/v1`; the legacy controllers stay at `/api/<controller>`, and the API reads the version from the URL segment only (#153: no `api-version`/`v` parameters in Swagger any more). `GET /api/v1/catalog/filter-options` (`FilterOptions/`, SPEC §8.1) lists the four layers with `excludedBy[]` (`layer`, `id`, `name`, no reason text) under the rules of the release named by `release=<version>` or of the active one (CAT-12, CAT-19); query names `useCase`, `appTypeIds[]`, `productPartIds[]`; only named values count as selected (also `IsAlwaysSelected` parts); inactive use cases are not listed but are accepted (CAT-18); use cases sorted by German name, the other layers by `SortOrder`; unknown or no active release → 404 `not-found`, unknown key → 400 `validation` keyed as sent (`appTypeIds[1]`), empty values count as not given. `CatalogReader` also caches the filter values and the release's exclusion rules (`CatalogReleaseSnapshot.Exclusions`). HTTP caching (§9.6 v22): `ETag` = hash of the body, 304 on `If-None-Match`, `Cache-Control` `public, max-age=3600, must-revalidate` with `release`, `public, no-cache` without, so the request after `catalog activate` returns the new release (CAT-09 HTTP part). `appTypesWithoutCriteria[]` comes with `/criteria` (S3-20). CAT-12 (endpoint part), CAT-18 (endpoint part) and CAT-19 (endpoint part) are `active` in TESTING_v34 (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Catalog/FilterOptionsResolverTests.cs`, `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/FilterOptionsEndpointTests.cs`). Staging has no active release until 2026.1 can be activated, so the endpoint answers 404 there; the dev and E2E stacks serve the test release `T.1` since S4-08 (see below).

### 2.22 Priority resolution and selection check (S3-20a)

Priority resolution and selection check (S3-20a, ARCHITECTURE_v23 C108; owner decisions of 2026-11-09): `Criteria/` holds them, pure and `internal`, for the endpoint `GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` of S3-20b. `PriorityResolver` lets each selected app type the criterion applies to (`CriterionRules.AppTypesApplyingTo`: an applicability row with that app type and no or a selected product part) run the chain (app type, use case) → (app type, –) → (–, use case) → (–, –) — without a use case only rules 2 and 4, for a procedure criterion only 3 and 4 — and takes the strictest result: level, then weight (none ranks lowest), then `AppType.SortOrder`; `MatchedRule` names the app type for rules 1 and 2. A chain without any rule gives null (incomplete release; the endpoint will answer 404) — never an unexplained priority (CAT-02). `CriteriaSelectionReader` returns the selection or field errors keyed as sent: `Invalid` (→ `validation`: unknown key, two use cases, no app type, an `IsAlwaysSelected` part missing) and, only for a complete selection, `Excluded` (→ `selection-excluded`: a value excluded by the release's rules); inactive use cases are accepted. Both endpoints read list parameters through `Selection/SelectionKeys`. CAT-01, CAT-14 and the unit parts of CAT-02 and CAT-11 are `active` in TESTING_v35 (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Catalog/Criteria/`).

### 2.23 Criteria endpoint (S3-20b)

Criteria endpoint (S3-20b, ARCHITECTURE_v24 C109, SPEC_v2.4 §8.2): `GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` (`Criteria/CriteriaEndpoint`, `CriteriaResolver`) answers the criteria of a checked selection: the requirement criteria that apply and one `PROC-…` per selected test type, in `SortOrder`/key order, each with `resolvedPriority`, `productPart` (lowest-sorted selected part of the matching applicability rows, else null), `allowedRatings` (union of the procedure texts mapped to the *selected* app types in the release, all six without a text, `[]` for `PROC-…`), those `procedureTexts` and `standardReferences`, plus `appTypesWithoutCriteria[]`; enum values are mapped to strings in the module (`level` `A`/`B`/`Info`). A release `catalog activate` would refuse answers 404 (`Releases/ReleaseCompleteness`, shared with the activator), so no priority goes out without explanation. `CatalogReleaseSnapshot` (`Reading/CatalogReleaseSnapshot.cs`, built by `From(release, testTypeKeys)` — also the unit tests' path) now holds every revision's content, the exclusions, the procedure mapping and the gaps; the reader loads the release once with split queries. Both catalog endpoints answer through `Http/CatalogJsonAnswer` (ETag, `Cache-Control`). CAT-06, CAT-16, CAT-20, CAT-21 are `active` and the contract parts of CAT-02, CAT-11 done in TESTING_v36 (`CriteriaResolverTests`, `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/CriteriaEndpointTests.cs`).

### 2.24 Criteria-suggestion link (S3-21)

Criteria-suggestion link (S3-21, R-SUG-01 footer part, ARCHITECTURE_v25 C110, SPEC v2.5): `Contact:CriteriaSuggestion` is the client setting `criteriaSuggestion` of `environment.ts` / `environment.prod.ts` (build time, one value for every environment, no endpoint), read through `CRITERIA_SUGGESTION_CHANNEL` in `core/contact/criteria-suggestion.ts`, which accepts only `mailto:<address>` (without query) or an https URL and builds the `mailto:` href with the subject `suggest.mailSubject` (no body from the footer; the body with the criterion key comes with a link from a criterion); `injectSuggestionLink()` there gives the footer and S11 the channel and its href (S4-18a). The footer's first link `shell.footer.suggest` carries the visually hidden suffix `suggest.external` (mail) or `suggest.external.url` (URL, UX copy v16 U85); `suggest` is an `AREAS` root. 🔧 Until O-6 the value is `mailto:kriterium-vorschlagen@example.invalid` (reserved domain) and the link is shown with it. The release build does not fail on it (it runs in CI, the client image and staging, owner 1a): `scripts/check-release-config.mjs` fails while the release environment holds a placeholder — run it before a production release; its self-test runs in CI stage 1 (TESTING_v37 V116); since S6-28 it also refuses the legal pages while they are placeholders (§2.89). Release 2026.1, part 1 (S3-27, owner decisions of 2026-11-09): `catalog/releases/2026.1/` holds the 183 criteria of Kriterienkatalog Teil A (122; its four parent steps are not criteria) and Teil B (61) with their BIT inklusiv texts, the exclusions of §3.1 (reasons as in §3.2), the procedure mapping of §4 (BIK Web/App have no texts and no mapping) and the five `PROC-…` criteria — no priorities, no plain-language titles (O-1, K-1), so `catalog activate 2026.1` refuses it. It was written once by a converter that is not kept (owner decision 3): from now on the folder is the source, changed by pull request; the conversion rules are in `docs/sprints/sprint_03/S3-27_release_2026_1_texts.md` §2.1. Product parts marked „Vorschlag“ are taken as written (decision 1), UA1 criteria have no standard reference (decision 2), the PDF test's examples per rating are split into positive („erfüllt“) and negative examples with its remarks after them, and its three „nicht anwendbar“ steps allow only `notApplicable`. `docs/kriterienkatalog_2026_1_luecken_v1.md` (German, decision 4) lists what is missing per criterion. Never import 2026.1 into a database that is kept (staging, a shared dev database) before the priorities are in the folder: a stored version never changes, so it could never be completed. `Release2026_1Tests` imports the folder (copied from `catalog/releases/**` into the integration test output) and checks that activation is refused (CAT-23).

### 2.25 Every release folder is validated in CI (S4-05)

Every release folder is validated in CI (S4-05, #239, O-S4-2 (a)): `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Catalog/Releases/CatalogReleaseFoldersTests.cs` finds each folder under `catalog/releases/` in the repository (not a copy in the build output) and runs `ReleaseFolderReader` on it with the seeded filter-value keys (`Fixtures/SeededFilterValueKeys` reads them from `FilterValueSeed`, no database), so a release pull request fails CI stage 3 with every error as `<file> <JSON path>: <message>`; a new version needs no new test. It checks what the import validates without the database — the `supersedes` keys and stored criteria are still checked only by `catalog import` — and accepts a folder without priorities (completeness is for `catalog activate`).

### 2.26 Data Protection key store (S3-24)

Data Protection key store (S3-24, #186, ARCHITECTURE_v27 C112, ahead of step 3): the IdentityAccess module has its first context, `IdentityAccessDbContext` (`Persistence/`, `internal`, schema `identity`, history `identity."__EFMigrationsHistory"`), which maps only `identity."DataProtectionKeys"`; `AddIdentityAccessModule(configuration)` registers it, its migrator and `AddDataProtection().SetApplicationName("ProcureAccess").PersistKeysToDbContext<…>()` (package `Microsoft.AspNetCore.DataProtection.EntityFrameworkCore`, MIT). Since S4-26 (#235, ARCHITECTURE_v45 C131; owner decisions O-S4-87 – O-S4-89, all (a)) the keys are encrypted at rest with `ProtectKeysWithCertificate`: `KeyRingCertificates` (`DataProtection/`) reads `DataProtection:Certificate` (compose `DATA_PROTECTION_CERTIFICATE`; a PFX with an RSA private key and without password, base64 on one line) and the optional `DataProtection:PreviousCertificate`, which only reads keys after a rotation; only `Development` and `E2E` may run without a certificate (plain keys, „No XML encryptor configured“), every other environment — the integration tests' `Testing` too — stops at startup without one, and an unusable value stops it everywhere; `AddIdentityAccessModule(configuration, environment)` takes the environment for this. `ProcureAccessApiFactory` gives every host one throwaway certificate per test run (`Tests.Shared/DataProtection/TestCertificates`). Keys stored in plain text before stay readable until deleted by hand once (O-S4-89); key store, backup, restore and rotation are documented in `docs/TECHNICAL.md` §7.6 (taken over from `docs/sprints/sprint_04/S4-26_key_ring_encryption.md` §6 by S6-29). Today this keeps the legacy Identity tokens (e-mail confirmation, password reset, e-mail change) valid across a new container; the cookie session and antiforgery of step 3 rely on it. Respawn keeps the key table (the shared API host caches the key ring); `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Operations/DataProtectionKeyStoreTests.cs` starts two hosts on one database. OPS-04 stays `planned` until the cookie session.

### 2.27 Account actions (S4-03)

Account actions (S4-03, #205): the legacy `UsersController` (`change-email`, `change-password`, `revoke-sessions`, `DELETE /api/Users`) carries `[Authorize]` and answers 401 to an anonymous request or a principal without a user id; its token-less `POST /api/Users/reset-password`, which set any user's password from the e-mail address alone, is removed (owner decision O-S4-7 (a)) — the password reset is the token-based `POST /api/reset-password`; `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Api/UsersControllerAuthorizationTests.cs`. Since S5-10 only `DELETE /api/Users` is left (§2.65).

### 2.28 Test catalog release (S4-08)

Test catalog release (S4-08, #241, ARCHITECTURE_v29 C115, TESTING_v41 V121; owner decisions O-S4-3 (a) + (b)): until 2026.1 can be activated, the dev and E2E stacks have the active release `T.1` (the MiniCatalog) — the catalog part of the E2E seeder of TESTING §6.3. At startup, after the migrations and `RebuildDatabase`, `SeedTestCatalogIfAllowedAsync` (`API/Extensions/TestCatalogSeedExtensions.cs`) calls `CatalogModule.SeedTestReleaseAsync` (`Releases/TestReleaseSeeder.cs`) when `Catalog:TestRelease:Path` and `Catalog:TestRelease:Version` are set and the environment is `Development` or `E2E` (never Staging or Production, checked on `IHostEnvironment` like SEC-06; the E2E stack still runs as `Development`): it imports the version if it is not stored and activates it if no release is active, so a release activated by hand stays active; a missing folder is skipped with a warning, a refused import stops the start. The folder stays in `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Shared/Fixtures/Releases/` and is never in the API image: `docker-compose.e2e.yml` and `docker-compose.dev.yml` mount it read-only at `/app/catalog/test-releases`, `appsettings.Development.json` names it for `dotnet run`; a deployment from `docker-compose.yml` alone has no folder. The stored `T.1` of a kept dev database does not follow a change of its folder (`docker compose down -v`). The integration host turns the seeder off (`ProcureAccessApiFactory`); `TestReleaseSeederTests`, `TestCatalogSeedGuardTests` and E2E `e2e/smoke/catalog.spec.ts` (both catalog endpoints answer 200 on the stack).

### 2.29 Configurator state (S4-09)

Configurator state (S4-09, R-CON-09, ARCHITECTURE_v30 C116, owner decision O-S4-8 (a)): `features/configurator/` holds the S10 state, no screen yet. `withConfigurator()` (`state/with-configurator.ts`) is a slice of the root store `ProcureAccessStore`; everything sits under the state key `configurator` (draft, filter options, pinned `release`, `optionsStatus`, `reset`), because the root store still has the legacy `appTypes`/`productParts`/`testTypes` of `withFilters()`; `withCatalog()` comes when the legacy filter goes. The draft lives in memory only — never in browser storage (`with-configurator.spec.ts` runs it with `Storage` blocked). `loadFilterOptions()` asks `CatalogApiService.getFilterOptions` (`services/api/`, `GET /api/v1/catalog/filter-options` with `useCase`, `appTypeIds[]`, `productPartIds[]`, `release`): the first answer pins the release, and every later request names it (ADR-002); every change of steps 1–3 loads again, only the latest answer is applied, and until it arrives choices below the changed layer are refused (`changedSinceAnswer`); 404 sets `noRelease` (for the switch of S4-12), other failures `failed`; it uses the `withLoading()` counter. The rules are pure functions in `state/selection-rules.ts`: `chooseUseCase` (one use case or „Keiner davon passt“, which sends no `useCase`), `toggleAppType`/`toggleProductPart`/`toggleTestType` (an unknown or excluded option, an always-selected part — from `alwaysSelected`, never the key `base` — and the same use case again return the same draft), `stepError` (`noUseCase`, `noAppType` → `criteria.select.error.*`), `isComplete`, `exclusionReasons` (names of the excluding values by layer) and `toCatalogSelection`; a change of a layer clears every lower layer except the always-selected parts, also values that would still fit (CFG-01), and returns a `ResetReport` with the changed layer (→ `criteria.select.reset.text.<layer>`) and the cleared layers that held a selection; the store keeps it in `reset` until the next selection. Test options in `features/configurator/testing/filter-options-fixture.ts`.

### 2.30 S10 frame and step 1 (S4-10)

S10 frame and step 1 (S4-10, R-CON-01, R-CON-02; owner decisions O-S4-9 – O-S4-13 of 2026-11-11): `CriteriaSelect` (`features/configurator/pages/criteria-select/`, route `/kriterien`, linked and titled since S4-12) shows H1, intro and the four steps in `pa-stepper` (`shared/components/stepper/`, ARCHITECTURE_v31 C117): a subclass of CDK `CdkStepper`, not `mat-stepper`, because SPEC wants display-only headers — from 600 px an `<ol>` of the step names with `aria-current="step"` and, for finished steps, the icon `check` plus hidden `common.stepper.done`; below 600 px the text line of its `stepLabelKey` input; a step change focuses the first heading of the new step and announces the step label; the page checks its step before `next()`. Step 1 is a `mat-autocomplete` combobox that filters case-insensitively by substring, keeps „Keiner davon passt“ (second line `criteria.select.step1.none.hint`, joined with `&ngsp;` so the accessible name has a space) as the last option also while filtering, shows `criteria.select.step1.noMatch` as a disabled option, restores the current answer when typed text chose nothing (since S4-30, #280, its trailing icons follow Figma: the `error` icon while the field error shows, and while the field holds text the icon button `cancel` „Eingabe löschen“ (`common.actions.clearInput`, UX copy v23 U108), which empties the text, keeps the answer, refocuses the field and opens the full list — one more tab stop before „Weiter“), and announces `criteria.select.step1.matches` (count of matching use cases) with the new per-key throttle of `A11yAnnouncerService` (`throttleMs`, Handoff §3.4: the newest text wins); below 600 px the field scrolls to the top on focus and the listbox ends at the bottom of `visualViewport` (Handoff §2.2). „Weiter“ without an answer shows `criteria.select.error.noUseCase` under the field (S10-E: `aria-invalid`, focus into the field, list closed). `criteria.select` is an `AREAS` entry of `check-i18n.mjs`. Component tests run `axe-core` through `src/app/testing/axe.ts` (`expectNoAxeViolations`, colour contrast off in jsdom; `axe-core` is a client dev dependency, MPL-2.0, test only — O-S4-11 instead of `vitest-axe`); the page test provides the real `withConfigurator()` slice under the `ProcureAccessStore` token. CFG-03 is `active` for step 1 in TESTING_v42; E2E `e2e/configurator/s10-step1.spec.ts` checks the page on `T.1` with axe in both appearances;  E2E tests pick a use case only through `chooseFirstOption` (`e2e/support/combobox.ts`, S4-14b, #269): Material clears the highlighted option one task after the list changes, so the helper presses ArrowDown only while nothing is highlighted and repeats ArrowDown + Enter until the field shows the name; the #52 suppression of the legacy `/filters` stepper was removed in S4-13, when J1 moved to S10 (O-S4-9).

### 2.31 S10 steps 2–4 (S4-11)

S10 steps 2–4 (S4-11, R-CON-03 – R-CON-06; owner decisions O-S4-14 – O-S4-16 of 2026-11-11): each step is a `fieldset` whose `legend` holds the H2 question, with `pa-filter-option` (`features/configurator/components/filter-option/`) per option — `mat-checkbox` with `disabledInteractive`, so an excluded or locked option is `aria-disabled="true"`, never `disabled`, stays focusable and changes nothing when activated; its lines (reason, „Immer ausgewählt.“, hint, explanation) are linked with `aria-describedby`, and an excluded option shows only its reason lines, in place of hint and link (Figma). The checkbox never toggles on its own: it undoes Material's toggle and `checked` follows the store, so a refused change leaves it unchanged. Reasons come from `exclusionReasonLines` (`state/selection-rules.ts`): `criteria.select.excluded.useCase`/`.appType`/`.both` in one line, `.productPart` (UX copy v17 U98) in its own line; several names are joined by `Intl.ListFormat` (disjunction, document language), never by German text in code. Step 2: hints by app-type key from the UX copy (`APP_TYPE_HINT_KEYS`; the catalog `hint` of the answer is not shown), `criteria.select.step2.hint.excluded` (U99) only while an app type is excluded, and „Weiter“ without an app type shows `criteria.select.error.noAppType` under the group (in the fieldset's `aria-describedby`) and focuses the first available option. Step 3: Basisfunktionalität checked and locked, „Erklärungen anzeigen“ (`mat-slide-toggle`, off) shows the catalog `explanation` under each option. Step 4: hints `testType.<key>.hint` (`TEST_TYPE_HINT_KEYS`) and the link `testType.link` with the domain of the catalog URL, `target="_blank"` and the hidden `common.newTab`; no required option (CFG-04 step-4 part; the submit with S4-12). „Weiter“ on steps 1–3 waits through `optionsCurrent()` of `withConfigurator()` until the options answer the current draft, and stays on the step when the reload failed (the explanation comes with S4-12). The combobox keeps the chosen use case shown when a later answer no longer lists it. `testType` is an `AREAS` root of `check-i18n.mjs`. CFG-02, CFG-04 (step-4 part) and the configurator part of CAT-12 are `active`, the step-3 part of CFG-03 is done in TESTING_v43; E2E `e2e/configurator/s10-steps-2-4.spec.ts` (T.1: exclusions by use case, app type and product part, by keyboard, axe in both appearances) finds the switch by role alone, because a name check of a `mat-slide-toggle` followed by axe crashes Firefox 159 (TESTING_v43 V129).

### 2.32 S10 reset, summary, hand-over and legacy switch (S4-12)

S10 reset, summary, hand-over and legacy switch (S4-12, R-CON-07, R-CON-08, R-CON-10; ARCHITECTURE_v32 C118; owner decisions O-S4-17 – O-S4-20 of 2026-11-11, all (a)): start page and header link `/kriterien`; its guard `configuratorReleaseGuard` (`features/configurator/guards/configurator-guards.ts`) waits for `withConfigurator().filterOptionsReady()` and opens the legacy `/filters` when the filter options answer 404 (no active release, O-S4-3) — the one place of the switch, removed with the legacy filter. `CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` (`core/errors/problem-details.ts`) lists problem types the caller shows itself, so the error interceptor shows no snackbar for them (filter options: `not-found`; criteria: `validation`, `selection-excluded`, `not-found`). The reset notice (`pa-notice` attention, `criteria.select.reset.title` + the text of the changed layer) sits in a `role="status"` region that every step renders before it, without focus move; it shows only when a lower layer held a selection. „Bisherige Auswahl“ (from step 2) is a list with one `criteria.select.summary` entry per earlier step and its „Ändern“ (`aria-describedby` = the entry), values joined by `Intl.ListFormat` (conjunction). „Kriterienliste anzeigen“ calls `withConfigurator().submitSelection()` (`GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` with the pinned release, `CatalogApiService.getCriteria`) with a 20 px `mat-progress-spinner` and `aria-disabled` in the button; accepted → `/kriterien/ergebnis`, refused → `criteria.select.error.excluded` (or the server text for `validation`) at the step of the first field (`layerOfField`), focus into the field; 404 → the legacy filter. S11 at `/kriterien/ergebnis` is a placeholder (`features/configurator/pages/criteria-list/`, selector `pa-criteria-list-page`; H1 `criteria.list.title`, „Auswahl ändern“) behind `completeSelectionGuard`, which leads back to S10 when the in-memory draft is gone; S4-14 – S4-18 build the list. Page titles: `PageTitleStrategy.setPageTitleState({ params, error })` fills the parameters of the route's `pageTitle.*` key and switches the prefix `shell.pageTitle.error`, without an announcement; a route change clears it (S10: `pageTitle.criteria.step` with `n`, „Fehler:“ while a step or server error is shown); `TitleStrategy` is provided with `useExisting`, so pages get the router's instance; `pageTitle.criteria.*` come from the UX copy through the `pageTitle` area (since S4-18a). The legacy filter no longer injects `LiveAnnouncer`. CFG-01 is `active` and CFG-04 complete in TESTING_v44; E2E `e2e/configurator/s10-reset-submit.spec.ts`.

### 2.33 S11 list state (S4-14)

S11 list state (S4-14, R-LST-01 – R-LST-06 prepared, ARCHITECTURE_v33 C119; owner decisions O-S4-24 – O-S4-27 of 2026-11-11, all (a)): `models/criteria.ts` types the criteria answer as the Catalog module sends it (`kind` `requirement`/`testProcedure`, procedure texts as strings, `plainLanguage` nullable, `PriorityLevel` `A`/`B`/`Info`). `withConfigurator()` holds `configurator.list`: `loadCriteriaList()` (S11 calls it on every entry; pinned release, latest answer only, 404 → `noRelease` like the hand-over, any other failure `failed` — no snackbar for a 400, S11 shows it), the decisions by criterion key (`included`, `overrideLevel`, `overrideWeight`, `note`; kept for the session when the selection changes, also for criteria that leave the list, O-S4-24), `searchCriteria()` (the field as typed in `searchInput`, applied to `filter.query` 300 ms after the last call), `filterCriteriaByPriority()`, `showOnlyIncludedCriteria()`, `clearCriteriaFilter()` and the computed `visibleCriteria`. The rules are pure functions in `state/criteria-list-rules.ts`: `resolvedPriority` is never changed, an override sits next to it (`effectiveLevel`, `effectiveWeight`; the recommended level again = reset, a level other than B drops the user's weight, an override from A/Info to B has no weight until the user sets one, O-S4-27); `setNote` keeps the text as typed and `noteLengthError` gives `{ max, current }` for `errors.field.maxLength` above 1 000 UTF-16 code units (SPEC §7.2, as the server's `string.Length`); `matchesSearch` looks at key/number, title, plain-language title and the original titles of the procedure texts, case-insensitive, umlauts not folded (O-S4-26); the priority filter compares the level that applies now (O-S4-25). 🔧 The weight range is the client setting `priorityWeight` (`{ min: 1, max: 10 }`, SD23) of `environment.ts` / `environment.prod.ts`, read through `PRIORITY_WEIGHT_RANGE` (`state/priority-weight-range.ts`). Test answer: `features/configurator/testing/criteria-fixture.ts`.

### 2.34 S11 cards and K5 (S4-15a)

S11 cards and K5 (S4-15a, R-LST-01, R-LST-02, owner decisions O-S4-28 – O-S4-34 of 2026-11-11, all (a)): S11 calls `loadCriteriaList()` on every entry and lists `visibleCriteria()` as `pa-criterion-card`s (`features/configurator/components/criterion-card/`) in a `<ul>`; `noRelease` opens the legacy `/filters`; the S11 request marks only `not-found` as `CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` (`CRITERIA_LIST_PROBLEMS`; S10's hand-over keeps `SELECTION_CHECK_PROBLEMS`), so a 400 there gets the generic snackbar (O-S4-34). A card: H2 = plain-language title, else the catalog `title` (O-S4-32); `criteria.item.sourceTitle` from the first procedure text; the meta line through `metaLine` (`state/criterion-card-rules.ts`) — product part name and the clause of the `en301549` standard reference, never the criterion number, with `criteria.item.meta.part`/`.standard` (UX copy v18 U100, confirmed by the owner 2026-11-11) when one part is missing, no line without both; K5; „Details anzeigen/ausblenden“ with `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` (the panel stays in the DOM with `hidden`) and `aria-describedby` = the title, whose id is the anchor `k-<key with dots as hyphens>` (never bind `id` on a component host: Angular Testing Library removes a rendered component by its host id). The panel starts with H3 „Warum diese Priorität?“ = `pa-priority-rationale` (rationale, `criteria.priority.rule.<kind>` with names from `catalogNames(options)` — a key the options lack is shown as it is — and always `criteria.priority.source.expert`, CAT-02 FE part in TESTING_v46). „Geprüft nach …“ cards (test type from the key `PROC-<testType>`): `criteria.item.testedBy.title`/`.text` with `testType.<key>`, `.meta` with the test type's domain as a new-tab link (no line without a URL), and the priority section plus `.note` in the panel (O-S4-33). K5 `pa-priority-indicator` (`shared/components/priority-indicator/`, `@figma 2011-203`; `PriorityLevel` lives there and `models/criteria.ts` re-exports it) shows the level (visible `criteria.priority.<level>`, hidden `criteria.priority.button`), the weight only for B and `criteria.priority.overridden` while the level differs from the recommendation; no button until the menu of S4-16, no „Warum …“ toggle until a screen shows K5 outside a detail panel. `criteria.list`, `criteria.item` and `criteria.priority` are `AREAS` entries.

### 2.35 Catalog sections of the panel (S4-15b)

Catalog sections of the panel (S4-15b, R-LST-01, Handoff 1.19 §5.5; owner decisions O-S4-29, O-S4-30, O-S4-36 – O-S4-39 of 2026-11-11, all (a)): every criterion of `GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` carries `appliesTo[]`, the selected app types it applies to as `{ id, name }` in app-type order (`CriterionRules.AppTypesApplyingTo`, empty for `PROC-…`; SPEC v2.9 §8.2, ARCHITECTURE_v34 C120).  Catalog texts are Markdown lines joined with `\n` (S3-15 P6): `parseCatalogText` (`shared/components/catalog-text/catalog-markdown.ts`, pure) reads paragraphs, `- ` lists nested by two spaces per level (a line starting with a space continues the item above), `**bold**` and `[text](url)` — a link only for an http(s) URL (`isWebUrl`), anything else as written — and `pa-catalog-text` renders them as Angular nodes, never `innerHTML` (a `<H2>` or `#todo#` in a text stays text); a link opens in a new tab with `common.newTab`; `marked`/`ngx-markdown` stay legacy-only. After „Warum diese Priorität?“ a requirement card's panel shows the sections of `state/criterion-detail-rules.ts` (`detailLayout`): „Was wird verlangt?“, „Wann gilt das?“, „Wen betrifft es?“, „Woran erkennt man es?“, „Wie wird bewertet?“ (`assessment.value.*` in scale order, a list with „ · “ from CSS) through `pa-procedure-sections` (`components/procedure-sections/`), with „So wird es geprüft“ (the test step link, new tab), „Gilt für“ (names joined by `Intl.ListFormat`) and the source line `criteria.item.detail.source`; a section without text is left out. With two or more procedure texts the sections whose content is the same in every text stand once above a `mat-tab-group` with one tab per procedure (`criteria.item.detail.tabs.<testType>`, UX copy v19 U101, confirmed by the owner 2026-11-11), each tab holding the other sections, its link and its source line; „Gilt für“ follows the tabs. Examples (`pa-example-group`, `components/example-group/`): per group („So ist es gut“ / „So ist es nicht gut“, H4) the first three top-level items, then „Alle {{count}} Beispiele anzeigen“ (described by the group heading and the card title), notes after the list always visible; after it the button is gone and `CatalogText.focusItem` focuses the fourth example. Component tests that switch a `mat-tab` provide `MATERIAL_ANIMATIONS` with `animationsDisabled`, because jsdom never ends the tab body's transition. `assessment.value` is an `AREAS` entry. The gap list `kriterienkatalog_2026_1_luecken_v2.md` Z-7 names the „#todo#“ in 11.1.3.2 (shown verbatim, O-S4-39).

### 2.36 Override, include/exclude and notes (S4-16)

Override, include/exclude and notes (S4-16, R-LST-03 – R-LST-05; owner decisions O-S4-40 – O-S4-43 of 2026-11-11, all (a)): K5 `pa-priority-indicator` has its level button (`matMenuTriggerFor`, icon `arrow_drop_down`, named `criteria.priority.button` + the visible text, described by the criterion title) and a `mat-menu` `criteria.priority.changeLabel` with one `menuitemradio` per level; `level` and `weight` are models, a choice announces `criteria.priority.changed` (the menu returns the focus). With a `weightRange` (S11: `PRIORITY_WEIGHT_RANGE`) a `mat-select` `criteria.priority.weightLabel` sets the weight while B applies; without one the weight stays in the button. The override line is `criteria.priority.overridden` or, when only the weight of a recommended B differs, `criteria.priority.overridden.weight`; „Empfehlung wiederherstellen“ emits `restoreRecommendation` (not `reset`, a DOM event name the lint refuses), focuses the level button and announces the recommended level. The card has a checkbox without visible text before the H2 (`aria-labelledby` = hidden `criteria.item.include` + title); an excluded card stays in the list with `criteria.item.excluded`, `surface-container-low` and every control (its panel `surface-container`). „Notiz hinzufügen/bearbeiten“ (`criteria.item.note.add`/`.edit`) is a disclosure for an outlined `textarea` (focus into it, open from the start when a note exists); the note is a `FormControl` whose validator wraps `noteLengthError`, so Material shows `errors.field.maxLength` once the field was left and then live until it fits, and the text is kept as typed (no `maxlength`). The card only emits (`includedChange`, `levelChange`, `weightChange`, `priorityReset`, `noteChange`); the S11 page hands each to the store methods of S4-14. UX copy v20 U102–U104 (confirmed by the owner 2026-11-11). CAT-08 stays `planned` (the stored `ConfigurationCriterion` of step 3).

### 2.37 Search, filter and empty states of S11 (S4-17)

Search, filter and empty states of S11 (S4-17, R-LST-06 – R-LST-08; owner decisions O-S4-44 – O-S4-47 of 2026-11-11, all (a); ARCHITECTURE_v35 C121): above the cards `pa-criteria-list-filter` (`features/configurator/components/criteria-list-filter/`; only shows and emits) — a group `criteria.list.filter.label` with the search field (`type="search"`), the fieldset „Priorität“ with one checkbox per level (none checked = every level) and „Nur aufgenommene Kriterien anzeigen“. Every change the user makes to the filter announces `common.results.shown` once (the search when it applies, 300 ms after the last keystroke; ≤ 1/s, newest count): its plural forms are separate keys, so they share one throttle through the new `throttleKey` option of `A11yAnnouncerService.announce`; nothing is announced on the first load. `loadCriteriaList()` clears search and filter when the list is for another selection (`sameSelection`, sets per layer) and keeps them for the same one. With no card left: `criteria.list.emptyFilter` and the button `criteria.list.emptyFilter.reset` (UX copy v21 U105, confirmed by the owner 2026-11-11), which clears the filter and focuses the search field. `appTypesWithoutCriteria[]` → info notice `criteria.list.noCriteriaFor` (names by `Intl.ListFormat`, conjunction) above the list, with „Auswahl ändern“ to S10 step 2; with no criterion at all it is the empty state and the filter row is left out; `criteria.list.empty` only when no app type is listed. S10 opens at a step named in the router state (`ConfiguratorNavigationState` in `guards/configurator-guards.ts`, read from `Router.currentNavigation()`; `openingStep` in `state/selection-rules.ts` never goes past the first unanswered step) — never in the URL, and without a step announcement (the route change already focuses the H1). The „Geprüft nach …“ cards needed no change (S4-15a, S4-16). CAT-20 is complete and CAT-16 has its S11 deselection part in TESTING_v47 (its export part follows with S4-19/S4-20).

### 2.38 S11 summary, notices, suggestion link and action bar (S4-18a)
