S11 summary, notices, suggestion link and action bar (S4-18a, R-LST-09, R-LST-10, R-LST-13; owner decisions O-S4-49 – O-S4-53 of 2026-11-11, all (a); S4-18 split by O-S4-52, the `CriteriaListShown` caller is S4-18b, O-S4-48): in the order of Figma `2020:70` the S11 page shows below the title row `pa-criteria-list-summary` (`features/configurator/components/criteria-list-summary/`: `criteria.list.summary.count` with the requirement criteria of the answer, then a `<dl>` of use case — „Keiner davon passt“ is `criteria.select.step1.none` — app types, product parts and, only with a selection, test types; names from `state/selection-names.ts`, joined by `Intl.ListFormat`), the priority note `criteria.list.priorityNote` (`pa-notice` info) always and the draft note `criteria.list.draftNote` (attention) while the legacy store says nobody is signed in (`store.user()` + `isAuthenticated()`; both at the top, UX copy v22 U107), then `criteria.list.counts` before the filter row — a frame with one plural key per level (`criteria.list.counts.a|b|info` with `.one`, U106) over the requirement criteria by the level that applies now, „Geprüft nach …“ not counted (`levelCounts` in `state/criteria-list-rules.ts`). After the list the wrapping link `criteria.list.suggest` with the hidden `suggest.external` / `.url`. Last the action bar (only when the answer has a card): the counter `criteria.list.status.count` over every card (`includedCount`), announced through `A11yAnnouncerService` (≤ 1/s) only when the user includes or excludes, and „Liste exportieren“, which opens the export dialog S13 (since S4-21b, see below); „Liste speichern“ and `criteria.list.unsaved` wait for saving in step 3. The bar is in the flow below 600 px width or height and sticky from there (`STICKY_ACTION_BAR_QUERY`, keep it in step with the SCSS); while sticky its height is `scroll-padding-bottom` on `<html>` (the page scrolls, not the sidenav content; `ResizeObserver`), and a `focusin` handler scrolls a focused element the bar overlaps above it, because Firefox ignores the padding when focus moves (WCAG 2.4.11). `criteria.export` and `pageTitle` are `AREAS` entries; UX copy v22 U106/U107. E2E `e2e/configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` (order, sticky and in-flow bar, Tab never ends behind the bar, S13 by keyboard since S4-21b) and S11 with an open detail panel in `reflow.spec.ts`. No invariant changes.

### 2.39 The first `CriteriaListShown` caller (S4-18b)

The first `CriteriaListShown` caller (S4-18b, R-MET-01, R-CON-10; SPEC v2.10 §8.7, ARCHITECTURE_v36 C122; owner decisions O-S4-48 (a), O-S4-55 (a), O-S4-56 (b) of 2026-11-11): the Configurator module has its first endpoint, `POST /api/v1/configurator/criteria-list-shown` (`Usage/CriteriaListShownEndpoint`, `internal`, `AllowAnonymous`, no body, 204), which calls `IUsageEventWriter.CriteriaListShown` with `signedIn` from the request's sign-in (the legacy JWT until step 3); `MapConfiguratorEndpoints()` is the module's second public member and is mapped after `MapCatalogEndpoints()`. On the client `ConfiguratorApiService` (`features/configurator/services/api/`) sends it, and `withConfigurator().loadCriteriaList()` calls it for an applied, loaded list whose selection differs from `configurator.list.countedSelection` (`sameSelection`; A → B → A counts three times, back to S10 and S11 without a change none); the selection is remembered before the request, so a failed report is never repeated. A request marked `BACKGROUND_REQUEST` (`core/errors/problem-details.ts`) gets no snackbar, offline notice or server page from the error interceptor (an answer still counts for `ConnectionStatus`) — use it only for requests the user did not ask for; it stays off the loading counter. A page test that renders a component injecting the real `withConfigurator()` provides a `ConfiguratorApiService` double, as it does for `CatalogApiService`. Its rate limit (SPEC §7.3, client IP, 30 per 10 minutes 🔧) is the policy `CriteriaListShown` since S4-21a. Integration tests `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Configurator/CriteriaListShownEndpointTests.cs` (wait for the rows through `Infrastructure/UsageEventRows`), store tests in `with-configurator.spec.ts`, E2E in `s11-list.spec.ts` (one report, none after a return without a change).

### 2.40 Export pipeline (S4-19)

Export pipeline (S4-19, R-LST-12; ARCHITECTURE_v37 C123, TESTING_v48 V143–V147; owner decisions O-S4-57 – O-S4-60, all (a), of 2026-11-12): `Catalog.Contracts` has its first interface, `ICatalogSelectionResolver` (plain records; implemented by `Criteria/CatalogSelectionResolver` on the code of `GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` — `CriteriaResolver.ResolveCriteria` yields typed results that both map, so the export and S11 never disagree); it answers whether the release is active, its criterion count, the German names of the selection and per criterion what a file needs (original title and test-step link of the first procedure text, for `PROC-…` the test type's page; product part name; recommended level, weight, rationale). The Export module (`internal` except `ExportModule`; `AddExportModule(configuration)`, `MapExportEndpoints()` after the Configurator endpoints) holds `IExportSource<TInput>` with `AdHocSelectionSource` (uses `Catalog.Contracts` only — ARC-03 checks it with `ModuleRules.TypeUsingAny`), `ExportContent`/`ExportRow`, `ExportFileWriter` (UTC date from `TimeProvider`, file name of SPEC §8.3) and `IExportFormatter`, for now one `StubExportFormatter` per format (plain text under the format's content type) until S4-20. `POST /api/v1/exports` (`Http/ExportEndpoint`, anonymous): the body (`Requests/ExportRequest`, unknown members refused, so a client-sent resolved level is a 400 at its path) names every criterion of the selection exactly once by revision (O-S4-57); only the active release is exported — unknown/incomplete 404, no longer active 400 `release-mismatch` at `selection.releaseVersion` (O-S4-60); selection errors keyed below `selection` (`selection.useCaseTemplateId`, `selection.appTypeIds[1]`), `selection-excluded` only without other errors; caps: entries ≤ the release's criteria, note ≤ 1 000, weight within 🔧 `Configurator:PriorityWeight` (`appsettings.json`, 1–10, keep it in step with the client's `priorityWeight`, O-S4-58) and only while B applies, body ≤ 1 MB through `RequestSizeLimit` metadata (Kestrel 413; the test server ignores it, so the test checks the metadata); every answer `Cache-Control: no-store`. Rate limit and `ExportCreated` since S4-21a (see below). Tests: `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Export/`, `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Export/` (`ExportTestCatalog` imports `T.1`/`T.2` and builds bodies from the criteria answer), `Catalog/CatalogSelectionResolverTests.cs`, `Api/CacheHeaderTests.cs` (TEN-04). The "persists nothing" helper `Infrastructure/DatabaseRowCounts.cs` counts every table through `information_schema`; `Infrastructure/CapturingLoggerProvider.cs` keeps every log event at every level (O-S4-59, no sink package); EXP-05 runs on its own database and host, counts every schema except `analytics` and `logging`, and searches the logs (and `logging."SeriLogs"` after the host is disposed) for a sentinel. EXP-05, EXP-07, TEN-04 (reworded to ARCHITECTURE §9.6) are `active`, CAT-12 has its export part and CAT-16 its ad-hoc source part in TESTING_v48.

### 2.41 Export texts and CSV (S4-20a)

Export texts and CSV (S4-20a, R-LST-12; ARCHITECTURE_v38 C124, SPEC v2.11 §8.3; owner decisions O-S4-6, O-S4-62 – O-S4-68, all (a), of 2026-11-12; S4-20 split into S4-20a and S4-20b): the sheet names, column headers and „Info“ labels of the files are UX copy keys under the root `exportFile` (UX copy v24 U109), not under `criteria.export`, which the client's `de.json` carries in full; the files also reuse single screen texts (`criteria.priority.*`, `criteria.list.summary.*`, the two notes, `shell.appName`). They are the embedded resource `src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Export/Texts/de.json` — never edited by hand: `check-i18n.mjs` has two targets (`AREAS` for the client, `EXPORT_AREAS` for the Export module — since S5-11 a third, `EMAIL_AREAS` for Platform; an area may be one key, which also takes its longer keys) and `--write` fills both. `ExportTexts` (`Texts/`, singleton read in `AddExportModule`) stops the start when a key of `ExportTextKeys.Required` is missing — a renderer that reads a new key adds it there. `CriteriaColumns` (`Formatting/`) is the one column list of SPEC §8.3 with header key and cell value (Titel = plain-language title, else catalog title; Kurzbeschreibung empty until S4-24; „Priorität (Ihre Wahl)“ = the level that applies; weight only for B); every renderer writes from it. `CsvExportFormatter`: UTF-8 with BOM, `;`, CRLF after every record, RFC 4180 quoting, „Katalogversion“ first (EXP-03), and a leading `'` on a text cell that starts with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab or CR (O-S4-67). Tests read the CSV back with the strict `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Shared/Export/CsvReader.cs`. EXP-04 and the CSV part of EXP-03 are `active`, CAT-16 has its CSV file row in TESTING_v49.

### 2.42 XLSX (S4-20b)

XLSX (S4-20b, ARCHITECTURE_v39 C125; owner decisions O-S4-64, O-S4-66): `XlsxExportFormatter` (ClosedXML 0.105.1, MIT; `StubExportFormatter` is gone) writes the sheet „Kriterien“ from `CriteriaColumns` as an Excel table named `Kriterien` (no style, no stripes, bold frozen header row, fixed widths with wrapped text, the test-step link as hyperlink; one empty data row without criteria) and the sheet „Info“ as label and value lines (tool, 🔧 `Export:ToolUrl` from `appsettings.json` — `ExportToolUrl` stops the start unless it is an absolute http(s) URL —, UTC date, release, selection, requirement criteria per level that applies, the two notes, the source line); no fill, no conditional format. ClosedXML stamps the document with the machine clock: the formatter sets created/modified from the document's `TimeProvider` time and the author to the tool name. Never call `AdjustToContents` or anything else that measures text: the API image has no system fonts. ClosedXML accepts SixLabors.Fonts up to 3.0, and 2.0+ carries the Six Labors Split License (fails §10): `.github/dependabot.yml` ignores it and `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Export/ClosedXmlLicenceTests.cs` fails if it is ever resolved. EXP-01, EXP-02 and EXP-03 (ad-hoc source) are `active`, CAT-16 has its XLSX row in TESTING_v50; the persisted source of step 3 follows with EXP-06.

### 2.43 Rate limiting and client IP (S4-21a)

Rate limiting and client IP (S4-21a, ARCHITECTURE_v40 C126, SPEC v2.12 §7.3; owner decisions O-S4-69 (a), O-S4-70 (a)): Platform runs ASP.NET's forwarded-headers middleware first (`RateLimiting/ClientAddress`, in `UsePlatformMiddleware`) — `X-Forwarded-For` and, since S5-07 (owner decision 1a), `X-Forwarded-Proto` (Traefik terminates TLS, and the Secure cookies and antiforgery refuse a non-HTTPS request with a 500; nginx passes Traefik's value through, else its own scheme), trusted only from proxies in private and loopback networks, for at most 🔧 `ForwardedHeaders:ForwardLimit` (2: Traefik → nginx → API) — so the connection's remote address is the client's; it is used only as the rate-limit partition (IPv4 address, IPv6 /64), never stored or logged, and holds only while the API port is not reachable from the internet. The limiter (`RateLimiting/RateLimitingRegistration`) registers one fixed-window policy per client IP for each name of `RateLimitPolicies` in `Platform.Contracts` (`AnonymousExport`, `CriteriaListShown`; 🔧 `RateLimits:<policy>:PermitLimit`/`:Window`, 30 per 10 minutes, a bad value stops the start); a module attaches one with `RequireRateLimiting(RateLimitPolicies.<name>)`, a new policy gets its name there, its settings in `appsettings.json` and an `INV:API-05` test. `app.UsePlatformRateLimiting()` runs after CORS and authentication in `Program.cs`, so a 429 carries the CORS headers. A rejection is 429 Problem Details `rate-limit` with `retryAfterMinutes` and `Retry-After` — the whole window, an upper bound. The limiter keeps its own clock, not `TimeProvider`.  `/health/ready` (#112): at most 🔧 `Health:ReadyConcurrentChecks` (2) checks at a time over all callers, a further probe 429 at once; the database check's timeout is 🔧 `Health:ReadyTimeout` (5 s). The integration host raises both limits (every test-server request has no address); `RateLimitTests` (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Api/`) runs a host of its own with a limit of 2 and sets the remote address through the startup filter `Infrastructure/TestRemoteAddress`; `docker-compose.e2e.yml` relaxes both limits. `POST /api/v1/exports` writes `ExportCreated` (format, signed in) for a created file only (`ExportCreatedEventTests`); the CORS policy exposes `Content-Disposition`. API-05 is `active` for both policies in TESTING_v51; the others come with their endpoints.

### 2.44 Export dialog S13 (S4-21b)

Export dialog S13 (S4-21b, R-LST-11; ARCHITECTURE_v41 C127; owner decisions O-S4-71 – O-S4-74 of 2026-11-12, all (a)): „Liste exportieren“ opens `ExportDialog` (`features/configurator/dialogs/export-dialog/`, `@figma` 2134-3825 first) through `ExportDialog.open(dialog)` — first focus on the checked format radio (`autoFocus` selector, also on iOS), panel class `pa-dialog` (`styles/main.scss`: full screen below 600 px, 600 px wide from there; reuse it for the other dialogs of the Handoff). It shows the format fieldset (XLSX preselected), `criteria.export.scope` with the cards in the list (`includedCount`), one info notice with the release note and, while nobody is signed in, the anonymous note, and the ⚖️ legal note. „Datei herunterladen“ keeps its label with the 20 px spinner and `aria-disabled` while `ExportApiService` posts the body of `toExportRequest` (`state/export-request.ts`: release of the answer, selection by key, every criterion once by revision, only an override, a weight while B applies and a non-empty note) as `responseType: 'blob'`; the file name comes from `Content-Disposition` (`fileNameOf`), `FileSaver` (`core/files/file-saver.ts`) downloads it through an object URL, `criteria.export.success` is announced and the dialog closes (MatDialog returns the focus). A failure shows an error notice (`role="alert"`, title `criteria.export.error.title`) above the actions, as the Handoff says: `criteria.export.error` or, for 429, `errors.rateLimit` with `retryAfterMinutes` (plural, `errors.rateLimit.one`, UX copy v25 U110) — the primary button becomes „Erneut versuchen“; `release-mismatch` shows `criteria.export.outdated` with „Schließen“ only, which takes the focus (O-S4-72; since S6-27 also „Kriterien neu laden“, §2.88). The request is marked `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES` (`core/errors/problem-details.ts`): no snackbar, no server page, the offline notice still on status 0 — use it for a request the user asked for whose screen shows every failure in place, `BACKGROUND_REQUEST` only for requests the user did not ask for. The error interceptor reads a JSON `Blob` error body first (`withProblemBody`), so a file request's problem type counts. A page test that renders S11 provides an `ExportApiService` double. E2E: S13 in `e2e/configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` (keyboard, download name, 429 alert, axe in both appearances) and at 320 px in `reflow.spec.ts`. No invariant changes.

### 2.45 Lazy routes and bundle budget (S4-22)

Lazy routes and bundle budget (S4-22, #42, O-S4-75 (a)): every feature page loads with its route (`loadComponent` in `app.routes.ts`), so the first download holds only the shell, the start page S04 and the error and legal pages (762.9 kB, 173.7 kB transferred; 1.94 MB before); the `initial` budget in `angular.json` is 850 kB warning / 1 MB error — a new page gets `loadComponent`, never an eager import in `app.routes.ts`. Since S5-02 (#264) the legacy criteria list (`features/criteria/list/`, inside `/filters`), the only user of `ngx-markdown`, provides `provideMarkdown()` itself instead of `app.config.ts`, so `marked` and `ngx-markdown` load with that route (first download 700.8 kB, 157.6 kB transferred). `npm start` (`ng serve`) renders again: the `development` build configuration in `angular.json` lists `marked-katex-extension`, an optional peer of `ngx-markdown` that is not installed, in `externalDependencies`, because the dev server's dependency pre-bundling cannot resolve its dynamic import and fails the whole app (the production build never needed it); both go with the legacy filter.

### 2.46 Language of the catalog answers (S4-23)

Language of the catalog answers (S4-23, API-06 `active` in TESTING_v53, ARCHITECTURE_v42 C128, SPEC v2.14 §8): both catalog endpoints pick their language from `Accept-Language` (`de`, `en`) through `Http/CatalogLanguage` — only the header, not the host's request localization, which also reads a query parameter and a cookie — and every release has German texts only (`CatalogLanguage.OfReleases`; the release format has no language, English texts need an ADR), so `en`, an unknown language or no header get German, never 406. `CatalogJsonAnswer` sets `Content-Language` and `Vary: Accept-Language` on 200 and 304 and hashes the language into the `ETag`; Problem Details answers get neither. The client's legacy `languageInterceptor` already sends the UI language with every request. Contract tests through `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Catalog/CatalogLanguageContract.cs`; the export files stay German.

### 2.47 Short description (S4-24)

Short description (S4-24, #237, ARCHITECTURE_v43 ADR-016; owner decisions O-S4-6, O-S4-78 – O-S4-80, all (a)): the Kurzbeschreibung of Kriterienkatalog §6.1 is the optional `CriterionRevision.ShortDescriptionDe` (catalog migration `AddShortDescription`) and the optional plain-text property `shortDescriptionDe` of `criteria/<key>.json` (no Markdown, no length limit, empty refused; a change is a new revision, CAT-07); `PlainLanguageDe` stays the plain-language title. `ICatalogSelectionResolver` hands it to the export, whose column „Kurzbeschreibung“ writes it (empty without a value); the criteria answer and the S11 card do not carry it yet (follow-up). In the MiniCatalog and the folders `T.1`/`T.2` only `99.1` has one (J1 checks its XLSX cell), so a kept dev database needs `docker compose down -v`; 2026.1 has none (gap list v3).

### 2.48 M3.4 groundwork (S4-25)

M3.4 groundwork (S4-25, ARCHITECTURE_v44 C130; owner decisions O-S4-81 – O-S4-86, all (a)): `IdentityAccessDbContext` maps `ContributorProfile` (random `Guid` id, `DisplayName` ≤ 60, `Kind` by name, `CreatedAt` passed from `TimeProvider` to `Create`) and `UserContributorLink` (key `ContributorProfileId`: one user per profile, several profiles per user; deleted with its profile; `UserId` without foreign key while users are legacy, ADR-014) in `Domain/` (migration `AddContributorProfiles`; no endpoint, DTO or service yet — PRV-04 stays `planned`). The display name is unique regardless of case through the ICU collation `identity_case_insensitive` in schema `public` — EF writes a column's collation without schema, so a collation in `identity` is not found. The legacy migration `RenamePlatformRoles` renames `Approver` → `Moderator`, `Admin` → `PlatformAdmin` and `objects:approve` → `moderation:review` (`Permissions.ModerationReview`) and creates `PlatformAdmin`, `CatalogEditor`, `Moderator` where missing (`Roles.PlatformRoles`), so every environment has them; `Member` stayed global until S5-12a removed it (see below); `Proposal.ApproverId` keeps its name until step 5. The claim policy `ModerationReview` (`AuthorizationPolicies`, `API/Extensions/AuthorizationExtensions.cs`) replaces `AuthApproverAttribute` on `POST /api/Proposals/approve` (403 Problem Details instead of 500); the legacy client checks `moderation:review`. The JWT code is unchanged; a token carries the new names from the next sign-in or refresh. Respawn keeps `identity."Roles"` and `"RoleClaims"` (reference data of that migration) and `StoreAsync` also stores IdentityAccess entities; the fixture user is `TestUsers.Moderator`. `roles list [<email>]`, `roles grant <email> <role>`, `roles revoke <email> <role>` (SPEC R-ADM-03) is a command mode of the API host like `catalog` (`API/Commands/RoleCommandLine.cs`, `API/Extensions/RoleCommandHost.cs`: Platform, IdentityAccess, legacy context and Identity, no JWT, no migration; it moves into IdentityAccess with the users in M3.1): only the three platform roles, users by e-mail address, exit 0 also when nothing changes, 1 refused, 2 usage; it logs user id and role, never the address. TEN-06 is `active` for the rename and the approve endpoint in TESTING_v54.

### 2.49 Licence notices of the API image (S4-27)

Licence notices of the API image (S4-27, #187, ARCHITECTURE_v46 C132, TESTING_v55 V173; owner decisions O-S4-90 – O-S4-93, all (a)): the image ships `/app/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt` (API Dockerfile), the committed `API/API.ProcureAccess/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt` — never edited by hand: `scripts/check-api-notices.mjs --write` generates it after a .NET build from the published `deps.json` (`dotnet publish --no-build -c Debug`, so only the shipped packages — 48 since MailKit 5 dropped BouncyCastle (S5-04) —, not EF Design or Roslyn; the set depends on the .NET SDK, which prunes packages the shared framework already has — SDK 10.0.402 published one more than 10.0.404 — so generate it with the SDK pinned in `global.json` (see the next sentence) or with `--deps` from the built image's `/app/API.ProcureAccess.deps.json`) and each package's `.nuspec`, licence and notice files in the NuGet package folder: per package licence, copyright line (else authors) and project, each distinct text once (the package's own licence file, else the standard text from `LICENSES/`, plus every notice file such as Microsoft's `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.TXT`). CI stage 2 runs the check (self-test in stage 1) and fails on a licence expression outside `STANDARD_TEXTS` (MIT, Apache-2.0) or a reviewed `PACKAGE_TEXTS` entry (Npgsql's PostgreSQL licence names its holder, so its texts are in `scripts/api-notices/`), on a new version of a package in `EMBEDDED` (`Swashbuckle.AspNetCore.SwaggerUI` embeds swagger-ui-dist without its bundle's licence comments; `scripts/api-notices/swagger-ui-dist.txt` adds them — check what the new version embeds, update the file and the pinned version), on an entry for a package no longer shipped, and on a file that differs from the shipped packages — so a NuGet update (Dependabot too) stays red until the file is regenerated and its diff reviewed. No endpoint serves it (O-S4-91); the .NET runtime's notices are in the base image. .NET SDK pin (S5-03, #306, ARCHITECTURE_v48 C134; owner decision O-S5-1 (a)): one SDK version, `sdk.version` `10.0.404` in `global.json` (`rollForward: latestPatch`), builds the code locally, in CI (the four `actions/setup-dotnet` steps read it through `global-json-file`; there is no `DOTNET_VERSION` variable any more) and in the API image (`FROM mcr.microsoft.com/dotnet/sdk:10.0.404 AS build`, tag only); CI stage 1 „Check the SDK pin“ fails when the Dockerfile tag differs from `global.json`. The runtime image `aspnet:10.0` still floats (it changes no shipped package). Update the SDK in one commit: `global.json`, the Dockerfile tag and, if `check-api-notices.mjs` turns red, `--write` of the notices file; Dependabot's `docker` ecosystem proposes the tag, and that pull request fails stage 1 until `global.json` is changed on it. Local builds need the pinned band 10.0.4xx — distribution packages ship the 1xx band, so install it with `dotnet-install.sh --version <version> --install-dir ~/.dotnet` and put it first in `PATH`.

### 2.50 Cookie session and antiforgery, server part (S5-07)

Cookie session and antiforgery, server part (S5-07, ARCHITECTURE_v47 C133, TESTING_v56 V175–V177; owner decisions S5-07 Q1 (A), Q2 (a), Q3 (a)): the IdentityAccess module registers the Identity cookie schemes (`AddIdentityCookies`) with the application cookie `.ProcureAccess.Session` (`HttpOnly`, `Secure`, `SameSite=Strict`, not persistent; 🔧 `Session:IdleTimeout` 30 min as sliding `ExpireTimeSpan`, 🔧 `Session:AbsoluteTimeout` 8 h checked in `OnValidatePrincipal` after the chained security-stamp validator from the sign-in time in `Items["pa:signedInUtc"]`; 401/403 instead of redirects; no renewal on the probe; cookie handler and security-stamp validator read the API's `TimeProvider`, so `FakeTimeProvider` drives the limits), the antiforgery pair (`HttpOnly` `.ProcureAccess.Antiforgery` + readable `XSRF-TOKEN`, header `X-XSRF-TOKEN`) and `GET /api/v1/auth/session` (`Session/`, `internal`; `MapIdentityAccessEndpoints()` under `/auth`): always 200, `{ user: null }` or `{ user: { id, email, roles }, expiresAt, absoluteExpiresAt }`, (re)issues the token cookie, never renews the session. `app.UseIdentityAccessAntiforgery()` runs last in the pipeline (after the rate limiter) and answers every state-changing request without a valid token with 400 Problem Details `antiforgery` — opt-out only by `DisableAntiforgery()` metadata: the anonymous export, and until S5-10 the five anonymous legacy identity POSTs (`/api/signIn`, `signUp`, `forgot-password`, `reset-password`, `refresh`); a request with a Bearer header skips validation until S5-08 (a JWT is no ambient credential). `AntiforgeryValidationTests` asserts the exact exemption list, so it can only shrink knowingly. `POST /api/v1/auth/login` and `/logout` are in the host (`API/Auth/AuthEndpoints.cs`) because they need `SignInManager<User>` of the legacy user (they move with the users, M3.1): login 204 with cookie and new token, or 400 `validation` with `errors["$"]` for a wrong address or password, a locked or a deleted account (never 401, Q2 (a)); logout 204 and a new token, also without a session; both are validated like every other state-changing endpoint, so the client needs the probe's token first. Coexistence for one PR: the default scheme is the policy scheme `CookieOrBearer` (`IdentityExtensions`), which forwards to the JWT handler for a Bearer header and to the cookie otherwise; the default policy names no scheme, so `[Authorize]` (`UsersController`, `ProposalsController`, `ModerationReview`) accepts both. Behaviour changes for the legacy client: the anonymous `POST /api/v1/configurator/criteria-list-shown` answers 400 until S5-08 calls the probe (the request is `BACKGROUND_REQUEST`, nothing is shown); JWT requests are unchanged. Integration host: `ProcureAccessApiFactory(…, realAuthentication: true)` keeps the API's schemes instead of the test scheme (own database, own clock; `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/IdentityAccess/CookieSessionTests.cs`); the probe answers 200 for the anonymous client and an unknown `X-Test-User` (#87), the hook copies the caller's headers to the probe (#89), and every client that posts needs the hook (`CreateClient`). SEC-01 (server part), SEC-02, SEC-03 (API part), SEC-09 and OPS-04 are `active` in TESTING_v56; the idle and absolute limits are tested untagged, SEC-07/SEC-08 stay `planned` for S5-29 (renewal on every request, expiry headers, extend). CI of the PR showed two more things (owner decisions 1a, 2a of 2026-11-13): behind Traefik the API sees plain HTTP, and antiforgery with `SecurePolicy = Always` throws on a non-HTTPS request (500 on the probe and every state-changing request), so Platform now also trusts `X-Forwarded-Proto` from the proxies and `nginx.conf` forwards it (`ForwardedProtoTests`); and the client calls the probe once at start (`core/session/session-probe.service.ts`, `provideSessionProbe()` in `app.config.ts`, `BACKGROUND_REQUEST`, never rejects), so Angular's built-in XSRF interceptor sends `X-XSRF-TOKEN` with every state-changing request to a relative URL (release build, E2E stack) — not with `npm start`, whose `apiUrl` is absolute. The dev and E2E stacks run plain HTTP end to end, so there (owner decision 3a) `SessionCookiePolicy` makes the session cookie, the antiforgery cookie and `XSRF-TOKEN` `Secure` only on HTTPS requests — in `Development` and `E2E` alone, like the key-ring certificate rule; every other environment keeps `Always` (`SessionCookiePolicyTests`, `ForwardedProtoTests`).

### 2.51 Client on the cookie session, JWT removed (S5-08)

Client on the cookie session, JWT removed (S5-08, ARCHITECTURE_v50 C136, TESTING_v58 V181–V183; owner decisions S5-08 1–5, all (a), E2E part of SEC-01 deferred to S5-14): `withIdentity()` (`features/identity/state/with-identity.ts`) holds the session from `GET /api/v1/auth/session` — `session`, `sessionExpiresAt`, `sessionAbsoluteExpiresAt`, `sessionStatus`, computed `signedIn` and `permissions`; `loadSession()` (a background request that never rejects, a failed probe counts as signed out; for a signed-in user it loads the legacy user with the UI settings from `/api/me` once), `sessionReady()` for guards on a deep link, `signIn()`, `signOut()`, `hasPermission()`; the root store's `init()` runs the probe at start (`provideSessionProbe()` is gone); nothing reads or writes browser storage for the session (`with-identity.spec.ts` runs with `Storage` blocked, SEC-01 FE part). `authInterceptor` (`core/interceptors/auth.interceptor.ts`) turns a 401 into the login overlay through `LoginOverlayService` (`core/session/`; one dialog for concurrent 401s, the component lazily loaded) and sends the request once more after a sign-in (`RETRIED_AFTER_SIGN_IN`, no loop); not for the probe or a `BACKGROUND_REQUEST`; the error interceptor is silent on a 401; the antiforgery header is Angular's XSRF interceptor (`withXsrfConfiguration` in `app.config.ts`), which needs the relative `apiUrl`. The minimal overlay `pa-login-overlay` (`features/identity/dialogs/login-overlay/`, Handoff §5.3) shows `shell.session.expired.*`, the S01 fields and „Anmelden“, announces `shell.session.restored` and closes with `true`; S5-09 completes it into the S01 form card; `auth.login` is an `AREAS` entry of `check-i18n.mjs`. `POST /auth/login` is marked `CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` for `validation` and `antiforgery` (`LOGIN_PROBLEMS`), which the form shows in place. The probe's answer is the contract record `SessionResponse`/`SessionUser` of `IdentityAccess.Contracts` (its first types, #320), and `user.permissions` carries the role claims of type `permission` (e.g. `moderation:review`), so `permissionGuard`, `authGuard` (functional, both wait for `sessionReady()`) and the `appHasPermission` directives read the store; the server's policies stay the authority. Server side the JWT is gone (owner decision 1 (a)): handler, `CookieOrBearer`, `JWTSettings`, `SignInAsync`/`RefreshTokenAsync`, `/api/signIn`, `/api/refresh`, the Swagger Bearer scheme, both `JwtBearer` packages (notices regenerated), `JWT_KEY` in `.env.example`, compose and CI, and the Bearer skip of the antiforgery middleware — `Identity.Application` is the only scheme, SEC-02's exemption list holds only the export since S5-13b (`signUp` went with S5-12b, `forgot-password` and `reset-password` with S5-13b); the `RefreshToken` table stays until S5-10. The CORS policy (`AddFrontendCors`, `Cors:AllowedOrigins`) is removed (decision 4 (a)): every client calls the API on its own origin. The legacy login page signs in through the store and loads the UI settings from the legacy user; „Abmelden“ calls `signOut()` and opens the start page; `jwt-decode`, `@auth0/angular-jwt`, `headersInterceptor` and the token injection tokens are gone; stale tokens in a browser's `localStorage` are left alone (decision 5 (a)); the replaced identity files left `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS`.

### 2.52 Sign-in S01, overlay and sign-out (S5-09)

Sign-in S01, overlay and sign-out (S5-09, ARCHITECTURE_v51 C137, SPEC v2.19; owner decisions S5-09 1 (a), 2 (a), 3, „Passwort vergessen?“ (a)): the `login` outlet sits in `main#main` (`app.html`); while it is open the primary page stays alive but `hidden` (wrapper `.pa-main__page`, `display: contents` otherwise), so a page's input survives a sign-in — never destroy the primary outlet for the login. S01 `LoginPage` (`features/identity/pages/login/`, no Figma frame, form card as in S51) is `/(login:anmelden)`; `auth` redirects there until S5-10. `LOGIN_PATH` and `LoginNavigationState` are in `features/identity/models/login-navigation.ts` with no imports (a cycle through `app.config.ts` left the route path undefined once); `AuthService.showLogin('save')` adds `auth.login.reasonSave` through the router state (S5-20 wires „Liste speichern“). S01 and the login overlay share `pa-login-form` (`features/identity/components/login-form/`): validation on submit only (custom `ErrorStateMatcher`), `pa-error-summary` with the focus, `auth.login.error.invalid` for the server's `$` error, `errors.antiforgery`, other failures left to the error interceptor; „Passwort vergessen?“ is a link to S03 since S5-13b (see there), and every refusal of the sign-in as a whole adds the lockout note. Material leaves `aria-invalid` off an empty required field and links its `mat-error` through `aria-describedby` instead — test that, not `aria-invalid`. `PageTitleStrategy` treats the open `login` outlet as the page (`pageTitle.login`, UX copy v27 U112; focus on the visible H1, announcement on open and close) and gives a page S01 hid its title state back; `focusPageHeading()` is for a change the router does not see. The root store's `startSession()` (sign-in plus the legacy user settings) and `endSession()` (sign-out, `resetConfigurator()`, proposals, favourites, anonymous settings — also when the request fails) are what S01, the overlay and „Abmelden“ call; „Abmelden“ then opens `/` and focuses its H1. E2E `e2e/identity/login.spec.ts` (real sessions with the seeded E2E user since S5-14, see below); S01 and the overlay are in `reflow.spec.ts` (#170 L1).

### 2.53 E-mail (S5-11)

E-mail (S5-11, #302, ARCHITECTURE_v52 C138, TESTING_v59 V184–V187; owner decisions S5-11 1 (A: plain text and HTML), 2a, 3a, 4a): `IEmailSender` (`Platform.Contracts/Email`) sends one class per message type — `AddressConfirmationEmail`, `PasswordResetEmail`, `AlreadyRegisteredEmail`, `OrganisationInvitationEmail` (with `InvitationRole`); classes, not records, so `ToString` names only the type; the product-request types come with their step — synchronously with one retry, then `EmailDeliveryException` without the cause (also for an address MimeKit cannot use); a caller keeps its action and shows `errors.email.sendFailed`. Platform (`Email/`) renders the texts of the embedded `Email/Texts/de.json` — the UX copy's `email.*` and the three `org.members.role.*` labels, the third target of `check-i18n.mjs` (`EMAIL_AREAS`), never edited by hand; `EmailTexts.Required` lists the keys a new type reads — into `multipart/alternative`: UTF-8 text and an HTML part built from the same text (one `p` per paragraph, the message's link as the only `a`, no image, style or script; values HTML-escaped, a value with a line break or control character refused; links absolute http(s)); `Date` from `TimeProvider`. Logs name the message type above `Debug`, the recipient and the SMTP error only at `Debug` (PRV-11, `active` for its integration part; E2E part with the first rebuilt screen that sends mail). Settings from the environment only: `Email:Smtp:Host`, `:Port`, `:Username`, `:Password`, `:Security` (`StartTls` default, `SslOnConnect`, `None`), `Email:From`, `Email:DisplayName`; only `Development` and `E2E` may send without credentials or TLS, every other environment — the integration tests' `Testing` too — stops at startup (`EmailSettings`). `AddPlatformEmail(configuration, environment)` is called by the web host only, never by the `catalog`/`roles` command hosts. Mailpit (`axllent/mailpit`, MIT, tag + digest; change `docker-compose.dev.yml`, `docker-compose.e2e.yml` and `MailpitContainer.Image` together) catches every mail of the dev and E2E stacks (UI `http://localhost:8025`). Integration hosts get dummy SMTP credentials and `ProcureAccessApiFactory.Emails` (`CapturingEmailSender`) instead of SMTP; `Platform/EmailDeliveryTests` sends every type through MailKit to a Mailpit Testcontainer. The legacy `EmailService`, `EmailTemplateService`, HTML templates and `EmailSettings` are gone: `UserService` sends the confirmation and reset to the user's own address (#302) through `IEmailSender` (SERVICES references `Platform.Contracts`), and a failed send is logged without the address while the request answers as before.

### 2.54 Registration and address confirmation, server part (S5-12a)

Registration and address confirmation, server part (S5-12a, #301, ARCHITECTURE_v53 C139, TESTING_v60 V188–V191, SPEC v2.21 §8.8; owner decisions O-S5-3 (a), S5-12 2–5; S5-12b builds S02, the confirmation pages and the notice): `POST /api/v1/auth/register` `{ email, password, privacyAcknowledged }`, `/confirm-email` `{ token }` and `/confirm-email/resend` are in the host (`API/Auth/RegistrationEndpoints.cs`, next to sign-in, until the users move, M3.1), all antiforgery-validated. Register answers 204 for a new and a known address alike — a known one (also one taken only as a user name) gets `AlreadyRegisteredEmail` with a link to `/(login:anmelden)` and its password is hashed anyway; a new one gets an unconfirmed account **without a role** and `AddressConfirmationEmail` (now with `ValidHours`, `{{hours}}` in `email.confirm.body`, U113) linking `<App:BaseUrl>/registrieren/bestaetigen?token=…` (`ClientLinks`); field errors 400 `validation` at `email`, `password`, `privacyAcknowledged` (checked, never stored); a failed send still answers 204 (decision 4 (a)). Confirm answers 204 when the address is confirmed now or the token was used before for a confirmed account (decision 4 (b), mail scanners), else 400 `validation` at `token`; resend needs a sign-in and answers 503 `email-delivery` (new problem type, `errors.email.sendFailed`, U119) after a failed send. The tokens: `IAccountTokens` in `IdentityAccess.Contracts/Accounts` (`IssueAsync`/`RedeemAsync`, purpose `EmailConfirmation`; S5-13 adds the reset), implemented by `Accounts/AccountTokenService` on `identity."AccountTokens"` (migration `AddAccountTokens`, `UserId` without foreign key): 32 random bytes as Base64url in the link, only their SHA-256 stored, 🔧 `Account:Tokens:EmailConfirmationLifetime` (24 h) on `TimeProvider`, redeemed by one conditional `UPDATE`, so it is single use also under concurrency; never log an `IssuedAccountToken`'s value (its `ToString` is the type). The gate: `IdentityAccessPolicies.ConfirmedEmail` (name in `IdentityAccess.Contracts`) — attach it with `RequireAuthorization(...)` to saving a list, creating an organisation, accepting an invitation, creating a contributor profile and every publication path, each with its own `INV:SEC-10` case; its handler (`API/Auth/ConfirmedEmailAuthorization.cs`) reads `EmailConfirmed` from the database on every gated request, and `ConfirmedEmailResultHandler` turns a refusal by it alone into 403 Problem Details `email-unconfirmed`. Its first real endpoint is `POST /api/v1/organizations` (S5-16a), on which `RegistrationTests` checks it. Password rule (`API/Auth/PasswordPolicy.cs`): 🔧 `Account:Password:MinLength` (12) to 128 (`MaximumLengthPasswordValidator`), no composition rules, any character in the user name; global, so the legacy change and reset follow and `SAMPLE_USER_PASSWORD` needs 12 characters. Rate limits: `Registration` (client IP) and `ConfirmationResend`, the first policy **per signed-in user** (`RateLimitingRegistration.UserPolicies`, partition `user:<id>`), 5 per hour 🔧 each. The global `Member` role is gone (O-S5-3 (a), #301): neither registration adds it, the Development rebuild no longer creates it, `Roles.Member` and the client's `EnUserRole.member` are deleted, `TestUsers.Member` keeps its name without a role; rows a database still holds are unused. The legacy `/api/signUp` and `/confirm-email` went with S5-12b (see below). SEC-10 is `active` for its integration part (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/IdentityAccess/RegistrationTests.cs`, a `realAuthentication` host on an only migrated database); API-05 covers both new policies.

### 2.55 Registration and address confirmation, client part (S5-12b)

Registration and address confirmation, client part (S5-12b, ARCHITECTURE_v54 C140, TESTING_v61 V192–V195, SPEC v2.22; owner decisions S5-12b 1 (a), 2 (a), 3 (a)): S02 `RegisterPage` (`features/identity/pages/register/`) at `/registrieren` (`REGISTER_PATH` in `models/login-navigation.ts`, which stays without imports), in place of the page — S01's „Jetzt registrieren“ closes the `login` outlet and opens it; `/register` redirects there. Form card like S01; checked on submit (required, `errors.field.email`, `errors.field.minLength`/`.maxLength` with the count, `auth.register.consent.required` under the checkbox); the 🔧 minimum is the client setting `passwordMinLength` of both environment files, read through `PASSWORD_RULE` (`models/password-rule.ts`) — keep it in step with `Account:Password:MinLength`; the link „Datenschutzhinweise“ inside the acknowledgement is the text of `legal.privacy.title` found in `auth.register.consent` and opens `/datenschutz` in a new tab; server field errors go to the fields, `antiforgery` and `rate-limit` into the error summary (`REGISTER_PROBLEMS`); success replaces the form with the focused H2 `auth.register.success.title`. The confirmation page `/registrieren/bestaetigen` (`CONFIRM_EMAIL_PATH`) is the lazy child routes `CONFIRM_EMAIL_ROUTES` (`pages/confirm-email/confirm-email.routing.ts`) — `app.routes.ts` must not import the store or a service (cycle through `app.config.ts`): `confirmEmailTokenGuard` puts the token into `PendingConfirmationToken` (memory only) and redirects to the path without it with `replaceUrl` (so it never stays in the address or the history), `confirmEmailResultResolver` waits for `sessionReady()` (the antiforgery token), posts it (`CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES`) with the loading counter and resolves `data[PAGE_TITLE_KEY]` to `pageTitle.confirm.done`, `.invalid` (400 `validation`, or no token) or `pageTitle.server`, so `PageTitleStrategy` titles, focuses and announces the result; `ConfirmEmailPage` shows it like S80. Guard and resolver run on every navigation there (`runGuardsAndResolvers: 'always'`; the redirect uses `onSameUrlNavigation: 'reload'`), and the resolver keeps the last result without a new token (S01 opening over the page). `pa-confirm-required-notice` (`components/confirm-required-notice/`) is the notice `auth.confirm.required` with „Bestätigungs-E-Mail erneut senden“ (`withIdentity().resendConfirmation()`, `RESEND_PROBLEMS`; result line `auth.confirm.sent` / `errors.email.sendFailed` / `errors.rateLimit`, announced) — not on a screen yet: the first gated screen (S5-16) places it where its 403 `email-unconfirmed` arrives and brings SEC-10's E2E part. `auth.register` and `auth.confirm` are `AREAS` entries of `check-i18n.mjs`. The legacy `POST /api/signUp`, `GET /api/confirm-email`, `UserService.SignUpAsync`/`ConfirmEmailAsync`/`SendConfirmationEmailAsync`, `RegistrationRequest` and the legacy register and confirm pages are gone. E2E `e2e/identity/register.spec.ts` registered on the real stack (since S5-14 that way runs in J8, see below) and reads the mail through `e2e/support/mailpit.ts` (Mailpit's API, `E2E_MAILPIT_URL`, default `http://localhost:8025`; PRV-11 E2E part `active`); S02 and both confirmation results are in `reflow.spec.ts`.

### 2.56 Password reset and lockout, server part (S5-13a)

Password reset and lockout, server part (S5-13a, ARCHITECTURE_v55 C141, TESTING_v62 V196–V199, SPEC v2.23; owner decisions S5-13 1–5 (a), S5-13a (a); S5-13b builds S03 and removes the legacy reset): `POST /api/v1/auth/password-reset` `{ email }` and `/password-reset/complete` `{ token, newPassword }` are in the host (`API/Auth/PasswordResetEndpoints.cs`, until M3.1), antiforgery-validated. The request answers 200 `{ validHours }` for every well-formed address and only queues it: `PasswordResetMailQueue` (bounded, 100) and the hosted service `PasswordResetMailer` (`API/Auth/PasswordResetMailer.cs`) look the account up, issue the link and send `PasswordResetEmail` to the stored address — so a known address answers as fast as an unknown one; never look an address up in the request path of that endpoint. Every non-deleted account gets a link, confirmed or not; the link is `<App:BaseUrl>/passwort/neu?token=…` (`ClientLinks.PasswordResetPagePath`), purpose `PasswordReset` of `IAccountTokens`, 🔧 `Account:Tokens:PasswordResetLifetime` (2 h). `complete` checks the new password with Identity's validators before it redeems the token (a refused password keeps the link), then sets it through `ResetPasswordAsync` (security stamp changes: other sessions end at the next stamp check, the immediate end is SEC-08 with S5-29), confirms the address, ends a lockout and revokes the other reset links (`IAccountTokens.RevokeAsync`). Integration tests wait for the background mail with `CapturingEmailSender.WaitForAsync`. Rate limits: `PasswordReset` per client IP (5 per hour 🔧) and `PasswordResetAddress` per address (3 per hour 🔧) — the address is in the body, so the endpoint calls `IKeyedRateLimiter` (`Platform.Contracts`; `KeyedRateLimiter` keys by the SHA-256 of the normalised address, memory only) and its `RateLimitExceededException` becomes the same 429 through Platform's exception handler; a new body-keyed limit goes into `KeyedPolicies` of `RateLimitingRegistration`. Lockout: 🔧 `Account:Lockout:MaxFailedAttempts` (5) and `Account:Lockout:Duration` (15 min) in `appsettings.json` (`LockoutPolicy`, no longer hard-coded); `ClockedUserManager` (`API/Auth/`, registered by `AddUserManager`) reads `TimeProvider` for the lock, because Identity 10 uses the system clock; a locked account answers like a wrong password (#319 decided: S01 shows a fixed note with every refusal, S5-13b). SEC-11 is `active` (invitation tokens with S5-17), API-05 covers both new policies (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/IdentityAccess/PasswordResetTests.cs`).

### 2.57 Password reset S03 and the lockout note, client part (S5-13b)

Password reset S03 and the lockout note, client part (S5-13b, ARCHITECTURE_v56 C142, TESTING_v63 V200–V202, SPEC v2.24, UX copy v29 U120–U124; owner decisions S5-13 4 (a), S5-13b 1 (a), 2 (a)): `ResetRequestPage` (`features/identity/pages/reset-password/`) at `/passwort` (`RESET_PASSWORD_PATH`) asks for the link (`AuthService.requestPasswordReset`, `RESET_REQUEST_PROBLEMS`) and replaces the form with the focused `auth.reset.sent` (address and the answer's `validHours`). `/passwort/neu` (`NEW_PASSWORD_PATH`) is the lazy child routes `NEW_PASSWORD_ROUTES` (`pages/new-password/new-password.routing.ts`): `newPasswordTokenGuard` keeps the token in `NewPasswordState` (memory only) and redirects to the path without it; the resolvers give the view and `pageTitle.reset` / `pageTitle.reset.invalid`; the token is sent only with „Passwort speichern“ (`completePasswordReset`, `RESET_COMPLETE_PROBLEMS`). A refused token shows „Link nicht mehr gültig“ at the same address through `PageTitleStrategy.changePageInPlace(key)` — title, H1 focus and announcement for a page change the router does not see (the router's own title update ignores a navigation to the same path); a reload shows it too; a second link opened on the page empties the field and calls it with the new view's key. „Zur Anmeldung“ opens S01 over the start page. Both steps share `pages/reset-password-pages.scss`. „Passwort vergessen?“ in `pa-login-form` is a link: in place on S01, in a new tab from the login overlay (`forgotInNewTab`). Every refusal at `$` adds `auth.login.error.lockout` with the 🔧 client setting `lockout` (`{ maxFailedAttempts: 5, minutes: 15 }` in both environment files, read through `LOCKOUT_RULE` in `models/lockout-rule.ts` — keep it in step with `Account:Lockout:*`) as the new optional `note` input of `pa-error-summary` (after the list, not counted). `auth.reset` is an `AREAS` entry of `check-i18n.mjs`; `auth.login.locked` is gone. Legacy removed: `/api/forgot-password`, `/api/reset-password`, their `UserService` methods and DTOs, the `SERVICES` reference to `Platform.Contracts`, the forgot-password dialog, the legacy reset page and its `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` entry, and the unrouted legacy login page; `/reset-password` redirects to `/passwort`. E2E `e2e/identity/password-reset.spec.ts` (registers through the API with the probe's token, resets through the Mailpit mail, signs in with the new password, refuses the used link); S03 in `reflow.spec.ts`.

### 2.58 E2E users and journey J8 (S5-14)

E2E users and journey J8 (S5-14, ARCHITECTURE_v57 C143, TESTING_v64 V203–V206; owner decisions S5-14 1–4, all (a)): the user part of the E2E seeder of TESTING §6.3 — at startup, after `RebuildDatabase` and the test catalog release, `SeedTestUsersIfAllowedAsync` (`API/Extensions/TestUserSeedExtensions.cs`, legacy `UserManager<User>` until M3.1) creates `e2e-confirmed@example.invalid` (address confirmed) and `e2e-unconfirmed@example.invalid` (not confirmed), without a role, with the password `TestUsers:Password`, if they do not exist; only in `Development` and `E2E` (`IHostEnvironment`, like the catalog part) and only when that password is set, which only `docker-compose.e2e.yml` does (`TestUsers__Password`), so the dev stack, `dotnet run`, staging and production have none. The addresses and the password are test data in the repository, not secrets: `e2e/support/users.ts` (`CONFIRMED_USER`, `UNCONFIRMED_USER`, `TEST_USER_PASSWORD`) and the compose override carry the same values — change them together. Tests share these accounts: never send a wrong password for them (five lock the account for every test, SEC-11) and never change their password or address; a test that changes an account registers its own. Organisation users come with S5-15/S5-16. `TestUserSeedGuardTests` (Operations). J8 `e2e/journeys/j8-account.spec.ts` (TESTING §5.4) registers on S02, reads the confirmation mail from Mailpit (PRV-11 E2E part), confirms, signs in on S01 and signs out, by keyboard with axe in both appearances on every state; after the real sign-in it inspects the browser (SEC-01 E2E part: the session cookie `HttpOnly` and `SameSite=Strict`, `XSRF-TOKEN` the only readable cookie, no token in `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`; `Secure` is off on the plain-HTTP stack). It took over the first test of `register.spec.ts`, which keeps the edge cases. `login.spec.ts` and the overlay case of `reflow.spec.ts` run on real sessions (a refused sign-in with an unknown address); `e2e/support/session.ts` keeps only `expireSessionOnce`.

### 2.59 Organisation domain and tenancy (S5-15)

Organisation domain and tenancy (S5-15, ARCHITECTURE_v58 C144, TESTING_v65 V207–V210; owner decisions S5-15 1–4 of 2026-11-15, all as recommended; ADR-007 accepted): `src/ProcureAccess.Modules.IdentityAccess/Domain/` holds `Organization` (random `Guid` id, name ≤ 120, `OrganizationType` public body / company / association, creator, `CreatedAt`) and `OrganizationMembership` (key organisation + user, `OrganizationRole` Reader < Editor < Owner, `InvitedAt`, `AcceptedAt`; `UserId` without foreign key, ADR-014; `Founder` is the creator's accepted Owner membership), tables `identity."Organizations"` and `"OrganizationMemberships"` (migration `AddOrganizations`); both enums are in `IdentityAccess.Contracts/Organizations`. **Tenant context:** `IOrganizationContext` (`Platform.Contracts/Tenancy`, `OrganizationIds`) is the set of organisations the request may see — the memberships of the signed-in user, never a client header or query parameter (decision 2 (a)); it is declared in Platform's contracts like `IShareableResourceProvider`, because every module may use them and Platform's own `ShareLink` will be scoped (decision 1 (a)). IdentityAccess implements it as the request-scoped `OrganizationContext` (`Tenancy/`), which `app.UseIdentityAccessOrganizationContext()` (inside `AddIdentityAuthMiddlewares`, between `UseAuthentication` and `UseAuthorization`) fills with one indexed query per signed-in request — the one allow-listed `IgnoreQueryFilters()` — and leaves empty for an anonymous one. **Filter:** every module context binds the global query filter of each entity with `OrganizationId` to it (`_organizations.OrganizationIds.Contains(e.OrganizationId)`; a field of the context, so EF parametrises it per query), `Organization` to its own id (decision 4): a foreign row does not exist for the request, so a foreign id is 404 by construction, and a query outside a request sees nothing. A context built outside a request (design-time factory, fixtures, command hosts) takes `FixedOrganizationContext.None`; a test that reads as a member passes `FixedOrganizationContext.Of(ids)` or loads the scoped `OrganizationContext` of its DI scope. A new scoped entity in any module gets the same filter in its context and a sample row in `OrganizationIsolationTests.Samples` — the discovery test fails until it has one. **Authorisation (TEN-05 skeleton, decision 3 (a)):** `OrganizationRoleRequirement.Reader|Editor|Owner` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts`) on an `IOrganizationResource` (a loaded entity or the route's `OrganizationReference`), checked with `IAuthorizationService.AuthorizeAsync(User, resource, requirement)`; `OrganizationRoleHandler` reads the loaded memberships (no query); answer a failure with `OrganizationAccess.StatusOf(result)` — 404 for a non-member or a user without organisation, 403 for a member below the minimum — and put `RequireAuthorization()` on the endpoint for the 401. Since S5-16a the matrix of `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Tenancy/AuthorizationMatrixTests.cs` runs on the real organisation endpoints (the test-only probe is gone); a new organisation, configurator or workspace endpoint adds its rows there. `IdentityAccess.Contracts` references the shared framework (`FrameworkReference`, no project reference, ARC-03 unchanged); no `Contracts` → `Contracts` reference was needed (O-22 open). **Tests:** TEN-01 `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Tenancy/OrganizationIsolationTests.cs` (reflection over every context's model, filter declared, A invisible to B and to no organisation), TEN-02 `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Architecture/Tenancy/IgnoreQueryFiltersAllowListTests.cs` (scan of `src/`, `API/`, `DAL/`, `SERVICES/` for the call `.IgnoreQueryFilters(` and, since S5-16a, raw SQL — `.FromSql…`, `.SqlQuery…`, `.ExecuteSql…` — against an allow-list with reasons: the membership loader, the organisation row lock of S5-16a and the three legacy repositories, whose bypass is the legacy soft-delete filter and raw SQL; a new or a vanished call site fails), TEN-05 `AuthorizationMatrixTests` (18 cells plus header cases). The Tenants fixture of TESTING §6.2 in part: `TestUsers.OwnerA` … `ReaderB` (no roles or permissions; `Member` is the user without organisation) and `Tenants.Build(now)` (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Shared/Fixtures/Tenants.cs`, stores A, B and six memberships); E2E organisation users come with S5-16. Existing accounts keep working without a membership (ARCHITECTURE §12 step 3).

### 2.60 Organisation endpoints, server part (S5-16a)

Organisation endpoints, server part (S5-16a, ARCHITECTURE_v59 C145, TESTING_v66 V211–V214, SPEC v2.27 §8.9; owner decisions S5-16 1a (split: S5-16b builds S20, S21 and the leave dialog), 2a (revoking invitations with S5-17), 3a (problem type `last-owner`), 4a (endpoints in the host; members see each other's addresses)): `POST /api/v1/organizations` `{ name, type }` (201 `{ id }`, the creator Owner; name trimmed, required, ≤ 120; `type` `publicBody`/`company`/`association`; the gate `ConfirmedEmail`, the first real one — the test-only `ConfirmedEmailProbeController` is gone), `GET …/{orgId}/members` (Reader and up: `{ organization { id, name, type }, members [ { userId, email, role } ] }`, Owners first, then by address), `PATCH …/members/{userId}` `{ role }` (Owner; `owner`/`editor`/`reader`) and `DELETE …/members/{userId}` (Owner for someone else, every member for their own id = leaving) live in the host (`API/Organizations/OrganizationEndpoints.cs`) until M3.1, because the addresses come from the legacy users through `UserManager<User>` (one query per list); the rules are `IOrganizationMembers` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts/Organizations`, with `OrganizationLimits` and `MembershipChangeResult`), implemented by the `internal` `Organizations/OrganizationMemberService` on the filtered context. Each endpoint checks the route's organisation with `OrganizationRoleRequirement` first (404 non-member, 403 lesser role); the `{orgId}` route has no `:guid` constraint, because API-01's discovery test expects a malformed id to answer 400 `validation`, not an unmatched route's 404. **Last-Owner rule (TEN-11):** a role change or removal runs in a transaction inside the context's execution strategy, locks the organisation row with `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` after the filtered read found it, counts the Owners and refuses through `OrganizationMembership.LeavesNoOwner` with 409 Problem Details `last-owner` (`ProblemTypes.LastOwner`; `conflict` stays API-03's) — so two Owners who demote each other at once leave one; a sole member can never leave and deleting an organisation is not designed. `OrganizationEndpointsTests` (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Organizations/`) stores the legacy accounts of the Tenants users (addresses, gate) and proves the lock deterministically: the test holds the row lock, waits in `pg_stat_activity` until both requests wait for it — on a connection of its own, because a transaction keeps its first snapshot of `pg_stat_activity` — and then releases it. API-01's discovery test stores a confirmed account for its Moderator, so gated endpoints reach model binding. No rate limit on creating organisations (SPEC §7.3 lists none) and no EF migration.

### 2.61 Organisation screens, client part (S5-16b)

Organisation screens, client part (S5-16b, ARCHITECTURE_v60 C146, TESTING_v67 V215–V218, SPEC v2.28 §8.9, UX copy v30 U125–U132 drafted for the owner's review; owner decisions S5-16 5a, 6a, 7a): `features/organization/` — `models/organization.ts` (types, `ORGANIZATION_PATH` `organisation` and `MEMBERS_SEGMENT` `mitglieder` in a file without imports, so `app.routes.ts` loads no page), `services/api/organization-api.service.ts` (`CREATE_PROBLEMS`, `MEMBER_CHANGE_PROBLEMS`; the members read is `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES`). S20 `pages/organization-start/` at `/organisation` (form card as S02; type as a radio group with `errors.field.requiredChoice`; the name is trimmed and at most 120; 403 `email-unconfirmed` shows `pa-confirm-required-notice` above the form with the focus and keeps the input; success opens S21). S21 `pages/members/` at `/organisation/:id/mitglieder` is lazy child routes (`members.routing.ts`) whose resolver loads the members before the page shows, so the route change titles, focuses and announces „Mitglieder von {{org}}“ with the name (`setPageTitleState` in an `effect`); the result goes to the page through the route-level provider `MembersPageLoad`; a 404 or 400 shows „Seite nicht gefunden“ and any other failure the S80 server text in place through the new optional `kind` input of `ErrorPage`. `mat-table` from 600 px and a `<ul>` below (`injectWideLayout()`; component tests use `provideLayout(wide)` of the shell test doubles, because the fake media matcher lacks the old `addListener` that `BreakpointObserver` calls); an Owner gets a `mat-select` per member (`org.members.role.label`, the hints as the options' second line, a `mat-select-trigger` with the role name only) and „Aus der Organisation entfernen“ for the others, every member „Organisation verlassen“; both ask through `ConfirmDialog` (`shared/components/confirm-dialog/`, S36 pattern: `autoFocus: 'first-heading'`, „Abbrechen“ then the action, stacked below 600 px with the action last — never `column-reverse`). A refusal (`last-owner`, `antiforgery`) shows the error notice `org.members.error.title` (`role="alert"`) above the list and reads the list again; rows are rebuilt after every reload (a `version` in the track key), so a select shows the stored role; when the focused control is gone after a change, the H1 takes the focus; leaving announces `org.left` and then opens S20. `PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS` maps `last-owner` → `org.members.lastOwner`; `org.start`, `org.create`, `org.join`, `org.members`, `org.leave`, `org.left` are `AREAS` entries (not `org.invite`, which comes with S5-17). No menu entry until S5-18. E2E seeder: three more confirmed users `e2e-org-owner|editor|reader@example.invalid` and „E2E-Organisation“ with the fixed id `0e2e0000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001` (`TestUserSeedExtensions.OrganizationId`, `e2e/support/users.ts` `E2E_ORGANIZATION` — change both together), through `IdentityAccessModule.SeedTestOrganizationAsync` (`Organizations/TestOrganizationSeeder.cs`; `Organization.Create` takes the optional id for it alone); an existing one of another Owner — users created again by the Development rebuild — is replaced, so its memberships never name a deleted user. E2E tests only read it: never change a role there; a test that changes an organisation builds its own once invitations exist (S5-17). `e2e/support/session.ts` has `signInThroughApi(page, email, password)` (probe token, `page.request` shares the context's cookies) for tests whose subject is not S01. E2E `e2e/organization/organization.spec.ts` (SEC-10 E2E part with a freshly registered account and the Mailpit link; S21 as Owner and Reader, the last-Owner refusal, cancelled dialogs, a foreign id); S20 and S21 in `reflow.spec.ts`.

### 2.62 Invitations by e-mail, server part (S5-17a)

Invitations by e-mail, server part (S5-17a, ARCHITECTURE_v61 C147, TESTING_v68 V219–V223, SPEC v2.29 §8.6, §8.9; owner decisions S5-17 D1–D8 (a) and the mismatch answer (a) of 2026-11-15; S5-17b builds the screens): `OrganizationInvitation` (`IdentityAccess/Domain/`, table `identity."OrganizationInvitations"`, migration `AddOrganizationInvitations`) holds the address as typed, its Identity normalisation `NormalizedEmail` (the address binding, compared like the sign-in compares addresses), the role, only the SHA-256 of the link's token (made like the account tokens: `AccountTokenService.TokenBytes`/`TryHash`), `CreatedAt`, `ExpiresAt` (🔧 `Organizations:Invitations:Lifetime`, 14 days) and `AcceptedAt`; one open invitation per organisation and address (partial unique index); it carries the organisation filter and its TEN-01 sample. The rules are `IOrganizationInvitations` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts/Organizations`), implemented by the `internal` `Organizations/OrganizationInvitationService`; inviting and accepting run in a transaction under `OrganizationRowLock` (`Organizations/`, the one raw-SQL row lock, which the member service's role changes and removals take too), and accepting reloads the invitation after the lock, so a link is accepted once also by two requests at the same moment. The endpoints are in the host (`API/Organizations/InvitationEndpoints.cs`, next to `OrganizationEndpoints`, whose role map, `RefusedAsync`, `UserIdOf` and `NameOf` are `internal` for it; until M3.1): `POST /api/v1/organizations/{orgId}/invitations` `{ email, role }` (Owner; 201 `{ id, validDays }`; replaces the open invitation of the same address; 400 at `email` for an invalid address or a member's; sends `OrganisationInvitationEmail` with `<App:BaseUrl>/organisation/einladung?token=…` (`ClientLinks.InvitationPagePath`), 503 `email-delivery` with the invitation kept; rate limit `Invitation`, 5 per hour per signed-in user 🔧), `GET …/invitations` (Owner only: the open ones by address), `DELETE …/invitations/{id}` (Owner; 404 when not open), `POST /api/v1/invitations/view` `{ token }` (anonymous: organisation name and role, `addressMatches` for a signed-in caller, the invited address only to a signed-in caller whose address differs, `alreadyMember`/`organizationId` for a member) and `POST /api/v1/invitations/accept` `{ token }` (`ConfirmedEmail`, 403 `email-unconfirmed`; 200 `{ organizationId }`, also for the new member's second click; 403 Problem Details `invitation-mismatch` with the extension `invitedEmail` — new in `ProblemTypes` — for another address, the invitation stays open; 400 at `token` otherwise). The token only ever travels in a POST body, never in a URL. Accepting never confirms an address (D6 (a)). `InvitationCleanupService` (hosted, on `TimeProvider`) deletes expired invitations, open or accepted, at start and every `Organizations:Invitations:CleanupInterval` (1 h); the role command host never starts its host, so only the web host runs it. TEN-02 allow-lists `OrganizationRowLock.cs` (instead of the member service), the read by token in `OrganizationInvitationService.cs` and the clean-up; TEN-05 has the rows of the three Owner endpoints; SEC-11 is complete and SEC-10 has its accept case (`tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Organizations/InvitationEndpointsTests.cs`, which advances the shared fake clock for the 14 days); API-05 covers `Invitation`. The integration host and `docker-compose.e2e.yml` relax `RateLimits:Invitation`.

### 2.63 Invitation screens, client part (S5-17b)

Invitation screens, client part (S5-17b, ARCHITECTURE_v62 C148, TESTING_v69 V224–V227, SPEC v2.30 §8.9, UX copy v31 U133–U137 drafted for the owner's review; owner decisions S5-17 D2 (a), S5-17b 1 (a), 2 (UX copy v30 confirmed)): S21's resolver reads the open invitations too when the signed-in user is an Owner (`loadMembersView` in `pages/members/members-page-load.ts`; the view's `invitations` is null for other roles); for an Owner S21 shows „Offene Einladungen“ (only while there are any; „Einladung zurücknehmen“ revokes at once and announces `org.invite.revoked`) and `pa-invite-form` (`features/organization/components/invite-form/`: address and role as radio buttons with hints, no preselection; a member's address is refused in the client against the loaded list; success announces `org.invite.sent` with the answer's `validDays`; 503/429/antiforgery in the error notice `org.invite.error.title`, after a 503 the list is read again; its field errors set „Fehler:“ in S21's page title through the `errorsShown` output). The page of the link `/organisation/einladung` (`INVITATION_SEGMENT`, `pages/invitation/`, lazy child routes `INVITATION_ROUTES`, no sign-in needed): `invitationTokenGuard` keeps the token in `InvitationPageState` (`invitation-page-state.ts`, memory only) and redirects to the path without it; the view and title resolvers share one `POST /invitations/view` per navigation (`InvitationPageState.loadFor`, keyed by `Router.currentNavigation().id` — a route's resolvers run side by side, so never read a value another resolver of the same route writes); `runGuardsAndResolvers: 'always'`, so closing S01 after a sign-in reads the invitation again; the token stays until accepted or refused; a member's second click redirects to S21 (`RedirectCommand` from the resolver). States: signed out (`org.invite.signIn`, „Anmelden“ → S01, „Konto erstellen“ → S02), invited address („Einladung annehmen“ → announces `org.invite.accepted`, opens S21; 403 `email-unconfirmed` → `pa-confirm-required-notice` with the focus; 400 → „Einladung nicht mehr gültig“ through `changePageInPlace`), other address (`org.invite.mismatch`, no button), invalid (H1, text, „Zur Startseite“), S80 server. `PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS` maps `invitation-mismatch`; `ProblemDetails.invitedEmail` types its extension; `org.invite` is an `AREAS` entry of `check-i18n.mjs`. The UX copy writes `pageTitle.org.invite.invalid` as a row of its own: the table shorthand `/ .invalid` replaces the last key segment, so `pageTitle.org.invite` / `.invalid` would be `pageTitle.org.invalid`. E2E: `e2e/support/accounts.ts` (`registerAccount`, `registerConfirmedAccount`, `createOrganizationThroughApi`, `inviteThroughApi`, `invitationLinkOf`) builds accounts and organisations through the API, so a test changes only its own organisation (`organization.spec.ts` uses it too); `e2e/organization/invitations.spec.ts` runs the journey invite → link signed out → S01 → accept → S21 → role change → removal, SEC-10's E2E accept case and the mismatch and invalid pages; the invite form's `<section>` has no `aria-labelledby`, because a named region there crashed Firefox's page process in axe runs on S21 in CI (TESTING_v69 V226, found on draft pull request #345 — keep it unnamed until a Playwright update proves otherwise), and the journey fills the form by CSS locators until its axe run, because Playwright's label and role lookups there followed by axe crash Firefox as well; `reflow.spec.ts` covers S21 with an open invitation and the form's error summary (both answered or submitted empty, so the seeded organisation stays unchanged) and the page of the link. Next: migration step 1 (roadmap M1, without the packages already done in S1-22 and S1-23).

### 2.64 Signed-in header states and organisation switch (S5-18)

Signed-in header and organisation switch (S5-18, ARCHITECTURE_v63 C149, TESTING_v71 V230–V234, SPEC v2.31 §8.9, R-SHL-02/03; UX copy v32 U138–U139, confirmed by the owner; owner decisions S5-18 1a – 4a): `GET /api/v1/organizations` in the host (`OrganizationEndpoints.ListMineAsync`) answers the signed-in user's organisations `[ { id, name, type, role } ]` by name (German collation) through `IOrganizationMembers.ListOfAsync`, which joins memberships and organisations under the request's filter (no `IgnoreQueryFilters()`); TEN-01 cases in `OrganizationEndpointsTests`, a `list-mine` row in `AuthorizationMatrixTests`. Client: `OrganizationApiService.mine()` (`BACKGROUND_REQUEST`); `withIdentity()` holds `organizations` and `activeOrganizationId` (memory only, decision 3a) and computes `activeOrganization` (the choice while still a member, else the first by name, null without a membership); a new signed-in user reads the organisations next to `GET /api/me`; `organizationJoined(id)` after S20 and accepting an invitation (the new one becomes active), `loadOrganizations()` after leaving on S21, `switchOrganization(id)` from the header; sign-out clears both. `pa-main-nav` shows the states: SignedIn the display `shell.org.active` and the tonal menu button „Konto“ (`shell.userMenu.label` with the address; `mat-menu` with „Konto“, „Organisation“ → S21 of the active organisation or S20, „Einstellungen“, „Abmelden“; „Organisation“ `aria-current` on `/organisation…`), SignedInNoOrg the menu alone; with more than one membership the display is a menu button named by its visible text whose menu (`shell.org.switch`) has a `menuitemradio` per organisation with a check on the active one; the compact drawer lists all of it flat, the switch as a `<ul>` of buttons with the active one `aria-current="true"`. Switching on S21 opens S21 of the chosen organisation; `MembersPage` takes the resolver's new view on a `paramMap` change, because the router keeps the page when only the id changes (the route data stays equal). „Projekte“ is not built (decision 1a); lists, projects and the compare select read `activeOrganization` when they are built (S5-21, workspace and directory steps; decision 4a, V232). Test doubles: `createShellDoubles(signedIn, organizations)` and `TEST_ORGANIZATIONS` in `testing/shell-test-doubles.ts`. E2E: `e2e/shell/header-states.spec.ts` (SignedInNoOrg with the seeded confirmed user, SignedIn with the seeded Reader, the switch wide and compact with an account and two organisations of its own; axe in both appearances on the start page — the legacy product list has an axe finding of its own when signed in, #350), the signed-in compact menu in `reflow.spec.ts`, and `e2e/support/session.ts` `accountMenuButton`, `openAccountMenu`, `signOutThroughHeader` for every sign-out.

### 2.65 Legacy auth cleanup (S5-10)

Legacy auth cleanup (S5-10, ARCHITECTURE_v64 C150, TESTING_v72 V235–V236, SPEC v2.32; owner decisions O-S5-4, S5-10 2a and 3a of 2026-11-16): `UsersController` (`API/…/Controllers/Identity/`) keeps only `DELETE /api/Users`, the account deletion of the legacy profile page, with the primary constructor `(IUserRepo repo)`; a missing user is 404 Problem Details `not-found` (`UserRepo.UserNotFoundCode`), any other failed `IdentityResult` throws for Platform's 500 Problem Details. `change-email`, `change-password`, `revoke-sessions`, `ChangeEmailRequest`, `ChangePasswordRequest` (namespace `MODELS.ProcureAccess.Entities.Identity`, gone) and `UserRepo.ChangeEmailAsync`/`ChangePasswordAsync`/`RevokeSessionsAsync`/`GetAsync`/`RestoreUserAsync` are removed; `UserRepo` takes `TimeProvider` for `DeletedAt`. `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.UI` is no longer referenced (`SERVICES` keeps `Identity.EntityFrameworkCore` and the ASP.NET Core framework reference). `RefreshToken` and its configuration are gone; the legacy migration `DropRefreshTokens` drops `public."RefreshTokens"`. Client: `errorInterceptor` shows `PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS[type]` or the general notice `ERROR.GENERIC` (`GENERAL_ERROR_KEY`); `core/models/error-translation.map.ts` and `features/identity/models/login.model.ts` are deleted, and of the legacy groups only `AUTH.PROFILE`, `.EMAIL`, `.DELETE_ACCOUNT`, `.DELETE_ACCOUNT_WARNING`, `.DELETE_ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION_MSG`, `.YOUR_EMAIL` (profile page and delete-account dialog, until S5-26/S5-28) and `ERROR.GENERIC` remain in `de.json`, `en.json`, `es.json`, `fr.json`. Tests: `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Identity/UsersControllerTests.cs`, `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Api/UsersControllerAuthorizationTests.cs`.

### 2.66 Personal data export (S5-27)

Personal data export (S5-27, ARCHITECTURE_v65 C151, TESTING_v73 V237, SPEC v2.33 §8.4; owner decisions S5-27 1a – 4a of 2026-11-16): `GET /api/v1/account/export` in the host (`API/Account/AccountExportEndpoints.cs`, mapped on the versioned group, global using `API.ProcureAccess.Account`) answers the signed-in user's data as the JSON of SPEC §8.4 — an attachment `ProcureAccess_Daten_<YYYY-MM-DD>.json` (`FileNameOf`, UTC day of `generatedAt`), `Cache-Control: no-store`, indented, records `AccountExport*` in the order of §8.4; 401 anonymous, 404 for a soft-deleted account; no confirmation gate, no antiforgery (GET), no rate limit. Address, confirmation and `createdAt` come from the legacy `User`; memberships (organisation name, role `owner`/`editor`/`reader`, `since` = `AcceptedAt ?? InvitedAt`) and contributor profiles (display name, kind, creation time) from `IAccountDataExport` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts/Accounts/`, implemented by `AccountDataExportService`), read through the organisation query filter of the request; the file carries no ids. Sections of later tasks (decision 1a): `account.preferences` `null` until S5-25, `createdByMe.criteriaLists` empty until S5-19, `createdByMe.projects` until step 5, `contributions` until step 6 — each of these fills its section and adds its `INV:PRV-07` case to `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/IdentityAccess/AccountExportTests.cs`. `User.CreatedAt` (`DateTimeOffset?`, legacy migration `AddUserCreatedAt`) is stamped by `ClockedUserManager.CreateAsync` from `TimeProvider` (decision 2a); accounts from before S5-27 have `null`. Not exported (decision 3a): legacy proposals and open invitations.

### 2.67 Configurator persistence (S5-19)

Configurator persistence (S5-19, ARCHITECTURE_v66 C152 and ADR-017, TESTING_v74 V239 – V243, SPEC v2.34 §8.4; owner decisions S5-19 1a, 2a, 3a and 4 of 2026-11-16): the Configurator module has its own context, `ConfiguratorDbContext` (`Persistence/`, `internal`, schema `configurator`, history table `configurator."__EFMigrationsHistory"`, migration `InitialConfiguratorSchema`, `ConfiguratorDatabaseMigrator`, design-time factory); `AddConfiguratorModule(configuration)` registers it. **Domain** (`Domain/`): `CriteriaConfiguration` (random `Guid` id, `OrganizationId`, `Name` ≤ 120, `ReleaseVersion`, `UseCaseKey?`, `CreatedByUserId`, `CreatedAt`, `ModifiedAt`) is the aggregate; `ConfigurationAppType`, `ConfigurationProductPart`, `ConfigurationTestType` (one value key each) and `ConfigurationCriterion` (`CriterionRevisionId`, `IsIncluded`, `ResolvedLevel`, `OverrideLevel?`, `OverrideWeight?`, `NoteDe` ≤ 1 000) are its rows, mapped as owned collections in `CriteriaConfigurationConfiguration` (tables `ConfigurationAppTypes` …, key list + value, deleted with the list, no `DbSet` of their own) — so the organisation filter on the list covers them, and a query reaches a row only through a list. The release is stored by version and values by key (decision 2a: what `Catalog.Contracts` and the API use; no foreign key leaves the schema, ADR-014). `CriteriaConfiguration.Create(…, criteria: ConfigurationCriterionDraft[])` takes a selection the caller has resolved through the catalog and throws for an invalid list (empty or too long name, no app type, a value or criterion twice); `Decide(revisionId, isIncluded, overrideLevel, overrideWeight, note, now)` stores a decision and stamps `ModifiedAt`. `ResolvedLevel` is set once and never changed (CAT-08); a level equal to the recommendation is stored as no choice, an empty note as none; a weight needs B and is at least 1 — the configured range (🔧 `Configurator:PriorityWeight`) and the check of values and criteria against the release are the save path's (S5-20). **Contracts** (`Configurator.Contracts`, which references `Catalog.Contracts` for `PriorityLevel` — the first contracts → contracts reference, allowed along the table of §4.3 only, ADR-017): `Configurations/IConfigurationReader.FindAsync(id)` → `SavedConfiguration` with `SavedCriterion`s, `null` for a list that does not exist for the request (`Configurations/ConfigurationReader`; the module registers the context with split queries, one per row table); `Accounts/ICriteriaListDataExport.GetCreatedByAsync(userId)` → the lists the user created (`Accounts/CriteriaListDataExportService`: names, numbers and titles from `ICatalogSelectionResolver` under the pinned release, organisation names from `IOrganizationMembers.ListOfAsync`, the stored `ResolvedLevel` as the recommendation). **Export:** `Sources/PersistedConfigurationSource` (`IExportSource<Guid>`, registered, no endpoint yet) reads through the reader, resolves the pinned release also when it is no longer active, answers 404 `not-found` for an unknown list and throws when a list does not hold exactly the criteria of its selection; both sources build rows with `ExportRow.Of` (`Documents/ExportContent.cs`), so they cannot differ (EXP-06). **Host:** `AccountExportEndpoints` fills `createdByMe.criteriaLists` (`AccountExportCriteriaList`, levels as `A`/`B`/`Info`, no ids; through the organisation filter, so not for an organisation the user has left — decision 4). **No endpoint** (decision 3a): `POST`/`GET /api/v1/configurations` come with S5-20, `PATCH`, delete and `POST …/{id}/exports` with S5-21 (the file name of a saved list gets its slug there; `xmin` as the ETag needs no migration). **Tests:** `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit/Configurator/CriteriaConfigurationTests.cs`, `Unit/Export/PersistedConfigurationSourceTests.cs`, `Integration/Configurator/` (`SavedLists` builds a list from the host's resolved `T.1` selection; `CriteriaConfigurationPersistenceTests`), `Integration/Export/ExportSourceParityTests.cs` (content, CSV records and XLSX cells of both sources; CAT-06 stored part), `Integration/Persistence/ConfiguratorMigrationTests.cs` (OPS-03), the sample in `OrganizationIsolationTests`, the PRV-07 case in `AccountExportTests`; `ApiFixture` migrates the context, keeps its history table and stores its entities.
