ARC-03: `ModuleRow.AllowedContractsReferences`, `ModuleReferenceTests` (contracts reference only listed contracts; the table has no cycle), `ModuleBoundaryTests.Export_SavedLists_AreReadOnlyThroughIConfigurationReader`. A scope that reads as a member outside a request loads the scoped `OrganizationContext` first (`ScopeOf` in the tests).

### 2.68 Saved-list endpoints and the shared draft check (S5-20a)

Saved-list endpoints, server part (S5-20a, ARCHITECTURE_v67 C153, TESTING_v75 V244 – V248, SPEC v2.35 §8.10; owner decisions S5-20 1a (split: S5-20b builds S12, S14 and J1), A (a′) and B (a) of 2026-11-16): **draft check** — `IConfigurationDraftCheck.CheckAsync(CriteriaDraft, DraftPurpose)` (`Configurator.Contracts/Drafts/`, records `DraftSelection` and `DraftCriterion` with unknown JSON members refused, `DraftCheckResult` with `DraftCheckOutcome`, `CheckedDraft`, `CheckedCriterion`) is implemented by `Configurator/Drafts/ConfigurationDraftCheck` with the rules and messages that were `AdHocSelectionSource`'s (S4-19) moved unchanged; `PriorityWeightRange` moved with it and `AddConfiguratorModule` reads it at start. The outcome carries no problem type (a contracts project has no `Platform.Contracts`); each caller maps it. `DraftPurpose.Save` refuses an inactive use case (CAT-18, read from `ResolvedCatalogSelection.UseCaseIsActive`), `Export` accepts it. `AdHocSelectionSource` is now a mapper: outcome → the export's Problem Details, the checked draft → `ExportContent` of the included criteria; `AdHocSelectionSourceTests` run unchanged on the moved check. ARC-03 (`ModuleBoundaryTests`): the ad-hoc source may use of `Configurator.Contracts` only `Drafts`, only `PersistedConfigurationSource` the other types; `ModuleRules` takes forbidden types by name too (`TopLevelTypeNames`). **Endpoints** (`Configurator/Configurations/ConfigurationEndpoints`, mapped by `MapConfiguratorEndpoints` below `/configurations`, all `RequireAuthorization()`; `ConfigurationService` on the filtered context): `POST /api/v1/configurations` `{ organizationId, name, selection, criteria[] }` — `ConfirmedEmail`, `RequestSizeLimit` 1 MB; role check Editor on `organizationId` first (`OrganizationReference`, 404/403), then name (trimmed, ≤ 120) and the draft check, whose field errors join the name's in one answer; 201 `{ id }`, `Location: /api/v1/configurations/{id}`; each row stores the catalog's recommendation (`ConfigurationCriterionDraft` with `RecommendedPriority.Level`). `GET /api/v1/configurations/{id}`: the filter answers 404 for another organisation's list; decisions by revision id, levels `A`/`B`/`Info` (`ConfigurationService.LevelName`). `GET /api/v1/configurations?organizationId=` (Reader): summaries without rows, most recently changed first. No rate limit, no EF migration; `no-store` is S5-22's (TEN-04 workspace part). **Tests:** `Unit/Configurator/ConfigurationDraftCheckTests.cs` (CAT-18 both purposes, the checked draft, the weight range), `Integration/Configurator/ConfigurationEndpointsTests.cs` (save, field errors, release-mismatch, selection-excluded, CAT-18, SEC-10 before and after the confirmation, TEN-01 on read and list), rows `save-list`, `read-list`, `list-lists` in `Tenancy/AuthorizationMatrixTests` (every fixture user with a confirmed legacy account; the save rows import `T.1` on first use), `Integration/Infrastructure/LegacyAccounts.cs` (legacy account of a fixture user, confirm).

Saving and the saved lists, client part (S5-20b, ARCHITECTURE_v68 C154, TESTING_v76 V249 – V254, SPEC v2.36, UX copy v33 U140 – U144 (drafts); owner decisions S5-20 2a – 7a and S5-20b 1a – 4a of 2026-11-16): **S11** shows „Liste speichern“ (outlined, before „Liste exportieren“; `saveAction`): signed out `AuthService.showLogin('save')`; S01 calls `keepDraftAfterSignIn()`, so S11 shows `auth.login.draftKept` until it is left (`forgetDraftKept()` on destroy) — `withConfigurator().draftKept`, memory only; signed in without membership the shared `ConfirmDialog` with `criteria.save.needOrg` → S20; a Reader of the active organisation sees no button; an Editor or Owner gets **S12** `SaveListDialog` (`dialogs/save-list-dialog/`; S13's panel class and rules; name required and ≤ 120 after trimming; `toSaveRequest` in `state/export-request.ts` builds the body from the export's; errors as in C154; success: `navigateByUrl('/kriterienlisten/:id')`, then `resetConfigurator()`, then the announcement). **A saved list** (`pages/saved-list/`, `SAVED_LIST_ROUTES` with `authGuard` and `savedListResolver`, `loadSavedListView` → `SavedListView` of list, selection, criteria and filter options of the list's own release, `decisionsOf` keys the stored decisions by criterion key): `SavedListPage` shows S11 read only — `pa-criterion-card` and `pa-priority-indicator` have a `readonly` input (no checkbox, menu, weight field, reset or note field; the note under `criteria.item.note.heading`). **S14** `SavedListsPage` (`pages/saved-lists/`, `authGuard`): an effect on the active organisation's id loads `ConfiguratorApiService.list` (again on the header switch), `sortSavedLists` (by `modifiedAt` desc or `Intl.Collator('de')`, each the other's tie-breaker), `mat-select` „Sortieren nach“, cards / `mat-table` by `injectWideLayout()`, `common.loaded` and `criteria.saved.sort.updated` announced. **Menu:** `main-nav` shows „Kriterienlisten“ (`/kriterienlisten`) with a membership, in the account menu and the compact list. `check-i18n.mjs` `AREAS` has `criteria.save` and `criteria.saved`. **Tests:** `save-list-dialog.spec.ts` (S11 states and S12, `INV:SEC-10`), `saved-lists-page.spec.ts` (`INV:TEN-01` switch), `saved-list-page.spec.ts`, the S11, S13, main-nav and login specs extended; E2E: J1's second test (`journeys/j1-configurator.spec.ts`; `test.fixme` on Firefox until #355, TESTING V254; own account through `support/accounts.ts` `organizationOfAccount`), S12, S14 and a saved list in `shell/reflow.spec.ts` (answered by `page.route`), `configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` with the second button.

### 2.70 Changing, deleting, copying and exporting a saved list, server part (S5-21a)

Saved-list changes, server part (S5-21a, ARCHITECTURE_v69 C155, TESTING_v77 V255 – V262, SPEC v2.37 §8.10, §8.3; owner decisions S5-21 1a – 8b and a of 2026-11-16, plus the defaults of the plan: every change gated by a confirmed address, Readers may export, the slug rule): **Endpoints** in `Configurator/Configurations/ConfigurationEndpoints` (each `RequireAuthorization(ConfirmedEmail)`, antiforgery by the host; `RefusedForListAsync` takes the organisation from the stored list through the filter — 404 — and checks Editor on it — 403): `PATCH /api/v1/configurations/{id}/criteria/{criterionRevisionId}` (`ConfigurationDecisionBody`, unknown members refused; `ConfigurationService.DecideAsync` checks level name, the weight range `PriorityWeightRange` and B only, the note limit, keyed `isIncluded`, `overrideLevel`, `overrideWeight`, `note`; 404 for a criterion not on the list; 204; also on a list of an older release), `PUT …/{id}/selection` (`ChangeSelectionBody` with a `DraftSelection`; `ChangeSelectionAsync`: the body's release must be the list's and active, else 400 `release-mismatch`; resolved through `ICatalogSelectionResolver`, errors keyed `selection.…` with `ConfigurationDraftCheck.SelectionKey`; an inactive use case refused (CAT-18); 200 `ChangedSelection { droppedDecisions }`), `DELETE …/{id}` (204), `POST …/{id}/copies` (`CopyConfigurationBody { release: "active" }`; `CopyToActiveReleaseAsync`; 201 `CopiedConfiguration { id, releaseVersion, droppedValues, notCarried }`). Service results are `EditResult<T>` with `EditOutcome`, mapped by `ProblemOf`. **Domain:** `CriteriaConfiguration.ChangeSelection(…)` replaces values and rows in place (rows that stay are the same objects); `ConfigurationCriterion.Reset(draft)` — the only path that sets `ResolvedLevel` anew (CAT-08) — and `HasDecision` (excluded, level, weight or note; ignored by EF). `Configurations/DecisionCarryOver.Carry(revision, recommendation, previous)` moves a decision as it is; a weight without a chosen level becomes „B chosen“ when the new recommendation is not B (owner decision S5-21 a). Reports name criteria as `ConfigurationCriterionName(revisionId, number, title)` (plain-language title, else the catalog title). **Catalog:** `Catalog.Contracts/ICatalogReleaseCarryOver` (`CatalogCarryOverResult`, `CatalogCarryOverOutcome`), implemented by `Catalog/Criteria/CatalogReleaseCarryOver` (registered scoped): resolves the old selection under its release, `Trim` drops an inactive use case and every value a kept higher-layer value of the active release excludes (layer order, named), resolves the rest under the active release and maps each old revision to its successor by key or `SupersededByKey` (followed; read from the database, not the release cache). A merge goes to the old criterion with its own key, then one with a decision. **Export:** `Http/SavedExportEndpoint` — `POST /api/v1/configurations/{id}/exports` (`SavedExportRequest { format }`, any member, `no-store`, `ExportCreated` signed in, body ≤ 4 KB, policy `RateLimitPolicies.SavedExport` per user 60 / 10 min 🔧 in `appsettings.json`, relaxed in the integration host and `docker-compose.e2e.yml`); `ExportSourceResult.ListName` carries the name from `PersistedConfigurationSource`; `ExportFileWriter.Write(content, format, listName)` puts `SlugOf(name)` after „Kriterien“ (umlauts and ß spelled out, accents dropped, other characters `-`, ≤ 50, case kept). No ETag (owner decision S5-21 2a, API-03 with step 5), no EF migration. **Tests:** `Unit/Configurator/DecisionCarryOverTests.cs`, `Unit/Configurator/CriteriaConfigurationTests.cs` (`ChangeSelection_*`, `HasDecision_*`), `Unit/Catalog/Criteria/CatalogReleaseCarryOverTests.cs`, `Unit/Export/ExportFileNameTests.cs`; `Integration/Configurator/SavedListChangeTests.cs` (decide, selection, delete, copy incl. CAT-22 on `T.1` → `T.2`, TEN-01, SEC-10), `Integration/Export/SavedExportEndpointTests.cs` (EXP-01, EXP-03 persisted, usage event, format, 404); rows `decide-in-list`, `change-selection`, `delete-list`, `copy-list`, `export-list` in `Tenancy/AuthorizationMatrixTests` (the last three on a list saved through the API, `copy-list` with `T.2` activated afterwards); `Api/RateLimitTests.SavedExport_*`. The screens, the delete dialog, the older-release notice and the J1 file comparison are S5-21b.

### 2.71 Saved lists on the client: edit, „Auswahl ändern“, copy, export, delete (S5-21b)

Saved-list changes, client part (S5-21b, ARCHITECTURE_v70 C156, TESTING_v78 V263 – V266, SPEC v2.38, UX copy v34 U145 – U147; owner decisions S5-21b 1a – 6a of 2026-11-16): **A saved list** (`pages/saved-list/saved-list-page`): `canEdit` from the user's role in the list's organisation (`organizations()`), not the header's. Editor and Owner: S11's cards with outputs into `SavedListEditor` (`saved-list-editor.ts`, route-level provider beside `SavedListLoad`; `start`, `setIncluded`, `overridePriority`, `setWeight`, `resetPriority`, `setNote`, `flush`; S11's rules from `criteria-list-rules`; `PATCH` per change through `ConfiguratorApiService.decide`, one chain of saves per criterion with the newest decision, note after `NOTE_SAVE_DELAY_MS` 800 and not while too long, a failure resets to the last saved decision unless changed again; `toDecisionRequest`), the filter row with its own search delay, the counts and the live counter announced as on S11, „Auswahl ändern“ below the H1 (not on an older list), and `pa-criteria-action-bar` with „Liste exportieren“. Readers: read-only cards, filter row, the bar with „Liste exportieren“ only. `SavedListView.activeRelease` (filter values without release, `null` on 404) decides the older-release notice (Editor and Owner only) with „Neue Liste mit derselben Auswahl“ (`copyToActiveRelease`, then `navigateByUrl(savedListPath(copy.id), { state })`). `SavedListArrival` / `SavedListNavigationState` in `saved-list-load.ts`: the copy's or the selection change's report, read from `router.currentNavigation()` on every `show()`, shown as an info notice and announced (`criteriaOf` joins „number title“). **„Auswahl ändern“** (`saved-list-selection.ts`): child route `auswahl` of `SAVED_LIST_ROUTES` (the list route is now `pathMatch: 'full'`) loads S10 with `savedListSelectionGuard` (list, role, active release, then `startSavedListSelection`) and the route-level `SavedListSelection { list }`; S10 (`criteria-select`) reads it optionally: „Abbrechen“ (`common.actions.cancel`) to the list, submit → `submitSelection`, `changeSelection` (`PUT …/selection`), navigate with the dropped decisions; `onDestroy` → `endSavedListSelection`. `withConfigurator()`: `startSavedListSelection(selection, release)` parks the configurator state and fills the draft (`draftOf`), `endSavedListSelection()` restores it (a request that was running is asked again), `resetConfigurator()` drops the parked state too. **S13** (`ExportDialog.open(dialog, savedList?)`, `SavedListExport`): release and count from the list, `ExportApiService.exportSavedList(id, format)`. **S14** (`saved-lists-page`): `canDelete` for Editor and Owner of the header's organisation, „Liste löschen“ in the card and in the name cell of the table (`aria-describedby` = `nameId(list)`), `ConfirmDialog` with `criteria.saved.delete.*`, `delete` without announcing the reload, then `focusPageHeading()`. **Shared:** `components/criteria-action-bar/` (`pa-criteria-action-bar`, `STICKY_ACTION_BAR_QUERY` moved here from S11) — S11 and the saved list. `ConfiguratorApiService`: `decide`, `changeSelection`, `delete`, `copyToActiveRelease` (failures shown by the interceptor); models in `models/saved-lists.ts` (`SavedListDecisionRequest`, `SavedListSelectionRequest`, `ChangedSelection`, `CopiedList`, `SavedListCriterionName`, `savedListSelectionPath`). **Tests:** `saved-list-page.spec.ts`, `saved-list-editor.spec.ts`, `saved-lists-page.spec.ts` (delete), `export-dialog.spec.ts` (saved list), `criteria-select.spec.ts` (saved-list mode), `with-configurator.spec.ts` (put aside); E2E: J1's second test exports the draft and the saved list and compares the files, then deletes through S14 (`exportThroughS13`); `reflow.spec.ts` the editable list and the delete dialog; `s11-list.spec.ts` locates `pa-criteria-action-bar`.

### 2.72 Saved-list debt: weight range, too-long note, unsaved state, no-store (S5-22)

Saved-list debt M3.5d (S5-22, ARCHITECTURE_v71 C157, TESTING_v79 V267 – V268, SPEC v2.39 §8.11, UX copy v35 U148 – U149; owner decisions S5-22 1a, 2a, 3b, 4 of 2026-11-16; TECH_DEBT TD-40, TD-45). **Weight range (TD-45):** `GET /api/v1/configurator/settings` (`Configurator/Settings/ConfiguratorSettingsEndpoint.cs`, anonymous, no rate limit) answers `ConfiguratorSettingsResponse { PriorityWeight { Min, Max } }` from the singleton `PriorityWeightRange`. Client: `features/configurator/state/configurator-settings.ts` (`ConfiguratorSettings`, root; `ready()` loads once and resolves false after a failure, which is not kept; `weightRange()` throws before it loaded) and `ConfiguratorApiService.settings()` (not marked: a failure shows the system state). `completeSelectionGuard` of S11 awaits `ready()` (false keeps the user where they are) and `savedListResolver` loads it beside the list (a failure is the `server` view). `withConfigurator`, `CriterionCard` and `SavedListEditor` read `weightRange()`; `PRIORITY_WEIGHT_RANGE` and `environment*.priorityWeight` are gone. Specs that render cards provide `provideLoadedConfiguratorSettings(range?)` from `features/configurator/testing/configurator-settings-stub.ts`. **`no-store` (TEN-04 workspace part):** `Platform/Caching/NoStoreDefault.cs` (`UseNoStoreDefault`, first in `UsePlatformMiddleware`) sets `Cache-Control: no-store` in `Response.OnStarting` on every answer below `/api/v1` that has none; the catalog's own header stays. **Too-long note (TD-40):** `firstTooLongNote(criteria, decisions)` in `criteria-list-rules.ts` (list order, excluded criteria too). `ExportDialog` checks it before any request (the draft from the store; a saved list passes `SavedListExport.tooLongNote` from its editor), shows status `noteTooLong` with `criteria.export.noteTooLong` and turns the same primary button into „Zur Notiz“ (`primary()`), which closes with `ExportDialogResult { toNote }`. S11 and the saved-list page subscribe to `afterClosed()`, clear a filter that hides the card and call `CriterionCard.showNote()` (open, touched, focus) after the next render through `viewChildren(CriterionCard)`. `toggleNote()` keeps a too-long note open and calls `showNote()`. **Unsaved state:** `CriteriaActionBar` takes `saveState` (a second line in the status block, Figma `2034:1196`); S11 passes `criteria.list.unsaved`, a saved list nothing.

### 2.73 Share links: model, management API, anonymous read (S5-23)

Share links M3.5 (S5-23, ARCHITECTURE_v72 C158, TESTING_v80 V269 – V271, SPEC v2.40 §8.5, §8.10; owner decisions S5-23 1a – 4a, 5 – 9 and a of 2026-11-16; TEN-08, TEN-12 active). **Model:** `Platform/Sharing/ShareLink.cs` (`OrganizationId`, `TokenHash` SHA-256, `ResourceType` = `ShareableResourceType.Configuration`, `ResourceId`, `CreatedAt`, `ExpiresAt`, `RevokedAt?`; `IsActiveAt`, `Revoke` keeps the first revocation), table `sharing."ShareLinks"` (`Persistence/Configurations/ShareLinkConfiguration.cs`, migration `AddShareLinks`, unique index on the hash, index on resource type and id). `PlatformDbContext` takes `IOrganizationContext` and filters `ShareLinks` (TEN-01); every construction outside DI passes `FixedOrganizationContext.None` (`UsageEventStore`, the design-time factory, the test fixtures), and `CatalogCommandHost` registers `FixedOrganizationContext.None` because it has no IdentityAccess module. **Contracts** (`Platform.Contracts/Sharing/`): `IShareLinks` (`CreateAsync` → `ShareLinkCreation` with the link or the expiry error, `ListActiveAsync`, `FindAsync` → `ShareLinkTarget`, `RevokeAsync`), `IShareableResourceProvider` (`ReadSharedAsync(resourceId, expiresAt)` → the redacted view or `null`), `ShareableResourceType`. **Service:** `Platform/Sharing/ShareLinkService.cs` (scoped; also `IShareLinks`): 32 random bytes, Base64url (43 characters), only the hash stored; `ReadSharedAsync(token)` refuses anything that is not 43 characters before decoding, looks the link up by hash with `IgnoreQueryFilters()` (TEN-02 allow-list), checks revoked and expired, then asks the provider of the link's type; one `null` (404) for every failure. Expiry: `ShareLinkSettings` (🔧 `ShareLinks:MaxExpiryMonths` 12, `ShareLinks:TimeZone` Europe/Berlin, read once in `AddPlatformModule`, an unknown zone stops the start) — `expiresOn` from tomorrow to today plus the maximum, both in the zone; `ExpiresAt` = next midnight in the zone, in UTC. **Anonymous read:** `Platform/Sharing/SharedEndpoints.cs`, `GET /api/v1/shared/{token}`, mapped by the new `PlatformModule.MapPlatformApiEndpoints()` on the versioned group in `Program.cs`; `AllowAnonymous`, no rate limit, `no-store` from the default. **Management** (Configurator, `Sharing/ShareLinkEndpoints.cs`): `POST`/`GET /configurations/{id}/share-links`, `DELETE /share-links/{id}`; the organisation from the list (`ConfigurationService.OrganizationOfAsync`) or the link (`IShareLinks.FindAsync`), then `ConfigurationEndpoints.RefusedAsync`/`RefusedForListAsync` (now `internal`) with Editor; `ConfirmedEmail` on POST and DELETE; body `CreateShareLinkBody(DateOnly? ExpiresOn)` refuses unknown members; field error at `expiresOn`. **Provider:** `Configurator/Sharing/ConfigurationShareProvider.cs` reads the list with `IgnoreQueryFilters()` (TEN-02 allow-list) and answers `SharedConfigurationResponse` (`Name`, `ReleaseVersion`, `ExpiresAt`, `Selection`, `Criteria`) through `ConfigurationService.SelectionOf`/`DecisionsOf`, which `FindAsync` shares. A deleted list's links stay and answer 404 (S5-23 8). **Logs:** `Platform/Logging/ShareTokenMaskingEnricher.cs` (registered after `FromLogContext`) masks `/api/v…/shared/<token>` in every text property; the client's `nginx.conf` logs `$log_uri` and `$log_referer` (maps that mask `/api/v…/shared/…` and `/geteilt/…`). **Tests:** `Configurator/ShareLinkTests.cs` (TEN-08, TEN-12, TEN-01, SEC-10; the server part of TEN-09), `Operations/LogSinkTests` (masking in the sink), `OrganizationIsolationTests` (`ShareLink` sample), `AuthorizationMatrixTests` (three rows), `IgnoreQueryFiltersAllowListTests` (two entries).

`ConfiguratorApiService.shareLinks|createShareLink|revokeShareLink|shared`, all `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES`; **S15** `dialogs/share-dialog/` (`ShareDialog.open(dialog, { listId })`, `autoFocus: 'dialog'`, the focus into „Gültig bis“ after `afterOpened` and the load; E2E `configurator/s15-s16-share.spec.ts` (TEN-09, own account through `saveListThroughApi` in `support/accounts.ts`) and S15, S16 in `shell/reflow.spec.ts`.

### 2.75 User preferences, server part (S5-25a)

Settings M3.7 (S5-25a, ARCHITECTURE_v74 C160, TESTING_v82 V276 – V277, SPEC v2.42 §8.4, §8.12, R-SET-01, R-SET-02; owner decisions S5-25 1a, 3 (System/High, (i)), 4 of 2026-11-16; the PRV-07 preferences part complete). **Model (1a):** `UserPreferences` (`IdentityAccess/Domain/UserPreferences.cs`, with `AppearancePreference`, `MotionPreference`, `ContrastPreference`) in `IdentityAccessDbContext`, table `identity."UserPreferences"` (migration `AddUserPreferences`), key `UserId` without a foreign key (ADR-014), the enums as text; no query filter (not organisation-scoped); `Defaults(userId)` is German and `System` × 3, `Change(…)` replaces all four and throws for anything but `de` or an undefined value. **Endpoints:** `GET` / `PUT /api/v1/account/preferences` (`Accounts/UserPreferencesEndpoints.cs`, mapped by `MapIdentityAccessEndpoints` on the versioned group), any signed-in user, no confirmed-address gate, no rate limit; GET answers the defaults without storing; PUT checks each field (`validation` at `language`, `appearance`, `motion`, `contrast`; exact lower-case spellings; `high` contrast refused until H2, decision 3) and answers the stored values. `UserPreferencesService` stores them; a unique violation of two first saves at once is retried once as an update (the last save wins). `UserPreferenceSpelling` is the one place of the spellings (`system`, `light`, `dark`, `reduced`, `high`). **Export (4):** `IUserPreferences` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts/Accounts/`, record `UserPreferencesValues`) gives `AccountExportEndpoints` the effective values as `AccountExportPreferences`; `account.preferences` is never `null` any more. Tests: `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/IdentityAccess/UserPreferencesTests.cs` and two cases of `AccountExportTests` (`INV:PRV-07`). **Still legacy until S5-25b:** the client's settings store (`features/settings/state/with-settings.ts`, `localStorage`), the page `/settings` and the server's `UICustomization` (JSON column of `identity."Users"`, `UICustomizationController`) — S5-25b replaces them and activates A11Y-06 (done, §2.76).

### 2.76 User preferences, client part (S5-25b)

Settings M3.7 and M3.7d (S5-25b, ARCHITECTURE_v75 C161, TESTING_v83 V278 – V282, SPEC v2.43 R-SET-01, R-SET-02, §8.12; owner decisions S5-25 1a, 2a, 3 (i), 4 of 2026-11-16; A11Y-06 and the explicit-setting part of A11Y-08 active; TD-25 of #234 done). **Slice:** `withSettings` (`features/settings/state/with-settings.ts`, needs `withIdentity()` before it — typed with `type<{ session }>()`) keeps `preferences` in memory; its `onInit` sets German (`TranslateService.use`, since S6-04; `LanguageService` is gone) and an effect on the session's user id calls `loadPreferences(id)`: `PreferencesApiService.get()` (`GET /api/v1/account/preferences`, `BACKGROUND_REQUEST`), applied through `AppearanceService`; `null` (signed out) or a failed read follow the system; an answer for an earlier user is dropped. classes `theme-*`, `motion-reduced`, `contrast-more` on `<html>`; `provideAppearance()` answers `MATERIAL_ANIMATIONS.animationsDisabled` through a getter (components created after a change follow it); `styles/theme.scss` shortens transitions under `html.motion-reduced` (0.01 ms) and keeps `.contrast-more` with the legacy high-contrast colours (decision 3 (i), H2 open). **Removed (1a, 2a):** client — the legacy store, `shared/state/with-local-storage.ts`, the page `features/settings/settings.*` with route `settings` and both menu entries (`main-nav`), `uiCustomization.model.ts`, `settings-api.service.ts`, `SETTINGS.*` in the four `public/i18n/*.json`, the icons `contrast`, `contrast_rtl_off`, `dark_mode`, `light_mode` (font subset regenerated), four `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` entries (`app.store.ts` included); server — `UICustomization`, `UICustomizationDto`, `Language`, `Languages`, the language maps of `AccountMappings`, `UICustomizationRepo` and its interface, `UICustomizationController`, the owned JSON configuration and its column (legacy migration `DropUserUICustomization`). **Tests:** `with-settings.spec.ts` (`INV:A11Y-06`, TD-25 cases), `appearance.spec.ts`; E2E `e2e/identity/preferences.spec.ts` (`INV:A11Y-06`, `INV:A11Y-08`, `INV:A11Y-01`; storage empty and blocked), J8 strict storage, the A11Y-05 E2E check `lang="de"`. **Two CI findings of #362:** `e2e/support/axe.ts` waits for running finite animations and transitions before every axe run and has `explicitAppearance` (one run, the page's `theme-*` class); J1's `exportThroughS13` waits for S13's first focus before tabbing.

The M3 group of `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` is empty (M2: 5 files, M5: 10 files remain).

### 2.77 Settings page S60 (S5-26)

Settings M3.7 (S5-26, ARCHITECTURE_v76 C162, TESTING_v84 V283 – V285, SPEC v2.44 R-SET-01, UX copy v37 U157; owner decisions S5-26 D1 (a), D2 (a) of 2026-11-17; R-SET-01 met, A11Y-08 complete). **Route:** `/einstellungen` (`SETTINGS_PATH` in `features/settings/models/user-preferences.ts`, with `SYSTEM_PREFERENCES`), `loadChildren` of `features/settings/pages/settings/settings.routing.ts`: `authGuard`, route-level `SettingsLoad` (`settings-load.ts`) and `settingsResolver`, which calls `store.readPreferences()` with the loading bar and titles `pageTitle.settings`, or `pageTitle.server` when the read fails (then the page shows `pa-error-page kind="server"`). „Einstellungen“ in `layout/main-nav` (menu „Konto“ and compact menu, after „Kriterienlisten“, `settingsLink`). **Page** `settings-page.{ts,html,scss}`: H1, intro, one outlined card with four `fieldset`s of `mat-radio-group` in the Handoff order — „Sprache“ (one option `de`, `settings.language.de`, hint linked by `aria-describedby`; D1 (a)), „Erscheinungsbild“, „Bewegung und Animationen“, „Kontrast“ (only `system` until H2); no button. A choice calls `store.changePreferences({ ...preferences(), ...part })` and announces `settings.saved` when it resolves `true`; a rejection is left to the error interceptor (D2 (a)). **Slice** `withSettings`: `readPreferences()` (`PreferencesApiService.read()`, `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES`, rejects on failure) and `changePreferences(next)` (applies at once, then `PreferencesApiService.save()` — `PUT`, unmarked — chained after the save in flight; resolves `true` only for the newest change; a failed save rejects and keeps the choice); one counter `latest` orders `loadPreferences`, `readPreferences` and `changePreferences` (it replaced `requestedFor`): only the newest answer is applied. i18n area `settings` in `check-i18n.mjs`. **Tests:** `with-settings.spec.ts` (S60 cases, `INV:A11Y-08`), `settings-page.spec.ts` (`INV:A11Y-06`, axe), `settings.routing.spec.ts`, `preferences-api.service.spec.ts`, `main-nav.spec.ts`; E2E `identity/preferences.spec.ts` (S60 test: `INV:A11Y-08`, `INV:A11Y-06`, `INV:A11Y-01`), S60 in `shell/reflow.spec.ts`, „Einstellungen“ in `header-states.spec.ts`; `shell-test-doubles.ts` routes `einstellungen` and `kriterienlisten` instead of the legacy `settings`.

### 2.78 Account deletion, server part (S5-28a)

Account deletion M3.8 (S5-28a, ARCHITECTURE_v77 C163, TESTING_v85 V286 – V288, SPEC v2.45 §8.6, §8.13, R-AUTH-10; owner decisions S5-28 1a, 2a, 3, 4, 5a, 6a and P1 – P3 of 2026-11-17; TEN-11 complete). **Endpoint:** `DELETE /api/v1/account` in the host (`API/Account/AccountDeletionEndpoints.cs`, `MapAccountDeletionEndpoints` on the versioned group; until M3.1): signed in, antiforgery, no body, no confirmed address, no rate limit. 204 signs out (`SignInManager.SignOutAsync`, new antiforgery token through `IdentityAccessModule.IssueAntiforgeryToken`); 404 for an account deleted before; 409 `sole-owner` (`ProblemTypes.SoleOwner`) with `organizations` (`OrganizationsExtension`), the names of the organisations with other members whose only Owner the user is. **Order** (no transaction spans the module contexts, ADR-014; every step can be repeated): `IAccountDeletion.BeginAsync` → `ICriteriaListDeletion.DeleteOfOrganizationsAsync` → `IShareLinks.DeleteOfOrganizationsAsync` → `IAccountDeletion.CompleteAsync` → the legacy `User`: address and user name `deleted-<guid>@deleted.invalid`, `EmailConfirmed` false, `PasswordHash` null, `IsDeleted`, `DeletedAt` from `TimeProvider`, stored by `UserManager.UpdateSecurityStampAsync` (one update, new stamp). **IdentityAccess:** `IAccountDeletion` (`Contracts/Accounts`) and `AccountDeletionService` (`Accounts/`): each step is one transaction inside the execution strategy that locks the rows of the user's organisations in the order of their ids (`OrganizationRowLock`), then reads the members; `BeginAsync` returns `AccountDeletionStart` (`SoleOwnerOf`, `OrganizationsToDelete`) and deletes the invitations of the organisations whose only member the user is; `CompleteAsync` checks again and deletes these organisations (memberships and invitations cascade), the user's memberships, `UserPreferences` row and `AccountTokens`. Everything through the organisation filter of the request (the user's own organisations), with `ExecuteDeleteAsync`; no allow-list entry. Contributor profiles and links are not touched (decision 3). **Configurator:** `ICriteriaListDeletion` (`Contracts/Accounts`) and `CriteriaListDeletionService` delete the lists of the given organisations (rows cascade); the handler of `DELETE /configurations/{id}` calls `IShareLinks.DeleteOfResourceAsync` after the list is deleted (S5-23 R5). **Platform:** `IShareLinks` gains `DeleteOfResourceAsync` and `DeleteOfOrganizationsAsync` (hard deletes through the filter). **Tests:** `Integration/IdentityAccess/AccountDeletionTests.cs` (rows are read with contexts of `FixedOrganizationContext.None` and `IgnoreQueryFilters()`; a second request after a deletion needs a new client, because the answer's antiforgery token is an anonymous one while the test scheme still signs the user in), `ShareLinkTests.DeleteList_…`. **Not here:** the client and the removal of the legacy deletion are S5-28b (§2.79).

### 2.79 Account page S61 and the legacy profile removed (S5-28b)

Account M3.8 (S5-28b, ARCHITECTURE_v78 C164, TESTING_v86 V289 – V292, SPEC v2.46 §8.13, UX copy v38 U158 – U159, Handoff 1.29; owner decisions S5-28 1a, 5a, P2 (a), P3 (a) and S5-28b 1 – 3 of 2026-11-17; R-AUTH-09 and R-AUTH-10 met for what is built, #234 closed). **Server:** `GET /api/v1/account` in the host (`API/Account/AccountEndpoints.cs`, `{ email, emailConfirmed }`, 404 for a deleted account). The legacy `UsersController` (`DELETE /api/Users`), `IUserRepo` and `UserRepo` with their registration and tests are gone. **Route:** `/konto` (`ACCOUNT_PATH` in `features/identity/models/account.ts`, import-free; also `Account`, `AccountExportFile`), `loadChildren` of `features/identity/pages/account/account.routing.ts`: `authGuard`, route-level `AccountLoad` (`account-load.ts`) and `accountResolver`, which reads the account with the loading bar and titles `pageTitle.account`, or `pageTitle.server` when the read fails. „Konto“ in `layout/main-nav` (`accountLink`). **API service** `features/identity/services/api/account-api.service.ts`: `get()`, `export()` (blob with the file name of `Content-Disposition`; no marker, so the interceptor shows failures) and `delete()` with `CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` `DELETE_ACCOUNT_PROBLEMS` (`sole-owner`). `fileNameOf` moved from the configurator's `export-request.ts` to `core/files/content-disposition.ts`. **Page** `account-page.{ts,html,scss}`: H1, `pa-confirm-required-notice` for an unconfirmed address, two outlined cards (plain `div`s, no named `section`: the Firefox rule of CLAUDE §3) with one outlined button each, spinner and `aria-disabled` while busy. `delete()` clears the refusal, asks through `DeleteAccountDialog.confirm`, calls the API; on 204 `store.forgetUser()`, `router.navigateByUrl('/')`, `pageTitle.focusPageHeading()`; on 409 `sole-owner` the names of `ProblemDetails.organizations` show in `pa-notice` error `account.delete.error.title` (`live="assertive"`). **Dialog** `features/identity/dialogs/delete-account-dialog/` (rebuilt in place; its spec stays the example of CLAUDE §9): a form with one outlined `type="email"` field labelled `auth.login.email` (an e-mail field drops surrounding spaces itself), „Konto behalten“ and the submit button with `[aria-disabled]="!matches()"` — the Material button's own input, because its host binding overwrites `[attr.aria-disabled]` when that starts as `true` — and `aria-describedby` to the visible reason; closes with `true` only when the typed address equals the account's exactly; `autoFocus: 'first-heading'`. **Store:** `withIdentity().forgetSession()` (the session cleared without a request) and `ProcureAccessStore.forgetUser()`, which `endSession` now shares; `deleteAccount`, `IdentityApiService`, `SnackbarService` and `Router` left `with-identity.ts`, and its `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` entry with them. `PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS['sole-owner']`. **Removed:** `features/identity/profile/`, `dialogs/update-user-dialog/`, `services/identity-api.service.ts`, the route `/profile`, the legacy keys `AUTH.*` (the whole group), `COMMON.CANNOT_UNDO_WARNING` / `.VALUE` / `.CHANGE` / `.DELETE` and six `PROPOSALS.*` keys in the four language files. **Texts:** `account` in `AREAS` of `scripts/check-i18n.mjs`; the script drops „⚖️“ in front of a UX copy text (`account.delete.text` is the first ⚖️ text of a built area). **Tests:** `account-page.spec.ts` (the real router through `provideRouter([])`, because `pa-error-page` needs it; `navigateByUrl` replaced on the instance), `account.routing.spec.ts`, `delete-account-dialog.spec.ts`; E2E `e2e/identity/account.spec.ts` (an aria-disabled button counts as disabled for Playwright: check `toHaveJSProperty('disabled', false)` and activate it by keyboard) and S61 in `shell/reflow.spec.ts`. **Local E2E without images** (the disk was too full to build them): PostgreSQL with `--tmpfs` and Mailpit as plain containers, the API with `dotnet run --no-launch-profile` in the environment `E2E` with the variables of `docker-compose.e2e.yml`, the client with `npx ng serve`.

### 2.80 Session hardening (S5-29)

Session hardening M4.1 (S5-29, ARCHITECTURE_v79 C165, TESTING_v87 V293 – V296, SPEC v2.47 §8.8; owner decisions S5-29 1a, 2a, 3a, 4a of 2026-11-17; SEC-07 and SEC-08 `active`). **Server** (`IdentityAccess/Session/`): `SessionCookieEvents.ValidatePrincipal` decides everything in this order — the limits (a session whose idle or absolute limit is reached is rejected and the cookie deleted; the cookie handler alone would still accept a ticket in the instant of its expiry), Identity's security-stamp validator, the renewal. Every authenticated request except the probe (`SessionProbeMetadata`) renews to min(now + `Session:IdleTimeout`, sign-in time + `Session:AbsoluteTimeout`): the handler renews by the ticket's own length, so the event sets `IssuedUtc` and `ExpiresUtc` and `ShouldRenew`; `CheckSlidingExpiration` always answers no. On the probe `ShouldRenew` is set back to false, because the stamp validator asks for a renewal whenever it checked — keep that line. The same event writes `X-Session-Expires-At` and `X-Session-Absolute-Expires-At` (ISO 8601 UTC, whole seconds) on every answer to an authenticated request, `SigningIn` on the answer of the sign-in, `SigningOut` removes them. `SessionCookieEvents.AbsoluteExpiry` cuts the absolute limit to the whole second (S6-01, #379): the ticket stores the idle limit in whole seconds, so the probe's `absoluteExpiresAt` with the fraction of the sign-in second named an absolute limit later than the idle limit capped at it, and the client's warning opened for the idle limit and again for the absolute one with the next answer; keep the probe, the headers and the check on the same whole second. `SessionExtendEndpoint` (`POST /api/v1/auth/session/extend`, signed in, antiforgery) answers 204 and does nothing: being an authenticated request is what renews. `SessionSettings` reads four 🔧 values: `IdleTimeout`, `AbsoluteTimeout`, `WarningLead` (00:05:00, positive and shorter than the idle limit; served by the probe as `warningLeadSeconds`, decision 2a) and `SecurityStampValidationInterval` (00:00:00 = on every request, decision 4a), which becomes `SecurityStampValidatorOptions.ValidationInterval`. **Consequences to know:** the validator reads the user with roles and claims on every signed-in request and replaces the principal, so a changed role applies at once and a reset password or a deleted account (both change the stamp) ends the other sessions with their next request (SEC-08); Identity checks only when *more* than the interval passed since the ticket was issued, so a test on the fake clock advances it (one second) before the request that must be refused; an answer that renews the cookie leaves with `Cache-Control: no-cache,no-store` from the cookie handler, also a signed-in read of the catalog (decision 3a). **Client** (decision 1a): `SessionLimits` (`core/session/session-limits.ts`, no imports) holds the two limits; `authInterceptor` writes the headers of every answer, also a failed one, into it — it must not inject the root store, whose first request runs while the store is built; `withIdentity()` sets the limits from the probe, exposes them as the computed `sessionExpiresAt` / `sessionAbsoluteExpiresAt` (null while nobody is signed in) and keeps `sessionWarningLeadSeconds`. Nothing shows them yet: the warning dialog and J5 are S5-30. **Tests:** `CookieSessionTests` (`INV:SEC-07`, `INV:SEC-08`, its own host with the real cookie scheme and the fake clock), `SessionSettingsTests`, `auth.interceptor.spec.ts`, `with-identity.spec.ts`. No migration.

### 2.81 Session warning and the completed login overlay (S5-30)

Session warning M4.2 (S5-30, ARCHITECTURE_v80 C166, TESTING_v88 V297 – V301, SPEC v2.48 §8.8, UX copy v39 U160, Handoff 1.30; owner decisions S5-30 D1 (b), D2 (a), D3 (b), D4 (a), Q1 (a), Q2 (a) of 2026-11-17; A11Y-04 `active`). **Client:** `SessionWarningService` (`core/session/session-warning.service.ts`, started once by `App`) plans from `sessionExpiresAt`, `sessionAbsoluteExpiresAt` and `sessionWarningLeadSeconds` a timer at the warning time and one at the earlier limit, in browser time; an effect plans again whenever an answer moves the limits. At the warning time it calls `refreshSession()` of `withIdentity()` — the probe, which does not renew; unlike `loadSession()` a failed probe leaves the state and answers `unknown` — and opens `SessionWarningDialog` (`features/identity/dialogs/session-warning-dialog/`, loaded with its first use) for a session that is still there; moved limits close it. At the limit it asks again: an ended session closes the warning and calls `LoginOverlayService.requestSignIn(previousUserId)`, once per ended session (`endedUserId`; with a late jump of the page's clock both timers find it). A limit already passed when planned shows nothing, so no probe loop. The dialog is a `MatDialog` with `role: 'alertdialog'` and `ariaDescribedBy` of its text; the minutes are a signal of the service (rounded up, changed when the next whole minute is reached), the text uses `translatePlural` with the `.one` keys; „Angemeldet bleiben“ calls `AuthService.extendSession()` (`POST /auth/session/extend`), announces `shell.session.extended` and closes, a failure keeps it; „Jetzt abmelden“ and „Abmelden“ of `MainNav` use `SignOutService` (`features/identity/services/sign-out.service.ts`: `endSession()`, the start page, `focusPageHeading()`). **Login overlay:** `LoginOverlay.open(dialog, injector, data)` is async — it takes the previous user (from `data`, else the store), calls the probe through `AuthService.session()` without applying the answer (a new antiforgery token; with the token of the ended session the sign-in was refused) and opens the dialog with `{ previousUserId }`. On sign-in the component compares the session's user: the same (or none before) announces `shell.session.restored` and closes with `true` (retry); another one closes with `false` and calls `SignOutService.anotherUserSignedIn()` → `clearUserData()` of the root store (configurator, proposals, legacy saves; `forgetUser()` = `forgetSession()` + `clearUserData()`) and the start page. Closed without a sign-in, its `DestroyRef` calls `refreshSession()`, so the header shows the user signed out. **Server (host only):** `Extensions/TestClock.cs` and `TestClockExtensions.cs` — with `TestClock:Enabled` in Development or E2E (`docker-compose.e2e.yml` sets it) `TimeProvider` is a `TestClock` over the real clock that adds the offset of the request's cookie `pa-test-clock` (0 to 172 800 seconds; anything else counts as 0), stored by the first middleware in `HttpContext.Items` and read through `IHttpContextAccessor` — not an `AsyncLocal`, so the cookie handler's response-start callbacks read the same time. **E2E:** `e2e/support/clock.ts` (`SessionClock.install(page)` before the first `goto`, `move(minutes)` moves both clocks, `moveApi(minutes)` only the API's); `e2e/journeys/j5-session.spec.ts` (J5, own account per test, on S60); the warning in `shell/reflow.spec.ts`. **Tests:** `session-warning.service.spec.ts` (Vitest fake timers, `vi.dynamicImportSettled()` after each advance), `session-warning-dialog.spec.ts`, `login-overlay.spec.ts`, `with-identity.spec.ts`, `sign-out.service.spec.ts`, `Integration/Operations/TestClockGuardTests.cs`. **To know:** on the dev server (`npm start`) the dev-only watcher `src/dev/dev-reload-health.ts` fetches `/health/live` with the session cookie every 5 seconds through the proxy, which renews the session, so the warning never shows there — check it on the E2E stack (S5-30 open point). After the sign-in in the overlay the settings read announces „Wird geladen …“ right after `restored`. No migration, no new dependency. In E2E, act in a warning or the overlay only after its first focus (`toBeFocused()` on the field or button the dialog focuses): `MatDialog` moves the focus when the open animation ends, and a key pressed before it lands there (S6-01, #379; S13 the same in #380).

### 2.82 Rating legend above the criteria lists (S5-31)

Rating legend (S5-31, issue #279, R-LST-01, Handoff 1.31 §4.11 and §5.5, UX copy U24, Figma mapping v1.26; owner decisions #279 (b) of 2026-11-12, O-S5-31-1 and O-S5-31-2 of 2026-11-17; no invariant flipped, A11Y-01 stays green). **Component:** `RatingLegend` (`shared/components/rating-legend/`, selector `pa-rating-legend`, `@figma 2099-760`, so the Figma link check now checks it) — a text button `assessment.legend.show|hide` with `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`, followed in the DOM by a panel that stays there while `hidden`: a `<dl>` with `assessment.value.<level>` and `assessment.value.<level>.explain` (term column 10.5rem from 600 px, stacked below), then `assessment.legend.footnote`. Inputs: `expanded` (`model(false)`) and `values` (`readonly AssessmentValue[]`, all seven by default); the levels always show in the scale order of `ASSESSMENT_VALUES`, which the component exports with the type `AssessmentValue` (the seven values of `assessment.value.*`, unlike the configurator's `RatingName`, which leaves out `unknown`). The open state is not kept (collapsed on every visit; only S34b will keep it in `withSettings()`). **Pages:** `ratingsOfList(criteria)` (`features/configurator/state/criterion-detail-rules.ts`) joins the criterion-level `allowedRatings` (the server's union over the shown procedures, none for „Geprüft nach …“, all six for a requirement without a procedure text of the selection) in scale order; S11 (between the filter row and the cards), a saved list (after the filter row, for every role) and S16 (after the counts) show one collapsed legend with these levels — from the whole list, so the filters never change it, and left out when the list allows none. „Wie wird bewertet?“ in the card keeps its list of the procedure's levels and has no legend. **Texts:** the area `assessment.legend` joined `AREAS` of `check-i18n.mjs`. **Tests:** `rating-legend.spec.ts` (closed, open with all seven, close again, `values` in scale order, axe), `ratingsOfList` cases in `criterion-detail-rules.spec.ts`, the legend on S11 (place, levels, kept with an empty filter, left out for „Geprüft nach …“ only), the saved list (Reader and Editor) and S16 in their page specs; E2E `configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` opens it with axe in both appearances, and `shell/reflow.spec.ts` opens it on S11, a saved list and S16.

### 2.83 Design fidelity of S10 and S11 (S5-32)

Design fidelity (S5-32, issues #278, #281, #282, R-LST-01, R-LST-10, R-SHL-10, Handoff 1.32 §2.2 and §5.1, Figma mapping v1.27; owner decisions S5-32 1 (a), 2 (a), 3 (a) of 2026-11-17; no invariant flipped, A11Y-01 and A11Y-09 stay green; styles only, no behaviour change). **S11 title row:** `criteria-list.html` wraps the H1 and „Auswahl ändern“ in `.pa-criteria-list__title-row` — stacked below 600 px, from 600 px one row with the H1 at the start and the button at the end (Figma `2020:102`); DOM order H1 → button, so `focusPageHeading` is unchanged. A saved list keeps its title and its actions stacked (decision 2 (a); its own follow-up). **Action bar:** while sticky (`STICKY_ACTION_BAR_QUERY`) `pa-criteria-action-bar` has no corners and paints its background to both viewport edges with `box-shadow: 0 0 0 100vmax` clipped to its height by `clip-path: inset(0 -100vmax)` — paint overflow only, so nothing scrolls sideways, and its box stays in the content column, so status and buttons stay aligned with the list (decision 3 (a)) and the `scroll-padding-bottom` and focus rule of S4-18a measure the same box; in the flow it keeps `corner-medium`. It applies to S11 and a saved list, which share the bar (decision 1 (a)). The shell must keep `overflow: visible` on `.pa-shell` and `.pa-shell__content`, or the background is cut at the column. **Stepper:** `pa-stepper` circles 2rem (32 px, Figma `2019:457`). **Summary:** `pa-criteria-list-summary` terms in `label-medium` (12 px; Figma `2101:3160`), also on a saved list and S16. **Tests:** `e2e/configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` checks the title row on 1280 and 360 px and that the sticky bar has square corners, reaches both viewport edges (pixels of a one-line screenshot, decoded in the page by `pixelsAt`, no image library) and adds no horizontal scrolling.

### 2.84 Quick wins of sprint 06 (S6-02)

Quick wins (S6-02, issues #375, #378, #350, #385, TD-64, TD-71; Handoff 1.33 §2.2; no invariant flipped, A11Y-01, A11Y-09 and TEN-09 stay green). **S16:** `shared-list-page.html` gates the cards and the rating legend by `shown.criteria.criteria.length`, the counts paragraph by `counts().total`, and shows `criteria.list.empty` only for a list without any criterion (a list of „Geprüft nach …“ criteria only has cards and no counts). **Action bar:** `criteria-action-bar.scss` adds `border-block-start: 1px solid CanvasText` under `@media (forced-colors: active)` inside the sticky query; the measured height includes it; E2E check in `e2e/configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` (Chromium). **Legacy product list:** the English `aria-label` is gone, the name is the translated `PRODUCTS.PROPOSE`; the profile page it also named was removed in S5-28b. **Dev health watcher:** `src/dev/dev-reload-health.ts` polls with `credentials: 'omit'`, so `npm start` no longer renews the session and the warning shows there too. **Dead code removed (TD-71):** the legacy helper classes `.secondary`, `.tertiary`, `.error`, `.text-error` in `theme.scss`, `core/models/appCustomization.model.ts`, the 14 placeholder `todo.txt` files (with the empty `features/feature-1/` folder and their `REUSE.toml` entries), the commented-out `localStorage` lines in `criterion-proposal.ts` and `product-proposal.ts`.

### 2.85 CI smoke: dev server and cache header of lazy chunks (S6-07)

CI smoke (S6-07, issue #388, TD-73; regressions of #264 and #308; no requirement, no invariant, no TESTING change). **Dev server (CI stage 1, job `lint`):** the step „Dev server smoke (ng serve renders)“ starts `npx ng serve --port 4200` in the client folder (no API: the dev server's proxy answers 5xx for `/api` and `/health`, which the script ignores) and runs `scripts/smoke-dev-server.mjs`. The script loads Playwright from `e2e/node_modules` (no dependency of its own; Google Chrome of the runner, `CHROME_PATH` for another one), opens `/` and `/filters` and fails on a 5xx of the dev server outside the proxied paths, an uncaught page error, or a page without a visible `<h1>` after 60 s. `/filters` is the lazy route whose chunk imports `ngx-markdown` through the pre-bundling (#264); when the legacy filter is removed (activation of 2026.1), name another lazy route that imports a package as the second path. **Cache header (CI stage 6, job `e2e`, project `chromium` only):** the step „Cache headers of the client image“ runs `scripts/check-chunk-cache.mjs http://localhost:4200` against the client container of the E2E stack: it reads `index.html`, takes `main-*.js` and `styles-*.css` from it and one lazy chunk (a dynamic `import("./chunk-<hash>.js")`) from the main bundle and requires `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` on all three. Self-tests: `smoke-dev-server.test.mjs` (the 5xx rule) and `check-chunk-cache.test.mjs` (parsing and the whole check against a local server), run in stage 1 in the dev-server step. Both scripts are plain Node (`node:http`, no `fetch` global, because the ESLint globals of `scripts/` are `console` and `process` only).

### 2.86 Root store and the M5 entries of `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` (S6-24)

Root store (S6-24, issue #158 M5 entries, TECH_DEBT_v5 §5; S5-02 P3, S3-02 R1, S3-09 R1; no requirement, no invariant flipped, no TESTING change). **One member each:** `ProcureAccessStore` (`core/state/app.store.ts`) adds `withLoading()` first and then the slices; no slice adds `withLoading()` or another slice again. A slice declares what it reads from the features before it as the typed input of `signalStoreFeature`: `LOADING_INPUT` (`shared/state/with-loading.ts`, the methods of the type `LoadingCounter` — a type literal, because NgRx's `MethodsDictionary` needs its implicit index signature) for `withIdentity`, `withConfigurator`, `withFilters`, `withCriteria`, `withProducts`, `withProposal`; `{ state: type<{ user: User | null }>() }` for `withFavorites` (it nested a second `withIdentity()` before); `withSettings` already declared `session` (S5-25b). A store that leaves out a feature a slice needs does not compile, so every test store of a slice adds `withLoading()` first (`signalStore(withLoading(), withConfigurator())`). The root `withState<AppState>` and `core/models/appState.interface.ts` are gone: its keys were unread (`loadingCount`, `identity`, `filters`) or declared again by the slices, which now hold their own initial values. NgRx no longer warns „SignalStore members cannot be overridden“ in dev mode. **Rejections (S3-02 R1):** the error interceptor already reports an unmarked failure (offline notice, S80 page or snackbar), so the callers of the legacy store methods only catch the rejection: `onInit` catches `init()` (the log line „store initialized“ went with the old `finally`), `startSession` catches `loadProposals()`, `products-container` catches `getCriteriaBySelectedCriteriaFilterIds` and stays on the page when `getProductsBySelectedCriteriaFilterIds` fails, `filters-selection` catches `loadFilters()` in a synchronous `ngOnInit`; the proposal screens go with S6-23. **Lint:** the findings of `favorites.ts`, `product-details.ts` (unused `SnackbarService` removed), `products-list.ts` (`void` for the two legacy `LiveAnnouncer` calls, M6), `products-container.ts`, `products-api.service.ts`, `with-products.spec.ts` (rejects with an `Error`), and of the M2 files `filters-selection.ts` (the misused `ViewChildren(MatListOption)` field removed) and `with-filters.ts` (`ofType` compares `filterTypeId === Number(type)`) are fixed and their eight entries deleted: `LEGACY_EXCEPTIONS` falls from 15 to 7 files (M2 3, M5 4 — the proposal files, which S6-23 deletes). The legacy `<strong>` in `mat-error` is only left in `product-proposal.html` and goes with S6-23. **Tests:** `core/state/app.store.spec.ts` builds the real root store (only the backend replaced): no override warning (`console.warn` spy), one loading counter across slices, and a start whose product read answers 500 without an unhandled rejection (Vitest fails a run on one).

### 2.87 Users in the IdentityAccess module, part a (S6-10a)

Users in IdentityAccess, part a (S6-10a, #387, TD-72, TD-75; ARCHITECTURE C167, ADR-014 rules 4 and 5; TESTING V302 – V305; owner decisions O-S6-1 (a) and S6-10 1 (b), 2 (a), 4 of 2026-11-18; no invariant changes its status, no route or answer changes, no client change). **Split:** the entity and every user of `UserManager<User>` had to move together (a module must not reference MODELS, and `User` is `internal`), so part a is the whole move and part b (S6-10b) adds the foreign keys and the clean-up. **Tables:** `IdentityAccessDbContext` derives from `IdentityDbContext<User>` and maps `identity."Users"`, `Roles`, `RoleClaims`, `UserClaims`, `UserLogins`, `UserRoles`, `UserTokens` through `IdentityTablesConfiguration` in exactly the legacy layout (names without `AspNet`, 200 characters for the text columns Identity leaves open); `User` (`Domain/`) carries `IsDeleted`, `DeletedAt`, `CreatedAt` and the soft-delete filter. **Migrations:** legacy `HandOverIdentityTables` removes the entities from the legacy model, drops no table and drops the two foreign keys `Proposals` → `Users` (`ProposerId`/`ApproverId` stay as ids with their indexes, no navigation); IdentityAccess `AdoptIdentityTables` creates the tables and indexes `IF NOT EXISTS` and inserts the platform roles with the reviewers' permission where they are missing. On every database the legacy context migrates first, so the module migration only records itself and no row changes; existing accounts sign in unchanged (`IdentityTablesHandOverTests`). **Never** let a scaffolded legacy migration drop an Identity table, and keep `IdentityTablesConfiguration` in step with the tables: a changed length is a real column change. **Registration:** `AddIdentityAccessModule` → `IdentityRegistration.AddIdentityAccessIdentity` (user and role stores, `ClockedUserManager`, sign-in manager, token providers, `ConfigureIdentityOptions` with 🔧 `Account:Password:MinLength` and `Account:Lockout:*`, the policy `ConfirmedEmail` with its handlers) and the password-reset queue and mailer; the host keeps `AddIdentityAuth` (default scheme and policy), `AddAuthorizationPolicies` (`ModerationReview`) and the middleware order. **Endpoints:** everything below `/api/v1/auth`, `/account`, `/organizations` and `/invitations` is mapped by the one call `MapIdentityAccessEndpoints`; `GET /api/me` by `MapIdentityAccessLegacyEndpoints` on the unversioned `/api` (the client's `auth.service.ts` still reads it). The endpoint classes, their bodies and answers are `internal`; the tests reach them through `InternalsVisibleTo`. **Across modules:** IdentityAccess depends on no module, so the export and the deletion call contracts of their own that the owning modules implement: `ICriteriaListDataExport` (levels as the names `A`, `B`, `Info`) and `IOrganizationDataDeletion` (the deletion endpoint calls every registered implementation, then `IShareLinks`); the Configurator registers both; a module with organisation data adds its implementation and nothing in IdentityAccess changes. **For other modules:** name a user by id only and read `IUserDirectory.GetDisplayNamesAsync` for what to show (the e-mail address; nothing for a deleted or unknown account; the caller decides who may see it). **Commands and seeds:** `IdentityAccessModule.IsRolesCommand` / `RunRolesCommandAsync` (`RoleCommandHost` builds Platform and IdentityAccess only; the command refuses while the module has pending migrations); `RebuildSampleUsersAsync` (sample user `IdentityAccessModule.SampleUserEmail`) and `SeedTestUsersAsync` behind the host's unchanged guards. Role names: `PlatformRoles` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts`); `Permissions` and `CustomClaimTypes` stay in MODELS until the legacy proposals retire. **Removed:** the host folders `Account/`, `Auth/`, `Organizations/`, `Commands/`, `Controllers/Identity/`; `UserService`, `IUserService`, `UserDto`, `User`, `UserConfiguration`, `Roles`, `SampleData`, `SampleDataInitializer` of the legacy projects; the Identity package reference of DAL and MODELS. **Host analyzer:** `ApiVersionConfiguration` suppresses AV0016 for `AssumeDefaultVersionWhenUnspecified` with its reason (the legacy controllers and `GET /api/me` still need the default version). **Tests:** `Infrastructure/TestAccounts.cs` is the one account helper (TD-75); a test that needs users on a database of its own creates `IdentityAccessDbContext` with `FixedOrganizationContext.None`. **Not yet (S6-10b):** the foreign keys onto `Users`, the soft-deleted rows' role, claim and lockout columns, the order of the Development rebuild.

### 2.88 Reload flows: lost lazy chunk and release change in S13 (S6-27)

Reload flows (S6-27, issues #296, #310, TD-39; owner decision O-S6-5; UX copy v40 U161; Handoff 1.34 §5.1 S13 row; ARCHITECTURE_v82 C168; no requirement, no invariant flipped, A11Y-01 stays green). **Lost chunk (#296):** `provideRouter(routes, withNavigationErrorHandler(handleChunkLoadError))` in `app.config.ts`; `core/errors/chunk-reload.ts` holds the handler and its parts. `isChunkLoadError` recognises the `TypeError` of a failed dynamic `import()` by the engines' messages, kept in one list (`CHUNK_LOAD_MESSAGES`: Chromium „Failed to fetch dynamically imported module“, Firefox „error loading dynamically imported module“, WebKit „Importing a module script failed“); every other navigation error keeps the router's default. `chunkFailureAction(target, reloadedFor, offline)` is the guard: offline → `stay` (no reload, the offline notice of `ConnectionStatus` stays), a history entry already reloaded for the same router URL → `errorPage` (S80 `server` in place through a `RedirectCommand` with `skipLocationChange`, URL kept), else `reload`. `DocumentReload` keeps the guard in the session history, never in browser storage (R-CON-09, R-SET-02): it writes `{ paChunkReload: <router URL> }` through Angular's `Location` into the target's history entry — `go` with the target URL, or `replaceState` keeping the state when the address already shows the target — and calls `location.reload()` (a protected `reloadDocument()`, replaced in the tests). Both run in the next task (`setTimeout`): after the handler returns, the router restores the previous address and state in the same call stack (`NavigationError` → `restoreHistory`), and the browser reloads only later, so an immediate write would be overwritten and the previous page reloaded (review finding of S6-27); the browser keeps an entry's state across the reload, so the next failure of the same target in that entry shows S80. The router copies the key into the navigation's `extras.state` on success; it is never read elsewhere. The in-memory S10/S11 draft is lost on the reload (owner decision O-S6-5, no warning). nginx answers a missing hashed chunk with 404 and `index.html` with `no-cache` (S6-07), so the reload gets the new files. Unit tests `core/errors/chunk-reload.spec.ts` (messages, guard, the handler in a router with a failing `loadComponent` on `provideLocationMocks()`, the history the router writes to, so the order of restore and mark is tested); E2E `e2e/shell/chunk-reload.spec.ts` (every chunk the start page did not load answers 404: once → one reload and S10; always → one reload and S80, axe in both appearances). **S13 after `release-mismatch` (#310, TD-39):** for the draft of S11 the outdated state shows „Schließen“ (text button) and „Kriterien neu laden“ (`criteria.export.outdated.reload`, filled, takes the focus), which closes the dialog with `{ reload: true }` (`ExportDialogResult` is now `{ toNote } | { reload: true }`; `mat-dialog-close` without a value closes with `''`, so the pages test `typeof result === 'object'`). A saved list exports under its own release and keeps „Schließen“ only. S11 calls `withConfigurator().reloadCriteriaListForActiveRelease()`: running requests dropped, search and filter cleared, pinned release, options and every per-criterion decision dropped, the draft of the four layers kept (as `criteria.export.outdated` says, not #310's proposal); the options load without a release and pin the active one (ADR-002), then the list; `loadCriteriaList` now pins the answer's release when none is pinned (options failed). Afterwards S11 moves the focus to the H1 (`focusPageHeading`, the button that opened S13 was gone while the list loaded), or opens the legacy filter for `noRelease`. A selection the new release excludes gets S11's failure state (generic snackbar, „Auswahl ändern“); `CriteriaListShown` is not counted again for the same selection. Tests: `with-configurator.spec.ts` (four cases), `export-dialog.spec.ts` (both buttons, the reload on S11); E2E in `e2e/configurator/s11-list.spec.ts` (400 `release-mismatch` through `page.route`, the options request without `release`, focus on the H1, axe on the outdated state).

### 2.89 Legal pages from Markdown with the placeholder check (S6-28)

Legal pages S71 (S6-28, GP.3, R-SHL-07; ARCHITECTURE_v83 C169, TESTING_v90 V306 – V308, Handoff 1.35; owner decision O-S6-6 (a); no invariant flipped, A11Y-01 and A11Y-09 stay green). **Texts:** `CLIENT/CLIENT.ProcureAccess/content/legal/impressum.de.md`, `datenschutz.de.md`, `barrierefreiheit.de.md` (in the client, because the client image is built from its folder; licence in `REUSE.toml`, so the files hold only the text). Until WERTE.IT delivers the texts each file is the single line `<!-- PLATZHALTER -->`. **To insert a text:** replace the marker line with the text; headings start at `##` (`##` H2, `###` H3, `####` H4 — the H1 is the UX copy title `legal.*.title`, a `# ` line is shown as written and a unit test refuses it in the repository's files); paragraphs, `- ` lists (two spaces per level), `**bold**` and `[text](https://…)` links as in the catalog texts (§2.35); `<!-- … -->` comments are never shown (a comment without its end hides the rest of the file); no `mailto:` links, no tables, no images (P1 of the protocol). **Build:** `angular.json` maps `.md` to esbuild's `text` loader (`build.options.loader`, inherited by `ng test`), `src/markdown.d.ts` types the import; `pages/legal/legal-texts.ts` imports the three files into `LEGAL_TEXTS` (a test provides its own). The three routes use `loadComponent` (`legalPage` in `app.routes.ts`), so the texts sit in the pages' lazy chunk, not in the first download (756.8 kB initial after the change, on main with S6-24 and S6-27). **Rendering:** `pages/legal/legal-markdown.ts` (`parseLegalText`) drops comments, splits at heading lines and hands the text between them to `pa-catalog-text`; `LegalPage` renders H1, the notice `legal.placeholder` while the file is a placeholder (marker in any spacing and case, or no content outside comments) — in every environment, above any content — and the blocks with H2–H4 in headline/small, title/medium, title/small. No Markdown library: `ngx-markdown`/`marked` stay only for the legacy filter (owner's S6-28 instructions). **Release check:** `scripts/check-release-config.mjs` reads the three files (`LEGAL_PAGES`, `checkLegalPage`, `checkRelease(root)`) and fails on a missing file, the marker or a file without content; client and script hold the same marker pattern (`LEGAL_PLACEHOLDER`) and the same comment rule (`withoutComments`, a scan, not a regex replacement, which CodeQL flags as incomplete sanitization; a comment without its end hides the rest of the file) — change both together. The self-test (CI stage 1) covers each rule, the whole release on a temporary root and the script itself on the repository (exit 1 with an `::error` line per placeholder page). The check runs before a production release, not in the image build (C110; TD-29 still has no release pipeline that runs it). Unit tests: `legal-markdown.spec.ts`, `legal-page.spec.ts`; E2E unchanged (`shell.spec.ts` axe in both appearances, `reflow.spec.ts`, `route-change.spec.ts` start on the legal pages).

### 2.90 Foreign keys onto the users and the clean-up, part b (S6-10b)

Users in IdentityAccess, part b (S6-10b, closes #387 and TD-72; ARCHITECTURE C170; TESTING V309 – V312; owner decisions S6-10 3 and 4 of 2026-11-18; no invariant changes its status, no route or answer changes). **Keys:** the migration `AddUserForeignKeys` makes `AccountToken.UserId`, `UserContributorLink.UserId`, `OrganizationMembership.UserId` and `UserPreferences.UserId` foreign keys onto `identity."Users"` with cascade and `Organization.CreatedByUserId` one with restrict (plus its index); the five columns are `character varying(200)` like `Users.Id`. The relations are configured without navigations (`HasOne<User>().WithMany()` in the five configurations). **Before the keys** the migration deletes rows without their user (only Development databases have them: the rebuild deleted the users) and every organisation whose creator row is gone, and it clears the role, claim, login and token rows and the lockout of accounts already soft-deleted. **Account deletion:** `AccountDeletionEndpoints` removes the same rows and resets `LockoutEnd` and `AccessFailedCount` in the save of the soft delete — it marks them on the `IdentityAccessDbContext` that `UserManager` saves, so there is one save. **Development rebuild:** `SeedUsers.RebuildAsync` deletes every organisation first (`IgnoreQueryFilters().ExecuteDeleteAsync()`, allow-listed for TEN-02), then the users; their tokens, memberships and settings cascade. **Consequences for new code:** a row that names a user can only be written for an existing account (a soft-deleted row counts); never hard-delete a user row outside the rebuild, and never give `Organization.CreatedByUserId` a cascade. A new table of this module with a user id gets its foreign key in the same migration that creates it. **Tests:** store the accounts before the rows that name them — `TestAccounts.Of(user, confirmed)` for the fixture users before `Tenants.Entities`, `TestAccounts.WithId(id)` where only the referenced row matters; `Persistence/UserForeignKeysMigrationTests.cs` covers the migration on a database with orphans. **Left:** criteria lists and share links of an organisation the migration or the rebuild deletes stay behind in their contexts (Development only).

Dependabot batch (S6-08, TD-76, TD-17, TD-51; no requirement, no invariant, no TESTING change). The four pull requests of 2026-11-16 were merged one at a time by the owner, each after `@dependabot rebase` on the previous merge: #366 (coverlet.MTP 10.1.1, Testcontainers.PostgreSql 4.17.0, test projects only — `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt` unchanged, 38 packages), #364 (Angular 22.2.6/22.2.7 patches), #365 (material-symbols 0.49.0, eslint 10.15, jsdom 31.0.1, prettier 3.10.1, with the subset regenerated on its branch: `material-symbols-outlined.woff2` 26 icons, the manifest pins 0.49.0), #363 (@playwright/test 1.65.1 and eslint 10.15 in `e2e/`, with `e2e/playwright-image/Dockerfile` on `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.65.1-noble@sha256:1507cbfc…` on its branch). **Playwright digest without buildx:** the index digest is the `Docker-Content-Digest` header of `curl -sI -H "Accept: application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json" https://mcr.microsoft.com/v2/playwright/manifests/v<version>-noble`; the image is re-pushed under the same tag (the digest of v1.65.0 changed after it was pinned), so the digest is the pin. `docker-compose.e2e.yml` names no Playwright image; CI reads the Dockerfile. **`npm audit fix`** (two commits, client and `e2e/`): dev dependencies only — minimatch 11.0.9 (GHSA-g3hv-75wg-m249, Dependabot alerts 12 and 13), typescript-eslint 8.73.0, angular-eslint 22.7.0 (client), `ignore`; `npm audit --omit=dev` stays at 0 in both. Left: the high picomatch ReDoS (GHSA-fc5q-39xc-vjpf, every version) under vite, vitest, `@angular/build`, `@parcel/watcher` and typescript-eslint's tinyglobby — no patched release yet, dev only.

### 2.92 Return to the guarded page after the sign-in (S6-03)

Return URL after the sign-in (S6-03, issue #386, TD-65; ARCHITECTURE_v85 C171; R-AUTH-03, SEC-03 stays green; no invariant flipped, no owner decision). **Guard:** `authGuard` (`core/guards/auth.guard.ts`) sends a signed-out user to `/(login:anmelden)` over the start page as before, now as a `RedirectCommand` with the navigation state `{ returnUrl: state.url }` (`LoginNavigationState.returnUrl`, `features/identity/models/login-navigation.ts`) — memory and the history entry only, never the URL or browser storage (R-SET-02). S01 cannot open over the guarded page: the guard would run again and the page would load signed out. **S01:** `LoginPage` reads the state of the navigation that created it, `currentNavigation() ?? lastSuccessfulNavigation()` — the fallback covers a deep link whose redirect activates the `login` route before the shell's outlet exists (unit test with the real page, which fails without it). After the sign-in a valid target is opened with `navigateByUrl` (closes the outlet; page title and H1 focus from the route change); without one S01 closes the outlet as before, so „Liste speichern“ (reason `save`, kept draft, `auth.login.draftKept`, R-SAV-01) is unchanged. **Open redirect:** `safeReturnUrl(value, origin)` (`features/identity/models/return-url.ts`, without imports) passes only a string that starts with one `/`, has no `//`, no backslash, no control character or whitespace and no `(login:` outlet, and resolves against `document.location.origin` to that origin; a refused target is ignored (S01 just closes). A real router URL never has `//` (the router encodes `/` in query values as `%2F`); only a fragment with `//` would be refused. **Tests:** `return-url.spec.ts` (each refused form: absolute URLs, `//host`, `///host`, `/\host`, `\\host`, backslash in the path, `javascript:` in any case and behind spaces, `data:`, tab, newline and space between the slashes, `//` later in the path, the `login` outlet, a path without `/`, empty, non-strings), `auth.guard.spec.ts` (through, the `RedirectCommand` and its state, the state at the outlet with the shell open and on a deep link), `login-page.spec.ts` (+6: return to `/konto?…`, four refused targets close only the outlet, the deep-link case); E2E `e2e/identity/login.spec.ts` „a signed-out deep link to a guarded page returns to it after the sign-in“ (`/konto`, H1 focus and title). **Not covered:** S01's link to S02 and the registration drop the target (S6-03 open point).

### 2.93 Procurement projects on the server (S6-11)

Projects M5.1, server part (S6-11, ARCHITECTURE_v86 C172, TESTING_v92 V313 – V316, SPEC v2.49 §8.6; R-PRJ-01, R-PRJ-05 server; owner decisions S6-11 1 (a), 2 (a), 3 (a), 4 (a) of 2026-11-18; no invariant flipped — TEN-01, TEN-04, TEN-05, SEC-10, API-01 and OPS-03 gain cases). **Module:** Workspace is no longer empty. `WorkspaceDbContext` (`Persistence/`, `internal`, schema `workspace`, history `workspace."__EFMigrationsHistory"`, migration `InitialWorkspaceSchema`, `WorkspaceDatabaseMigrator`, design-time factory) filters by `IOrganizationContext` like the Configurator's context; `AddWorkspaceModule(configuration)` registers it and `ProjectService`, `MapWorkspaceEndpoints()` is mapped in `Program.cs` on the versioned group. The project references EF Core and Npgsql in the versions of the other modules. **Entity:** `ProcurementProject` (`Domain/`; `Create`, `Rename`, `ChangeStatus`, `IsArchived`; name ≤ 120 by `NameMaxLength`; `ProjectStatus` Open / Done / Archived stored by name; `CreatedByUserId` and `OrganizationId` without foreign keys, ADR-014; `ModifiedAt` moves only when the name or the status really changes). `Rename` throws on an archived project. **Endpoints** (`Projects/ProjectEndpoints`, minimal APIs, tag „Projects“): `GET` and `POST /api/v1/organizations/{organizationId}/projects`, `GET` and `PATCH /api/v1/projects/{id}`; no DELETE (SPEC §3, a test checks that no project route accepts it). The list answers every project of the named organisation, the archived ones too, the most recently changed first — sorting by name and the filter „Archivierte Projekte anzeigen“ are the client's (decision 3 (a), as S14). Answers: `ProjectSummaryResponse` (`id`, `name`, `status`, `productCount`, `modifiedAt`) and `ProjectResponse` (plus `organizationId`, `createdAt`); the creator is stored, not served (decision 4 (a)). `status` is `open` / `done` / `archived` (`ProjectService.StatusOf`/`NameOf`), the suffixes of `projects.status.*`; `productCount` is the constant `NoProductsYet` until S6-15 counts candidates. **Rules:** the role check comes first — Reader for list and read, Editor for create and change, on the organisation of the route or of the stored project read through the filter (404 non-member, 403 below the role; never a header) —, then validation (400 keyed `name`, `status`; the name trimmed, a body with unknown members refused); create and change need `ConfirmedEmail` and antiforgery. On an archived project a PATCH that names a name answers 409 `ProblemTypes.ProjectArchived` (`project-archived`, new; SPEC §8.6), also together with a status; a PATCH with only a status changes it to either other value (decisions 1 (a), 2 (a)). **Seam for S6-20:** every change of a stored project runs through `ProjectService.ChangeAsync` (load, change, one save); the concurrency token and `If-Match` (API-03) join there, nothing is mapped for it yet. **Tests:** `Unit/Workspace/ProcurementProjectTests`, `Integration/Workspace/ProjectEndpointsTests`, `Integration/Persistence/WorkspaceMigrationTests`, the `ProcurementProject` sample and context in `OrganizationIsolationTests`, 30 rows in `AuthorizationMatrixTests`; `ApiFixture` migrates the context, keeps its history out of Respawn and stores its entities. **Left for later tasks:** the client mapping `project-archived` → `projects.archived.note` in `PROBLEM_TYPE_KEYS` when the area `projects` joins `AREAS` of `check-i18n.mjs` (S6-13/S6-14; until then the client shows the general error text), and its row in UX copy §6.3; `CriteriaConfigurationId` and the copy (S6-12); candidates and `productCount` (S6-15); whether a change below a project moves its `ModifiedAt` (S6-15, S6-18); `IOrganizationDataDeletion` and the personal data export for projects (done in S6-25, §2.102).
