### 2.95 Mail and token hygiene: SMTP timeout, token clean-up, nginx error log (S6-05)

Mail and token hygiene (S6-05, issues #382, #383, #381, TD-58, TD-59, TD-57; ARCHITECTURE C174 – C176, SPEC v2.50; owner decision O-S6-3, task decisions D1 (a), no index, `crit`; SEC-10, SEC-11, PRV-11, TEN-08 stay green, no invariant flipped). **SMTP timeout (TD-58):** `EmailSettings.Timeout` from the 🔧 `Email:Smtp:Timeout` (`appsettings.json` `00:00:15`), required like the other settings — missing, zero, negative or above `int.MaxValue` ms stops the start (note: a bare `15` parses as 15 days). `MailKitSmtpTransport` sets it as `SmtpClient.Timeout`, which bounds every network operation (connect, greeting, sign-in, each send command); a stalled server ends the attempt with an exception the sender's retry treats like any failure, so a request waits at most about 2 × 15 s. Every test that builds `EmailSettings` from an in-memory configuration must now set `Email:Smtp:Timeout`. `SmtpTimeoutTests` (integration project, no container) run the real transport against a loopback `TcpListener` that stalls before the greeting, at `AUTH` or at `MAIL FROM`, with a 1 s timeout; without the timeout the three cases ran past 300 s. **Token clean-up (TD-59):** `AccountTokenCleanupService` (`IdentityAccess/Accounts/`), a `BackgroundService` built like `InvitationCleanupService`: at startup and every `Account:Tokens:CleanupInterval` (1 h, operations setting) on a `PeriodicTimer` over `TimeProvider`, `DeleteExpiredAsync` deletes every row with `ExpiresAt <= now`, used or not (D1 (a)). A used row stays until its expiry, so the second click on a confirmation link still answers 204; after that it answers 400 like an unknown link. No index on `ExpiresAt`, no migration (the table holds one lifetime of tokens); no query filter on the table, so no allow-list entry. Tests that advance their host's clock past a token's lifetime now race the clean-up, which is harmless: an expired token answers the same with or without its row. `AccountTokenCleanupTests`: the rule around the expiry, and the hosted service on the shared `FakeTimeProvider` (advance one interval, poll up to 30 s, as `LogRetentionTests`). **nginx error log (TD-57):** `CLIENT/CLIENT.ProcureAccess/nginx.conf` sets `error_log /var/log/nginx/error.log crit;` in the `server` block: nginx's error log has no format, and an upstream failure (API down) wrote the request line and the referrer with the share token; a rule for the share paths alone would have missed the referrer of the page's other API calls. The 502 stays visible in the masked access log. Checked by hand on `nginx:1.30-alpine` (protocol S6-05 §4); the Traefik access log stays TD-28 (9).

### 2.96 Rate limits of sign-in and organisation creation (S6-26)

Rate limits of sign-in and organisation creation (S6-26, issue #318, TD-60; ARCHITECTURE C177, SPEC v2.51 §7.3, §8.8, §8.9, TESTING V323 – V325; owner decision O-S6-2, task decisions Q1 (a) count-based tests, Q2 (a) `errors.rateLimit` as it is; API-05 gains three policies, SEC-11 stays green, no invariant flipped, no migration). **Policies** (`RateLimitPolicies`, each 🔧 under `RateLimits:<policy>` in `appsettings.json`, registered in `RateLimitingRegistration`'s groups): `SignIn` per client IP (30 per 10 minutes, `ClientIpPolicies`) on `POST /api/v1/auth/login`; `SignInAddress` per e-mail address (10 per hour, `KeyedPolicies`), asked by `AuthEndpoints.LoginAsync` through `IKeyedRateLimiter` with `ILookupNormalizer.NormalizeEmail(email.Trim())` after the empty-field check and **before** `FindByEmailAsync` — every attempt with both fields counts, also a successful one, an address without an account gets the same 429, and an attempt refused with 429 never reaches `CheckPasswordSignInAsync`, so it does not count towards the lockout; `OrganizationCreation` per signed-in user (5 per day, window `1.00:00:00`, `UserPolicies`) on `POST /api/v1/organizations`, behind `ConfirmedEmail` (authorization runs before the limiter, so a 403 does not count). The integration host (`ProcureAccessApiFactory`) and `docker-compose.e2e.yml` relax all three; `RateLimits:Invitation` is unchanged. **Client:** `LOGIN_PROBLEMS` and `CREATE_PROBLEMS` list `rate-limit`; `pa-login-form` (S01 and the login overlay) and S20 show `errors.rateLimit` with the server's `retryAfterMinutes` in their error summary (the sign-in without the lockout note; S20 says „1440 Minuten“ for the day window, Q2 (a)). **Tests:** `RateLimitTests` (login in the per-IP theories, organisation creation per user with two stored confirmed accounts, the defaults), `IdentityAccess/SignInRateLimitTests` (own host with real authentication, per-address limit 2; the lockout tests in `PasswordResetTests` keep the relaxed address limit); like every API-05 test they check the count and the window's length, not its passing, because the limiters keep their own clock.

### 2.98 Candidate products on the server (S6-15)

Candidates M5.2, server part (S6-15, ARCHITECTURE_v91 C179, TESTING_v96 V329 – V334, SPEC v2.53; R-CAN-02, R-CAN-05 server; owner decisions S6-15 1 (a) – 5 (a) of 2026-11-18; **WSP-01** and **SIG-03** (workspace part) `active`; TEN-01, TEN-04, TEN-05, SEC-10, API-01 and OPS-03 gain cases). **Entities** (`Workspace/Domain/`, migration `CandidateProducts`): `CandidateProduct` is the aggregate — facts (`CandidateFacts`: only the name required, `NameMaxLength` 200, website `https` ≤ 2 048, summary ≤ 2 000), `TestStatus` (Unknown / Tested / NotTested by name, `NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Unknown'`; the EF sentinel `(TestStatus)(-1)` makes EF always send the value), `SoftwareProductId` (always `null` until M6, decision 1 (a)) and its `TestEvidences` (`TestEvidence`, `TestEvidenceFacts`: `TestKind` Procedure / Other / Own, `TestTypeKey` of a catalog procedure, `OtherTestTypeName` ≤ 120, tester ≤ 200 and date required, version ≤ 200, report link). `AddEvidence`, `ChangeEvidence`, `RemoveEvidence`, `ChangeTestStatus(status, removeEvidences)` keep WSP-01 and answer outcomes instead of throwing for the rule cases (`EvidenceChangeOutcome`, `TestStatusChangeOutcome`); `ChangeEvidence` keeps the row for a new product version (C65). A partial unique index (candidate, `TestTypeKey`) holds „one per procedure“ in the database. **Private rows:** `CandidatePrivateSection` (contacts, internal notes; key = candidate) and `TestEvidencePrivateNote` (qualification note ≤ 2 000; key = evidence; decision 4 (a)), both deleted with their row by the database; the candidate and the evidence have no member that leads to them, so the publication of step 6 cannot copy a private field (unit test `CopiedTypes_Members_NoPrivateFieldAndNoWayToThePrivateRows`). Every Workspace entity carries `OrganizationId` and the organisation filter in `WorkspaceDbContext`. `ProcurementProject.MarkChanged` and `CandidateProduct.MarkChanged` record a change below them (decision 5 (a)). **Endpoints** (`Candidates/CandidateEndpoints`, tag „Candidates“; bodies and answers in `CandidateContracts`, checks in `InputCheck`, wire names in `CandidateNames`; `CandidateService` loads the candidate with its evidences and its project, changes and saves once — the seam for the S6-20 ETags): `POST /api/v1/projects/{id}/candidates`, `GET`/`PATCH /api/v1/candidates/{id}` (a change names what changes, `""` clears an optional field), `PUT /api/v1/candidates/{id}/test-status { testStatus, removeEvidences? }`, `POST /api/v1/candidates/{id}/test-evidences`, `PUT`/`DELETE /api/v1/test-evidences/{id}`. Role check first through `WorkspaceAccess` (shared with `ProjectEndpoints` now: `RefusedAsync`, `NotFound`, `ProjectArchived`, `UserIdOf`), the organisation taken from the stored project, candidate or evidence; then validation (400 keyed by the JSON names); `ConfirmedEmail` and antiforgery on every change; an archived project answers 409 `project-archived`. **Problem type** `ProblemTypes.TestStatusConflict` (`test-status-conflict`, 409, `HttpValidationProblemDetails` with `errors.testStatus` and the extension `evidenceCount`; decision 3 (a)); a second evidence of a procedure is 400 at `testTypeId`. **Date:** not after today in 🔧 `Workspace:TimeZone` (`WorkspaceSettings`, `appsettings.json`, checked at the start). **Catalog.Contracts:** `ICatalogTestTypes` (implemented by `Catalog/Reading/CatalogTestTypes` on the cached filter values). **Projects:** `ProjectResponse` gains `candidates[]` (`CandidateSummaryResponse`: id, name, vendor, test status, in the order they were added) and the real `productCount`; the list counts the candidates per project in one query. **Tests:** `Unit/Workspace/CandidateProductTests`, `Integration/Workspace/CandidateEndpointsTests`, `WorkspaceMigrationTests.CandidateTestStatus_Schema_NotNullWithDefaultUnknown`, four samples in `OrganizationIsolationTests` (a sample is now a list of rows, so it can store its project), 42 rows in `AuthorizationMatrixTests`; `ProjectEndpointsTests` compares answers with `ShouldBeEquivalentTo` (a record with a list has no value equality). The integration tests date evidences before the fixture clock's start (2026-03-02). **Left for later tasks:** the client mapping of `test-status-conflict` and the area `candidate` in `AREAS` (S6-16, S6-17); „(optional)“ on the S33 labels and `candidate.evidence.qualification` in the UX copy; the name-match hint and „Merken“ (M6); removing a candidate (done in S6-22a, §2.112); ETags (S6-20); attachments; `IOrganizationDataDeletion` and the personal data export for candidates (done in S6-25, §2.102).

### 2.99 Project copy of a criteria list on the server (S6-12a)

Project copy, server part (S6-12a, M5.1; ARCHITECTURE C180, TESTING V335 – V338, SPEC v2.54; R-PRJ-02, R-PRJ-04, R-PRJ-06 server; owner decisions S6-12 D1 (a) – D4 (a) of 2026-11-18; **WSP-03** `active` without its candidate clauses, TEN-08's „project copy → 409“ covered, WSP-02 tested but `planned` until S6-18). **Link on the copy only (D1 (a)):** `CriteriaConfiguration.ProcurementProjectId` (nullable; `IsProjectCopy`), Configurator migration `ProjectCopies` (column plus a unique index where set, no foreign key, ADR-014). `ProcurementProject` has **no** `CriteriaConfigurationId` and Workspace no migration in this task: the project's list is found by asking Configurator. **Contract:** `IProjectCriteriaLists` (`Configurator.Contracts/Configurations/`): `CopyIntoProjectAsync(source, project, organisation, user)` → `ProjectCopyResult` (`Copied` with `ProjectCriteriaList { Id, Name, ReleaseVersion }`, `SourceNotFound` — unknown or another organisation's, also for a member of both —, `SourceIsProjectCopy`), and `FindOfProjectAsync(project)`. Implemented by `Configurations/ProjectCriteriaLists`: reads the source through the filter, removes the project's old copy and adds the new one in **one** `SaveChanges` (EF orders the delete before the insert on the unique index); a unique violation of two first assignments at once is retried once, as `UserPreferencesService` does (the last assignment wins). **Domain:** `CriteriaConfiguration.CopyForProject(project, user, now)` copies organisation, name, release, the selection keys and every row with its stored `ResolvedLevel` and decision; it throws for a copy as source; `ChangeSelection` throws on a copy. **Endpoint** (Workspace, `Projects/ProjectCriteriaListEndpoint` + `ProjectCriteriaListService`): `PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}/criteria-configuration` `{ sourceConfigurationId }` → 200 `{ id, name, releaseVersion }`; role (Editor, from the stored project) before the body; 400 `validation` at `sourceConfigurationId` for missing / unknown / foreign / copy; 409 `project-archived`; 409 `locked-criteria-list` with an `errors` extension at `sourceConfigurationId`. `ProjectEndpoints`' helpers `RefusedForProjectAsync`, `RefusedAsync`, `NotFound`, `UserIdOf` are `internal` now for it. `ProjectResponse` (read and PATCH) carries `CriteriaList` (`ProjectCriteriaListResponse` or `null`), read through the contract; creating a project answers `null`. Assigning does not move the project's `ModifiedAt`. **The lock:** since S6-18 `ProjectCriteriaListService.IsLockedAsync` on the assessment rows (§2.101); the hook `ICriteriaListLock` / `NoAssessmentsYet` of S6-12a is gone. **Configurator endpoints and a copy:** `ConfigurationResponse.ProcurementProjectId`; `ConfigurationService.ListAsync` skips copies; `OwnerOfAsync` → `ListOwner(OrganizationId, IsProjectCopy)` (`OrganizationOfAsync` uses it); `ChangeSelectionAsync`, `DeleteAsync` (now an `EditResult<bool>`) and `CopyToActiveReleaseAsync` answer `EditOutcome.ProjectCopy` → 409 `project-copy` (`ConfigurationEndpoints.ProjectCopyConflict`); `ShareLinkEndpoints.CreateAsync` answers it after the role check. `PATCH …/criteria/{rev}`, the read and the saved export are unchanged for a copy. **Problem type:** `ProblemTypes.ProjectCopy` (`project-copy`) without a client key (D2 (a)). **Tests:** `Unit/Configurator/CriteriaConfigurationTests` (copy, copy as source, selection of a copy), `Integration/Workspace/ProjectCriteriaListTests` (13: assign, replace, copy as source and the S14 list, missing/unknown/foreign source, archived, the lock through a host with a test double `AlwaysLocked`, the race, the unique index, decisions on a copy, its selection, delete and copy, its share link, SEC-10), `AuthorizationMatrixTests` row `assign-project-list`. WSP-02's tests are untagged until S6-18 flips it (TESTING V336). **Not built:** the client (S6-12b: S11 in project-copy mode at `/projekte/:id/kriterienliste` (D4 (a)), the list choice of S31, `projects.detail.criteriaAdded`); the server's refusal of a change to an archived project's copy (D3 (a)).

`projects.detail.noCriteria` without a list). **E2E:** `e2e/workspace/projects.spec.ts` „… adjusts its own copy in S11; S32 reports the criteria taken in“ and „S31 offers „Keine Liste“ and the saved lists of the organisation“, which ends with S31's axe run and finds the select by CSS (Firefox crash, TESTING V341); ### 2.101 Assessments of candidate products on the server (S6-18)

Assessments M5.3, server part (S6-18; ARCHITECTURE C182, TESTING V342 – V347, SPEC v2.56; R-CAN-06 – R-CAN-08 server, R-PRJ-04 lock; owner decisions S6-18 1 (a) – 5 (a) of 2026-11-18; **CAT-15**, **CAT-17** (workspace part), **SIG-13**, **WSP-02** `active`, WSP-03 with its candidate clauses; TEN-01, TEN-04, TEN-05, SEC-10, API-01 gain cases). **Entity** (`Workspace/Domain/CandidateAssessment`, migration `CandidateAssessments`): key (`CandidateProductId`, `CriterionRevisionId`), `OrganizationId` with the query filter, `Fulfillment` (enum `Fulfillment`: `Unknown` and the six levels, by name), `EvidenceNote` ≤ 1 000, `ModifiedAt`; cascade from the candidate, no foreign key to the catalog revision. `Change(value, note, at)` says whether anything changed; `SaysNothing` (no value, no note) is the same as no row. **Nothing of the list is stored in the workspace:** `Assessments/AssessmentReader.ScopeOfAsync` reads the project's copy (`IProjectCriteriaLists.FindOfProjectAsync`, `IConfigurationReader.FindAsync`) and resolves its selection under its release (`ICatalogSelectionResolver`, cached snapshot) on every request into a `ProjectCriteriaScope`: `Requirements` (included requirement criteria — assessed and counted), `Procedures` (included procedure criteria — derived), `RatingsFromSeveralProcedures`. A copy that vanishes between the two reads (replaced in that moment) reads as „no list“. **Catalog.Contracts** for it: `ResolvedCatalogCriterion.AllowedRatings` (the union of C31 from `CriteriaResolver`; `Rating.None` for a procedure criterion) and `.TestTypeKey`, `ResolvedCatalogSelection.MappedTestTypeKeys` (an init property, so the existing constructor calls stay). **Pure rules** (`AssessmentProjection`): wire names of the values (`fulfilled` … `notApplicable`, `unknown`) and of a `Rating` set, `IsAllowed(value, allowed)` (CAT-15; `Unknown` always), `AnswerOf(testStatus, evidenceKeys, testTypeKey)` (`yes` / `no` / `notStated`, CAT-17), `SummaryOf(scope, values, …)` → `CandidateAssessmentSummary` (progress, `FulfillmentCountsResponse` with exactly seven members, procedure answers; a value outside `Requirements` is ignored, a criterion without a value is `unknown`). **Write** (`AssessmentService.SetAsync`): candidate and project through the filter, 409 for an archived project, the criterion must be in `scope.Requirements` (`NotInList` → 400 at `criterionRevisionId`: procedure criterion, excluded, unknown, no list — decision 2 (a)), the value allowed (400 at `value` with the allowed names in the message); the row is added, changed or removed (`SaysNothing`), and a change moves `ModifiedAt` of candidate and project (decision 3 (a)); an unchanged write saves nothing. Last write wins: a `DbUpdateException` (two first writes meeting at the key, or a row removed in between) clears the change tracker and repeats the change once — EF logs the first failure as an error, which is expected. **Endpoints** (`AssessmentEndpoints`, tag „Assessments“): `GET /candidates/{id}/assessments` (every member; `AssessmentReader.FindAsync`) and `PUT /candidates/{id}/assessments/{criterionRevisionId}` (Editor, `ConfirmedEmail`, antiforgery by the middleware; the route has no `:int` constraint, so a malformed id is a 400 Problem Details like every other route value, API-01). **Views:** `CandidateSummaryResponse` (in `ProjectResponse.Candidates`) carries `Progress`, `Fulfillment`, `ProcedureAnswers`, `CandidateResponse` carries `Progress`, `Fulfillment` — `null` while the project has no list; `ProjectService` loads the evidence keys with the candidates and asks `AssessmentReader.SummariesAsync` once for all of them (one query on the rows with a value), `CandidateService.ResponseOfAsync` does the same for one. **Lock (WSP-02):** `ProjectCriteriaListService.IsLockedAsync` — any row with a value other than `Unknown` of a candidate of the project (also on an excluded criterion); after a successful copy the project's rows without a value are deleted (`ExecuteDeleteAsync` through the filter; decision 4 (a)). Lock check and copy are two saves in two contexts — open point 1 of the protocol. **Tests:** `Unit/Workspace/AssessmentProjectionTests`; `Integration/Workspace/AssessmentEndpointsTests` and the helper `ProjectLists` (saves a list of `T.1` through the API, assigns it, sets an assessment; `WebAndMobileSelection` is the selection with two procedures); `ProjectCriteriaListTests` has the lock on real rows and the WSP-03 candidate clauses; `AuthorizationMatrixTests` (`read-assessments`, `set-assessment` — the matrix's project has no list, so the permitted roles get 400), `OrganizationIsolationTests` (sample). A public theory cannot take the module's internal enums as parameters: pass `nameof(…)` and parse. **Not built:** S34b (S6-19), the counts on S32 (S6-14), removing a candidate (done in S6-22a, §2.112), published assessments (step 6); Workspace still implements no `IOrganizationDataDeletion`.

### 2.102 Projects in the account deletion and the personal data export (S6-25)

Account deletion and data export for step 5 (S6-25; ARCHITECTURE C183, TESTING V348 – V350, SPEC v2.57 §8.4, §8.13; R-AUTH-09, R-AUTH-10; owner decisions S6-25 D1 (a), D2 (a), D3 (a) of 2026-11-18; **PRV-07** (workspace part) `active`, TEN-11 gains its project cases; no migration, no client change). **Deletion:** `Workspace/Accounts/ProjectDeletionService` is the module's `IOrganizationDataDeletion`, registered in `WorkspaceModule` next to the Configurator's; `DELETE /api/v1/account` calls every implementation with the organisations that end. It deletes their `ProcurementProject` rows through the filter (`ExecuteDeleteAsync`); candidates, private sections, evidences and private notes go by the database's cascading foreign keys — **a new table below a project or a candidate needs a cascading foreign key, or its own delete there** (the assessments of S6-18 have one). A project's copy of a list is a `CriteriaConfiguration` of the organisation and goes with `CriteriaListDeletionService`, unchanged. **Export:** `IProjectDataExport` (`IdentityAccess.Contracts/Accounts/`, with `ExportedProject`, `ExportedProjectCriteriaList`, `ExportedCandidate`, `ExportedTestEvidence`), implemented by `Workspace/Accounts/ProjectDataExportService`: the projects with `CreatedByUserId` = the user through the filter (only organisations the user still belongs to), oldest first; candidates with their evidences in one query, the private rows in one query each, both in the order they were added; organisation names from `IOrganizationMembers`, the list's name and release from `IProjectCriteriaLists.FindOfProjectAsync` (one read per project). Status, test status and kind through `ProjectService.NameOf` and `CandidateNames.Of`, so the file spells them as the API does; `testType` is the catalog key. `AccountExportEndpoints` maps the answer to `AccountExportProject`, `AccountExportProjectCriteriaList`, `AccountExportCandidate`, `AccountExportTestEvidence` (the property order is the order of SPEC §8.4); `createdByMe.projects` is typed now. The copy with its decisions stays in `createdByMe.criteriaLists` of the user who assigned it (D2 (a)): `CriteriaListDataExportService` does not skip copies. **Tests:** `AccountExportTests.Export_UserWhoCreatedProjects_…` (`INV:PRV-07`); the fixture of `AccountDeletionTests` has a project with candidate, assessment, evidence, both private rows and a copy in A and in „Einzelbüro“ (`ProjectRows`), its `Rows` count the six Workspace tables, the assessments among them, and the last-member case checks them. **Left for later tasks:** the assessments in the export (TESTING V350); contributions, profiles, entries and reports (steps 6 – 7).

### 2.104 Optimistic locking of the workspace resources: ETag and If-Match (S6-20a)

Concurrency M5.4, the shared parts and the Workspace module (S6-20a; ARCHITECTURE C185, TESTING V357 – V362, SPEC v2.59; R-PRJ-07, R-CAN-11 server; owner decisions S6-20 D1 (a) – D4 (a) of 2026-11-18; **API-03** (workspace part) and **API-04** active). The saved lists and the project's copy in Configurator follow with S6-20b, the conflict dialog S37 with S6-21.

- **The tag:** `EntityTags` (`Platform.Contracts/Concurrency`) formats `"<epoch>-<version>"` and compares it with `If-Match` — exactly one strong tag; `*`, a weak tag and a list match nothing. Platform registers one instance from the 🔧 `Concurrency:ETagEpoch` (`Platform/Concurrency/ConcurrencySettings`, `appsettings.json`: 1; missing or below 1 stops the start). Raise it after a database restore: every tag issued before is stale then.
- **Version and editor:** `ProcurementProject`, `CandidateProduct` and `TestEvidence` have `Version` (int, `IsConcurrencyToken()`, column default 1) and `ModifiedByUserId` (nullable, no foreign key), migration `ResourceVersions`. Not `xmin` (owner decision D1 (a)): the row is also written when a change below only stamps `ModifiedAt` (`MarkChanged`), and that must not make the tag stale. `MarkEdited(userId)` counts a version; the **services** call it, not the other domain methods, when the change changed something: project — name, status, list assignment; candidate — facts, private section, test status, an evidence added or removed (the set of evidences belongs to the test status, WSP-01); evidence — fields and private note. Assessments count nothing (R-CAN-11).
- **A change in a service** takes an `EditPrecondition(UserId, IfMatch)`, loads the row, answers the outcome `Conflict` with the row's `ModifiedByUserId` when `tags.Matches` fails, then checks the rest (archived, WSP-01), changes, calls `MarkEdited` and saves once. Every changing method runs inside `ConcurrentChange.RunAsync(db, …)` (`WorkspaceConcurrency.cs`): a `DbUpdateConcurrencyException` drops the tracked rows and runs the change again (three attempts), so a stale `If-Match` becomes the conflict of the fresh load and a save that only stamped a parent lands on the row as it is now. **A new changing method of the Workspace module goes through `RunAsync` too**, also one that needs no `If-Match`, because it stamps its parents.
- **Endpoints:** `[FromHeader(Name = "If-Match")] string? ifMatch`; after the role check `WorkspaceAccess.PreconditionRequired()` (428 `precondition-required`) for a missing header, before the validation; `WorkspaceAccess.ConflictAsync(users, editorUserId, …)` builds the 409 `conflict` with `editorDisplayName` from `IUserDirectory`. The answers set `response.Headers.ETag` from the DTO's `Etag` (`[JsonIgnore]` on `ProjectResponse` and `CandidateResponse`; a body member `etag` on `TestEvidenceResponse`, because an evidence is read inside its candidate). The answer of `PUT …/criteria-configuration` carries the **project's** new tag.
- **List assignment:** `ProjectCriteriaListService` lets Configurator store the copy first and counts the project's version after, so a refused source keeps the sender's tag valid; a project change between the check and the save answers 409 although the copy is replaced (two contexts).
- **Found on the way:** `CandidateService.ChangeEvidenceAsync` recognised a changed evidence by the candidate's `ModifiedAt`, which says nothing when two changes share an instant; it asks `evidence.Holds(facts)` before the change now.
- **Client:** `core/concurrency/etag.ts` — `withEtag(response)` joins the body with the `ETag` header (request with `observe: 'response'`), `ifMatch(etag)` builds the header. `Project` and `Candidate` have `etag` (declared in the models, which stay import-free), `TestEvidence` its body member. `CandidatesApiService.change(id, body, etag)` and `ProjectsApiService.assignCriteriaList(projectId, sourceId, projectEtag)` (through `withWorkspace().assignCriteriaList`) send it; S31 assigns with the tag of the project it has just created. Spec fixtures: `bodyOf(resource)` in `testing/candidate-fixtures.ts` gives the body without the tag. A 409 `conflict` shows the general error text until S37.
- **nginx:** the client image keeps `/api/` uncompressed, and must — nginx turns a strong ETag into a weak one when it compresses, and the API accepts only the strong tag.
- **Tests:** `Workspace/WorkspaceConcurrencyTests.cs`; every other test that changes a workspace resource uses `Infrastructure/IfMatchRequests.cs` (`PatchCurrentAsync`, `PutBelowCurrentAsync`, `SendWithTagAsync`, `FirstTag`).

### 2.105 Project overview S32, the dialog „Projekt bearbeiten“ and `pa-breadcrumb` (S6-14)

Projects M5.1, S32 (S6-14; ARCHITECTURE C186, TESTING V363 – V368, SPEC v2.60, UX copy v44 U177 – U181, Handoff 1.39, Figma mapping 1.30; R-PRJ-03, R-PRJ-05 UI, R-PRJ-04 UI in part; owner decisions S6-14 D1 (a) – D6 (a) of 2026-11-18; no invariant flipped — SIG-13, WSP-01, WSP-02, A11Y-01, A11Y-08, A11Y-09, TEN-01, API-02, SEC-10 gain cases; no migration). **Server (Workspace):** `CandidateSummaryResponse` carries `TestEvidences` (`TestEvidenceTypeResponse`: kind, procedure key, other name; read with the candidates in one query, ordered like the candidate answer: `CreatedAt`, then `Id`); `ProjectResponse` carries `CriteriaListLocked` from `ProjectCriteriaListService.IsLockedAsync`, which `ProjectService` now takes as a dependency. **Models:** `models/projects.ts` — `Project.candidates` (`ProjectCandidate`, `ProjectEvidenceType`), `criteriaListLocked`, `ProjectChange`, `PROJECT_STATUSES`; it imports only types from `candidates.ts` (`import type`, no cycle at run time). **API/store:** `ProjectsApiService.change` (`PATCH /projects/{id}` with `ifMatch` of the project's tag and `withEtag` on the answer, S6-20a; `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES`); `withWorkspace().changeProject` updates the item in the loaded S30 list of the project's organisation. **Load:** `pages/project-detail/project-detail-load.ts` `loadProjectView` reads the project and, with a list, its copy through `loadSavedListView` (as S11 of a copy and S34); `ProjectCriteriaFacts` holds list name, release, the scope (`levelCounts` over the **included** criteria; included „Geprüft nach …“ criteria counted apart) and the selection by name (`selectionNames`); a failed read of the copy is the server's (S80). **Page** `project-detail.ts`: see Handoff „S32 as built“; „Projekt bearbeiten“ for Editor/Owner (`isProjectEditor` of `candidate-load.ts`), menu and „Kriterienliste anpassen“ only when not archived; the products reversed on the client (the server lists them as added, `orderNote` says last added first); „Abgleich“ uses `ASSESSMENT_VALUES` for the order and the seven `directory.entry.row.*` keys — added to `de.json` by hand, because `directory` is not an area of `check-i18n.mjs` yet (step 6 adds it; the texts equal the UX copy, rule 2 of the check); evidence kinds as `testType.<key>`, the entered name or `testType.own`; an organisation switch in the header navigates to S30 (`toObservable` of the active organisation, first value skipped). **Dialog** `dialogs/edit-project-dialog/`: as S31 (error summary, `pa-confirm-required-notice`, error notice), radio group for the status, `autoFocus` `.mat-mdc-radio-checked input` for an archived project (jsdom finds nothing tabbable, so `first-tabbable` would focus the container), sends only what changed. **Shared:** `shared/components/breadcrumb/` `pa-breadcrumb` (`BreadcrumbItem`; inline template; separator `content: '›' / ''`), used on S32 and S34. **Checks:** `check-figma-links.mjs` `PENDING` is empty again. **Not built:** assigning or replacing the list from S32 (D2 (a), follow-up issue); the publication states of a product (step 6).

### 2.106 Optimistic locking of the saved lists and the project's copy (S6-20b)

Concurrency M5.4, the Configurator part (S6-20b; ARCHITECTURE C187, TESTING V369 – V373, SPEC v2.61; R-SAV-03, R-PRJ-07 for the copy, R-CAN-11 server; owner decisions S6-20 D1 (a) – D4 (a), no new one; **API-03 complete**). Builds on §2.104 (`EntityTags`, `precondition-required`, `core/concurrency/etag.ts`, `IfMatchRequests`).

- **Version and editor:** `CriteriaConfiguration` has `Version` (`IsConcurrencyToken()`, column default 1) and `ModifiedByUserId`, migration `ListVersions`; `Create` (also `CopyForProject`) starts at version 1 with the creator. `ConfigurationService` calls `MarkEdited(userId)` after `Decide` and `ChangeSelection` — every stored decision counts, also one that repeats the stored values.
- **A change in the service** takes a `ListEdit(UserId, IfMatch)`: load, `EditResult.Conflict(editor)` when `tags.Matches` fails (before the criterion is looked for, before `project-copy` and the validation), change, `SaveAsync`. `SaveAsync` answers a `DbUpdateConcurrencyException` — another decision, a selection change that removed the criterion (the 500 of S5-21a R1), a deletion stored since the load — as `Conflict` with the stored editor or `NotFound` when the list is gone. No retry as in the Workspace: nothing stamps a list from below.
- **Endpoints** (`ConfigurationEndpoints`): `GET /configurations/{id}` sets the `ETag` header (`[JsonIgnore] Etag` on `ConfigurationResponse`); `ConfigurationSummaryResponse` has the body member `etag` (S14 deletes from the list); `PATCH …/criteria/{id}` (204), `PUT …/selection` and `DELETE` take `If-Match`, 428 after the role check, 409 through `ConflictAsync`; `AnswerAsync` sets the new tag. The 428 and 409 answers are built here as in `WorkspaceAccess` — `Platform.Contracts` has no ASP.NET reference to share them.
- **Replacing a project's copy:** `ProjectCriteriaLists.CopyIntoProjectAsync` runs its replacement a second time also after a `DbUpdateConcurrencyException` (the copy changed or went since it was loaded); the project's `If-Match` guards the assignment, not the copy's version.
- **Client:** `SavedList.etag` (header), `SavedListSummary.etag` (body). `ConfiguratorApiService.decide(…, etag)` resolves to the list's new tag; `changeSelection(…, etag)`, `delete(…, etag)`. `SavedListEditor.start(listId, etag, …)` keeps the tag and saves **one change at a time for the whole list** (one promise chain, before one per criterion): two saves under way would send the same tag. After a failed save the tag stays, so a conflict stays one until the list is loaded again. `SavedListSelection.list` carries the tag for S10's selection change; S14 deletes with the entry's tag.
- **Tests:** `Configurator/SavedListConcurrencyTests.cs`; the race is made with a save interceptor on a context the test gives `ConfigurationService`, so it does not depend on timing. Other tests change lists through `PatchListAsync`, `PutListAsync`, `DeleteListAsync` of `IfMatchRequests`. An E2E spec that answers a list itself sends an `ETag`.

`EVIDENCE_LIMITS` (the server's lengths); `TestEvidenceRequest` (every field; `testTypeId` only with `procedure`, `otherTestTypeName` only with `other`). **API** (`candidates-api.service.ts`): `changeTestStatus(id, status, removeEvidences, etag)` with `SAVE_TEST_STATUS_PROBLEMS` (`SAVE_CANDIDATE_PROBLEMS` + `test-status-conflict`) and the candidate's tag, `addEvidence` (no tag), `changeEvidence(id, body, etag)` and `removeEvidence(id, etag)` with the evidence's own tag (S6-20a's open point 3; `@figma` `2022:267`, `2144:3508`): the radio group in a card (fieldset/legend, hint by `aria-describedby`), `candidate.evidence.details.hint` while not „Ja“; with „Ja“ the H2, `common.requiredNote` and the cards; „Weiteren Prüfnachweis hinzufügen“ focuses the new card's select, „Prüfnachweis n entfernen“ announces `.removed` and focuses the previous card's H3 or the add button. **One „Speichern“** (D1 (a)): `PUT test-status` with the candidate's tag when the status changed (leaving „Ja“ with `removeEvidences` when saved evidences were removed — separate `DELETE`s would change the candidate's tag first; The form is filled once per candidate id (`filledFor`), so the refresh keeps what the user typed. a blank note is sent as `null`). **K6** `shared/components/assessment-input/` (`AssessmentInput`, `@figma 2012-2430`): inputs `criterionTitle`, `allowedValues`, `layout` (`radios` | `compact`), `noteMaxLength`; `loadAssessments` (shows the stored view of the same candidate while reading again; merges rows still waiting or in flight), `changeAssessment` (applies at once; one timer per row; the saves of a row chained; nothing for Readers and archived projects (Q3 (a); the read-only view since S6-22a, §2.112); on destroy `flushAssessments`, then `get` of each changed candidate and only `progress`/`fulfillment` into `CandidateLoad` (the candidate's tag stays). `CandidateTabPlaceholder` is gone; ### 2.110 The conflict dialog S37 for the workspace forms (S6-21a)

Concurrency M5.4, the dialog and the workspace forms (S6-21a; ARCHITECTURE C191, TESTING V387 – V391, SPEC v2.65, UX copy v47 U188 – U189, Handoff 1.43, Figma mapping 1.34; R-CAN-11 UI; owner decisions S6-21 D1 (a) – D5 (a) of 2026-11-19; no invariant flipped — API-03 gains its client cases, A11Y-01, A11Y-08, A11Y-09 gain S37; no server change, no migration). Builds on §2.104 and §2.106.

- **Dialog** `shared/components/conflict-dialog/conflict-dialog.ts` (`ConflictDialog`, `@figma 2056-2519`): `ConflictDialog.show(dialog, { editorDisplayName, changes })` resolves to `true` for „Neue Fassung laden“, `false` for Escape. `role: 'alertdialog'`, `ariaDescribedBy` the body, `autoFocus` `.pa-conflict-dialog__copy`, panel class `pa-dialog`. „Meine Änderungen kopieren“ writes `changes` with `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, announces `conflict.copied` and stays open; without a clipboard the general error (`ERROR.GENERIC`, snackbar). `conflict.body.unknown` when the name is `null` (D1 (a)).
- **State of a form** `edit-conflict.ts`: `EditConflict` — `caught(error)` takes a `conflict` (true) and nothing else, `blocked` until `clear()`, `editorDisplayName`; `changesText(groups)` — „label: value“ per field, a group's heading above, groups apart by an empty line (D2 (a), U189). **A form that saves a shared resource with `If-Match`:** `caught` in its catch → `ConflictDialog.show` with its changed fields; at the start of a save `if (conflict.blocked)` → show S37 again and send nothing; on `true` read the resource again, `clear()`, refill and `PageTitleStrategy.focusPageHeading()`.
- **Callers:** `EditProjectDialog` (S37 over itself; closes with `RELOAD_PROJECT`; `EditProjectResult`), `ProjectDetailPage.reload` (`loadProjectView` with the loading bar, then the H1); `CandidateFormPage` (S33: `valuesOf(candidate)` is what was read, the changed fields are copied; `reload` → `CandidateLoad.view`, the effect refills); `CandidateEvidence` (S34a: a `conflict` anywhere in the sequence stops it — the refresh in `finally` still runs, so the other tabs show what is stored —, `unsaved()` = the changed status and each card that `needsSaving()` under its H3 with `EvidenceCard.changedFields()`; `reload` sets `filledFor = null` so the effect fills the tab again). `SAVE_CANDIDATE_PROBLEMS` gains `conflict`; `ProjectsApiService.change` was `CALLER_SHOWS_FAILURES` already. `ProblemDetails.editorDisplayName`.
- **i18n:** `conflict` joins `AREAS` of `check-i18n.mjs`.
- **Tests:** `conflict-dialog.spec.ts` (dialog, `changesText`, `EditConflict`), the conflict cases of `edit-project-dialog.spec.ts`, `project-detail.spec.ts`, `candidate-form.spec.ts`, `candidate-evidence.spec.ts`; E2E `e2e/workspace/conflict.spec.ts` (Owner and Editor in two browser contexts; the Editor joins through `POST /api/v1/invitations/accept` with the mailed token) and S37 in the S32 case of `reflow.spec.ts` (the mocked `PATCH` answers 409).
- **S6-21b:** S11 and S10 of the configurator, §2.111. S14's delete keeps the general error.

### 2.111 The conflict dialog S37 for the saved lists (S6-21b)

Concurrency M5.4, the configurator part of S37 (S6-21b; ARCHITECTURE C192, TESTING V392 – V396, SPEC v2.66, Handoff 1.44, Figma mapping 1.35; R-CAN-11 UI, R-SAV-03, R-PRJ-07; owner decisions S6-21 D3 (a), D4 (a) of 2026-11-19; no invariant flipped, no server change, no new UX key). Builds on §2.106 and §2.110.

- **`SavedListEditor`** (`pages/saved-list/saved-list-editor.ts`): an `EditConflict` of its own; after a `conflict` the refused decision stays shown, every save that would be sent emits on **`conflicts`** (an `Observable<void>`) instead, `unsaved()` → `UnsavedDecision[]` (`criterion`, `saved`, `shown`) in list order, `conflictEditor`; `start()` clears the conflict. Other failures still put the criterion back.
- **`SavedListLoad.reload`**: the loader of the route, set by `savedListResolver` (`loadSavedListView`) and `projectCriteriaListResolver` (`loadProjectCriteriaList`); default: the view as it is.
- **S11** (`saved-list-page.ts`): subscribes to `editor.conflicts` (`takeUntilDestroyed`), `showConflict` opens S37 once at a time (`conflictShown`) and never after the page is destroyed; `changedGroup` builds the copied text (`criteria.item.include` + `common.yes|no`, `criteria.priority.changeLabel` + the level that applies, `criteria.priority.weightLabel`, `criteria.item.note`; heading `cardTitle`: the card's H2); `reload()` runs `load.reload()` with the loading bar, then `show({ fromRoute: false })` — no added-criteria report, no arrival — and `focusPageHeading()`.
- **S10** (`criteria-select.ts`): `SavedListSelection.list` is a `SavedListSelectionContext` with the stored `selection`; `submit()` checks `conflict.blocked` before the server check; `saveSelectionOfSavedList` answers `'saved' | 'conflict' | 'failed'`, S37 opens after the spinner stopped; the copied layers come from `selectionNames` of the draft and of the stored selection with the S11 summary labels; „Neue Fassung laden“ navigates to `/kriterienlisten/:id`.
- **API:** `decide` and `changeSelection` set `CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` `['conflict']` (`conflictContext()`); `delete` does not.
- **Tests:** the conflict cases of `saved-list-editor.spec.ts`, `saved-list-page.spec.ts`, both routing specs, `criteria-select.spec.ts`, `configurator-api.service.spec.ts`; E2E `e2e/workspace/conflict.spec.ts` gains the saved list (`ownerAndEditor`, `countPatches` shared by both tests).

### 2.112 Removing a candidate and the Reader view of S34 (S6-22a)

Candidates M5.2 and M5.5, first part of S6-22 (S6-22a; ARCHITECTURE C193, TESTING V397 – V403, SPEC v2.67, UX copy v48 U190 – U191, Handoff 1.45; R-CAN-09, R-CAN-10; owner decisions S6-22 D1 (a) – D4 (a) of 2026-11-19; **WSP-04** `active` for its workspace part, **TEN-05** complete for the workspace endpoints; API-03, TEN-01, SEC-10 gain cases; no migration). The journey J2 with A11Y-02 is S6-22b.

- **Server:** `DELETE /api/v1/candidates/{id}` (`CandidateEndpoints.RemoveAsync`, name `DeleteCandidate`): Editor through `WorkspaceAccess`, `ConfirmedEmail`, `If-Match` with the candidate's tag (428 / 409 `conflict`), 409 `project-archived`, 204. `CandidateService.RemoveAsync` in `ConcurrentChange.RunAsync`: `db.CandidateProducts.Remove`, `project.MarkChanged`; the database cascades to the private section, the evidences with their notes and the assessments (no code reads them). The published-entry part of WSP-04 is step 6.
- **Client, removal:** `CandidatesApiService.remove(id, etag)` (`CALLER_HANDLES_PROBLEMS` `['not-found']`). `CandidateOverview.remove`: `ConfirmDialog` with `candidate.remove.title|body|confirm` → `DELETE` → `router.navigateByUrl(projectPath)` → `candidate.removed` announced; `not-found` counts as removed; after another failure `readAgain` puts the stored candidate into `CandidateLoad.view`. The button sits in `.pa-overview__remove` after the last card, under the same `canEdit()` as „Bearbeiten“. An announcement made right after a route change is followed by the page title in the live region — both are read; an E2E test that checks it records the live region (as `j5-session.spec.ts`).
- **Client, read only:** `CandidateEvidence` — `@else if (candidate(); as candidate)` after the form: `.pa-evidence__facts` lists, `storedHeading`, `storedKindKey`, `date`. `CandidateAssessment` — `shown` no longer depends on the role, `editable` does; the template keeps one structure and switches intro, filter, union note, K6 / `.pa-assessment__card` with `.pa-assessment__facts`, and the link of the derived rows on `editable()`; the derived rows carry `.pa-assessment__card .pa-assessment__procedure`. A Reader's `loadAssessments` is the same store call; `leave()` has nothing to flush.
- **Texts:** `candidate.evidence.version.heading`, `.url.heading`, `.qualification.heading`, `candidate.assessment.note.heading` (area `candidate`, written by `check-i18n.mjs --write`).
- **Tests:** `CandidateEndpointsTests` (`Remove_*`, the removal in the archived, unconfirmed and other-organisation cases; helpers `RemoveAsync`, `StoreAssessmentAsync`), `AuthorizationMatrixTests` (`remove-candidate`), `WorkspaceConcurrencyTests` (`Change.RemoveCandidate`); `candidate-overview.spec.ts`, `candidate-evidence.spec.ts` and `candidate-assessment.spec.ts` („read only“), `candidates-api.service.spec.ts`. **E2E:** `e2e/workspace/candidates.spec.ts` (removal; a Reader in a second browser context, `storeEvidenceAndAssessment`), `e2e/shell/reflow.spec.ts` (read-only S34a and S34b), `acceptInvitationThroughApi` in `e2e/support/accounts.ts` (used by `conflict.spec.ts` too).
- **Not built:** J2 as a keyboard-only journey (done in S6-22b, §2.113); the publish button, `candidate.remove.publishedNote` and the entry that survives the removal (step 6); a Figma frame for the removal and the Reader view.

### 2.113 Journey J2 up to the assessment (S6-22b)

The M5 exit check, second part of S6-22 (S6-22b; TESTING V404 – V406; no invariant flipped — A11Y-02 gains J2, A11Y-01 and A11Y-08 its states; no server or client change, no migration, no new UX key). `e2e/journeys/j2-workspace.spec.ts`: one test, keyboard only as J1 (`tabTo` with Tab or Shift+Tab, `arrowToOption` for an open `mat-select`, `openTab` for the ARIA tabs of S34), at each browser project's own viewport — the compact K6 and its note button below 600 px are a branch of the test (`compact`). The Owner's path S30 → S31 → S32 → S11 of the copy → S33 → S34a → S34b → Überblick → S32, then a Reader in a second browser context through S32 and the three tabs. Axe in both appearances on twelve states, not on S31 (TESTING V341). **Rules it follows, for the next journey of these screens:** CSS locators only (Firefox, TESTING V165, V226, V368); wait for the dialog's first focus before typing; wait for the response of an autosave before the next step; the way back from S11 of a copy is `page.goBack()`. The account, the organisation, its saved list (`saveListThroughApi`: no test procedure, so no „Geprüft nach …“ row) and the Reader's membership (`acceptInvitationThroughApi`) come through the API. **Not built:** the publication half of J2 (step 6).

### 2.114 The Firefox crash in axe runs and the S10 fade-in (S6-06)

Issues #355 and #339 (TESTING V407 – V411; no invariant flipped, no server or client change, no migration, no new UX key). **Cause:** Firefox's page process segfaults when `ariaLabelledByElements` of one element is read in the page's main world (axe) and in an isolated world (Playwright's name lookups); stock Firefox 160 does it too. **`e2e/support/axe.ts`:** `keepAxeOffLabelledByElements`, called first in `expectNoAxeViolations`, deletes the property from `Element.prototype` in the main world, in Firefox only, so axe reads the attribute (same findings; `axe-helper.spec.ts` has the regression test). Run axe only through this helper and never read the property in a test's `page.evaluate`. **J1:** the second part runs in Firefox again; `tabTo` takes `Tab` or `Shift+Tab` as in J2, and the step to S14 backs up to „Konto“, because past the end of the page Firefox goes on inside `main`. **`s10-step1.spec.ts`:** `openPage` waits for the field's `mat-form-field-animations-enabled` and the end of its animations before axe. **Not changed:** the CSS locators and other Firefox workarounds in eleven spec files and the rule that a new `<section>` has no accessible name (TESTING V229); they can go in a follow-up.

### 2.115 Nightly backup and rehearsed restore (S6-29)

R-ADM-04 (completion), ROADMAP GP.2; ARCHITECTURE C194, SPEC v2.68 (SD26); owner decisions S6-29 D1 (a) – D4 (a). No invariant flipped, no EF migration, no new dependency. The runbook is `docs/TECHNICAL.md` §7 (§1 until S6-30). **Service:** `backup` in `docker-compose.yml`, image of `db`, behind the compose profile `backup`, so the dev and E2E stacks never start it; staging and production set `COMPOSE_PROFILES=backup`. **Scripts** (`scripts/backup/`, POSIX `sh`, they run in the Alpine image): `schedule.sh` is the service's main process (nightly at `BACKUP_TIME_UTC`, catches up at its start when no dump is younger than a day); `backup.sh` dumps every schema under a temporary name, renames it only after `pg_restore --list` has read it, and only then applies the retention (🔧 `BACKUP_RETENTION_DAYS`, 14) by the time in the file name; `restore.sh <dump> <new database>` only creates a new database, refuses an existing name and removes what a failed restore created. **ETag epoch:** compose passes `Concurrency__ETagEpoch` from `CONCURRENCY_ETAG_EPOCH`; raise it after every restore, never lower it. **Rehearsal:** `scripts/rehearse-restore.mjs` on `docker-compose.rehearsal.yml` (own project and network, no fixed host port, database in memory, generated secrets, the real `.env` not read), so it runs beside the dev or E2E stack; not in CI. **Rules it found:** the API must not start in `Development` on a restored database, because `appsettings.Development.json` sets `RebuildDatabase` (the rehearsal override switches it off); the seeders are off for the restored database, so what it serves can only come from the dump. **Tests:** `scripts/backup-restore.test.mjs` runs the shell scripts against fake PostgreSQL tools (CI stage 1). **Not built:** the off-host copy and an alert on an unhealthy service (GP.1), the file store's backup (step 7), WAL archiving (D1 (a)).

### 2.116 The Firefox workarounds of the E2E specs retired (#434)

Follow-up of S6-06 (TESTING V412 – V415; no invariant flipped, no server or client change, no migration, no new UX key). With the axe helper of §2.114 the eleven spec files that avoided Playwright's name lookups in Firefox find controls by role and accessible name again; J1's `labelledBy` is gone, and J2 runs axe on S31 (`j2-s31`). CSS stays only where it stands for no name (the K6 and evidence cards, counts, the live announcer, the active `mat-option`). **Rule now:** a spec finds controls by role and name; axe runs only through `expectNoAxeViolations`, and no test reads `ariaLabelledByElements` itself. A `<section>` may be named where the Handoff describes a region; the existing unnamed sections are unchanged (K1 and K2 with SIG-09 in step 6). **Open:** one closed Firefox page in `invitations.spec.ts` in 3,581 repeated runs in CI, not reproduced in 600 more runs of that spec and without a segfault in the runner's kernel log (TESTING V415).

### 2.117 `TECHNICAL.md` and the README (S6-30)

R-MET-02, ROADMAP GP.6, process step 16, #4; owner decisions S6-30 D1 (a), D2 (a), D3 (a) of 2026-11-24. No invariant flipped (TESTING has none for R-MET-02), no EF migration, no new dependency, no UI change. **`docs/TECHNICAL.md`** is now the whole operations and onboarding document, unversioned like step 16 names it: §1 architecture in short (pointers only), §2 local setup (SDK pin, Node, disk space, dev stack, sample user, source runs, tests), §3 configuration and secrets (every compose variable; the four settings compose does not pass yet — `App:BaseUrl`, `Email:From`/`DisplayName`, `Export:ToolUrl`, `ProblemTypes:BaseUri`; the client's build-time values; the release check), §4 deployment (release steps, migrations at start with one instance, rollback, the `CREATE` privilege on `public` for the ICU collation, which ports stay closed, the proxy and ETags), §5 the `catalog` and `roles` commands (`T.1` against a real release; a role change applies at the next sign-in), §6 logs and health, §7 backup and restore (the S6-29 chapter, moved from §1 unchanged), §8 the indicator queries of SPEC §9.2, §9 where to read more. Text of ARCHITECTURE, SPEC, TESTING and the Handoff is pointed to, not copied. **Metric queries (§8):** one `sql` block per indicator whose first line is `-- metric: <name>` — `completed-configurations`, `exports` (`analytics."UsageEvents"` per UTC month and dimension), `saved-lists` (lists without `ProcurementProjectId`), `workspace-entries` (`workspace."CandidateProducts"`), `priority-overrides` (per `Criterion.Key`: in how many saved lists, level changed, weight set, excluded); published against private entries and the Kanal growth follow with step 6 (D2 (a)), pilot procurements are counted by hand. Run on the host through `psql` with `default_transaction_read_only`. **Test (D1 (a)):** `tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Integration/Operations/MetricQueryTests.cs` reads the blocks from the document (linked into the test output as `Docs/TECHNICAL.md` by the project file), checks that the set of names is the one it knows, and runs each in a read-only transaction against two organisations' lists, a project copy that must not count, three candidates and six usage events; it compares the rows and refuses a `uuid` column. A renamed table or column of §8 therefore fails CI. **README:** what ProcureAccess is, status, quick start with the dev stack, technology, documents, contributing, and the licence section as before. **`CLAUDE.md`:** the row `TECHNICAL.md` of the document table (added by S6-09) describes the whole document; S6-09 had already made room by moving the state paragraph here (§2.0), so D3 (a) needed no shortening.

### 2.118 The causes of the E2E failures on `main` (S7-01)

Issues #451, #452, #436 and TD-99 (TECH_DEBT TD-84, TD-85, TD-99; A11Y-01, A11Y-02, A11Y-03, A11Y-08 stay green; no invariant flipped, no EF migration, no new dependency, no new UX key). Each cause comes from the trace of the failed CI runs; the protocol `docs/sprints/sprint_07/S7-01_e2e_causes.md` has the evidence. **#451 (test):** `shell/start-and-error-pages.spec.ts` routed every API request to a 500 right after `goto`, while the legacy start-up read `GET /api/Products` (after the session probe) could still be pending: S80 showed before the click, the click moved the focus to the link, and the router dropped S10's second navigation to `error` as the same address (`skipLocationChange`), so nothing moved the focus. The test now fails only S10's catalog reads (`CATALOG_API`). That the router drops a repeated 5xx while S80 shows in place (no focus, title or announcement after the user's click) is a proposed issue, not changed. **#452 (b) (product):** `mat-sidenav` records the element to return the focus to each time it reports „opened“, also on a late `transitionend` of its own (its container turns transitions on 200 ms after it is created, so a click inside that window starts the transform after the drawer has opened); by then the focus is on the first item, and Esc returned it there. `App.menuClosing()` (the drawer's `closedStart`) moves the focus to „Menü“ through `Header.focusMenuButton()` while the focus is still in the menu or nowhere; a chosen page item keeps the page's focus move to its H1, because the focus has left the menu by then. `app.spec.ts` reproduces the late `transitionend` and fails without the fix. **#452 (a) (test):** under reduced motion `html.motion-reduced * { transition-duration: 0.01ms }` (`theme.scss`) gives every element a transition of every property, so an inherited colour reaches one DOM level per frame: after a switch to dark the cards' text kept its light colour for many frames, after the axe helper's single wait had returned (seen in 38 of 60 repeated runs). `settled` in `e2e/support/axe.ts` now waits frame by frame until a frame passes without a running finite animation (at most 200 frames), and `showAppearance` uses it. The visible colour ripple itself is a proposed issue (Handoff §1.7), not changed. **#436 (test):** `identity/account.spec.ts` waits for the dialog's first focus before it types; typing earlier lost the text to MatDialog's focus move. **TD-99:** `e2e/support/keyboard.ts` holds `tabTo` (Tab or Shift+Tab, at most 80 presses), `arrowTo`, `arrowToOption` with `activeOption`, `settled(locator)` and `DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT`; the 18 specs that had a copy import it, and every `waitForEvent('download')` has the bound. **Rule now:** a spec takes these helpers from `support/keyboard.ts` and bounds a download wait with `DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT`.
