```
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Catalog.Contracts/ `ICatalogSelectionResolver` and its records (S4-19), `ICatalogReleaseCarryOver` and its records (S5-21a), `ICatalogTestTypes` (S6-15), enums `CriterionKind`, `PriorityLevel`, `Rating`
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform.Contracts/ `IUsageEventWriter`, `ProblemTypes`, `IModuleDatabaseMigrator`, `RateLimitPolicies` (names of the rate-limit policies, S4-21a), `IKeyedRateLimiter` and `RateLimitExceededException` (limits keyed by a value of the body, S5-13a); Email/ (`IEmailSender`, the message types, `EmailDeliveryException`, S5-11); Tenancy/ (`IOrganizationContext`, the organisation set of the request that every module's query filter reads, and `FixedOrganizationContext` for contexts outside a request, S5-15); Sharing/ (`IShareLinks`, `IShareableResourceProvider`, `ShareableResourceType`, S5-23); Concurrency/ (`EntityTags`, the ETag of a shared resource, S6-20a)
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.IdentityAccess.Contracts/ Session/ (`SessionResponse`, `SessionUser` — the answer of the probe with `roles` and `permissions`, S5-08, #320); Accounts/ (`IAccountDataExport` and its records, the memberships and contributor profiles of the personal data export, S5-27; `IAccountTokens` and its records, `IdentityAccessPolicies.ConfirmedEmail`, S5-12a; `IAccountDeletion`, S5-28a; `IUserPreferences`, S5-25a; since S6-10: `ICriteriaListDataExport` with its records and `IOrganizationDataDeletion`, which the modules that own the data implement, `IUserDirectory`, the display name of a user for another module, and `PlatformRoles`, the platform role names; `IProjectDataExport` with its records, implemented by Workspace, S6-25); Organizations/ (`OrganizationRole`, `OrganizationType`, `IOrganizationResource` with `OrganizationReference`, `OrganizationRoleRequirement` and `OrganizationAccess.StatusOf` — the resource-based role check, S5-15; `IOrganizationMembers`, `OrganizationLimits`, `MembershipChangeResult`, S5-16a; `IOrganizationInvitations` and its records, S5-17a)
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.IdentityAccess/ Persistence/ (IdentityAccessDbContext with the Data Protection key ring `identity."DataProtectionKeys"`, Migrations/) (S3-24); DataProtection/ (`KeyRingCertificates`, the certificate that encrypts the key ring, S4-26); Domain/ (`ContributorProfile`, `UserContributorLink`, `ContributorKind`) (S4-25; `AccountToken` S5-12a); Accounts/ (`AccountTokenService`, `AccountTokenSettings` 🔧, S5-12a; `AccountTokenCleanupService`, the clean-up of expired tokens, S6-05; `AccountDataExportService`, S5-27; `UserPreferencesService` and `UserPreferencesEndpoints`, `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/account/preferences`, S5-25a); Session/ (`SessionSettings` 🔧, the Identity cookie set-up and its events, the antiforgery token cookie and validation middleware, `GET /api/v1/auth/session`) (S5-07; renewal on every request, expiry headers, stamp check and `SessionExtendEndpoint`, S5-29); Domain/ `Organization`, `OrganizationMembership` and Tenancy/ (`OrganizationContext`, the memberships of the request, its middleware and `OrganizationRoleHandler`) (S5-15); Organizations/ (`OrganizationMemberService`, the rules behind the organisation endpoints and the last-Owner rule, S5-16a; `TestOrganizationSeeder`, the E2E organisation, S5-16b; `OrganizationInvitationService`, `InvitationSettings` 🔧, `InvitationCleanupService` and the shared `OrganizationRowLock`, S5-17a); Domain/ `OrganizationInvitation` (S5-17a); Domain/ `UserPreferences` (S5-25a); since S6-10 the users: Domain/ `User`, Persistence/Configurations/IdentityTablesConfiguration (the seven Identity tables in the legacy layout) and the migration `AdoptIdentityTables`; Auth/ (`IdentityRegistration` = ASP.NET Core Identity on the module's context with the 🔧 password and lockout rules and the confirmation gate, `AuthEndpoints` = sign-in and sign-out, `RegistrationEndpoints`, `PasswordResetEndpoints`, `PasswordResetMailer`, `ConfirmedEmailAuthorization`, `PasswordPolicy`, `LockoutPolicy`, `ClockedUserManager`, `ClientLinks`); Accounts/ (`AccountEndpoints`, `AccountExportEndpoints`, `AccountDeletionEndpoints`, `LegacyAccountEndpoint` = `GET /api/me`, `UserDirectory`, `SeedUsers` = the sample user of the Development rebuild and the E2E users); Organizations/ (`OrganizationEndpoints`, `InvitationEndpoints`); Commands/ (`RoleCommandLine`, the `roles` command)
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform/ Concurrency/ (`ConcurrencySettings`: the 🔧 `Concurrency:ETagEpoch`, S6-20a); Sharing/ (`ShareLink`, `ShareLinkService`, `ShareLinkSettings`, `SharedEndpoints` = `GET /api/v1/shared/{token}`; table `sharing."ShareLinks"`, S5-23); Logging/ShareTokenMaskingEnricher (S5-23); Caching/ (`NoStoreDefault`: `no-store` on every `/api/v1` answer without a `Cache-Control`, S5-22); Email/ (`EmailSettings`, `EmailTexts` with Texts/de.json from the UX copy, `EmailComposer`, `SmtpEmailSender`, `MailKitSmtpTransport`, S5-11; the 🔧 `Email:Smtp:Timeout`, S6-05); Logging/, Analytics/, Health/, Persistence/ (PlatformDbContext, Migrations/) (S2-07), Errors/ (Problem Details, S2-10), RateLimiting/ (client IP from the trusted proxies, rate limiter, S4-21a); Health/HealthSettings (🔧 timeout and concurrent ready checks, S4-21a)
src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Catalog/  Domain/ (releases, criteria, revisions, standard references; filter values, exclusions, applicability, priorities, procedure texts S3-13), Persistence/ (CatalogDbContext, Configurations/, Migrations/, Seed/, CatalogImmutabilityInterceptor) (S3-12, S3-13, S3-16), Releases/ (release folder reader and validator S3-15, importer S3-16, activator S3-17), Reading/ (`CatalogReader`, cache by release S3-17), FilterOptions/ (`GET /api/v1/catalog/filter-options`, S3-19), Criteria/ (selection check and priority resolution S3-20a; `GET /api/v1/catalog/criteria` S3-20b), Selection/ (`SelectionKeys`, list parameters of both endpoints); Criteria/CatalogSelectionResolver (`ICatalogSelectionResolver`, S4-19), Criteria/CatalogReleaseCarryOver (`ICatalogReleaseCarryOver`, S5-21a), Http/ (`CatalogJsonAnswer`, caching of both endpoints; `CatalogLanguage`, the language of the answer, S4-23), Commands/ (`catalog` command line, S3-16)
catalog/releases/<version>/         Catalog release folders for `catalog import`: 2026.1 since S3-27 (texts only, not activated until O-1); test folders are in tests/…/Shared/Fixtures/Releases; every folder is validated by the unit test CatalogReleaseFoldersTests (S4-05)
tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.{Unit,Architecture,Integration,Shared}/   Backend tests; Shared = builders (`TestData.ACriterion()` …, catalog builders `Builders/Catalog/CatalogData`) and fixtures (`Fixtures/MiniCatalog`, S3-14; test release folders `Fixtures/Releases/T.1`, `T.2`, S3-15), the strict CSV reader `Export/CsvReader` (S4-20a), the Tenants fixture `Fixtures/Tenants` with the tenant users of `Auth/TestUsers` (S5-15), not a test project;
                                    Architecture/Modules/ = ARC-01 – ARC-03 with the module table `ModuleMap.cs` (S2-08); Architecture/Tenancy/ = TEN-02, the `IgnoreQueryFilters()` allow-list (S5-15); Architecture/Api/ = ARC-05, the `BaseCrudController` allow-list (S6-23)
                                    Integration/Tenancy/ = TEN-01 (reflection-driven isolation) and the TEN-05 matrix (S5-15; on the real organisation endpoints since S5-16a); Integration/Organizations/ = the organisation endpoints and TEN-11 (S5-16a), the invitation endpoints with SEC-11's invitation part (S5-17a)
                                    Integration/Api/ = API-01 and the other Problem Details tests (S2-10), TEN-04 cache headers (S4-19)
                                    Integration/Export/ = the export endpoint and EXP-05; Integration/Infrastructure/ DatabaseRowCounts ("persists nothing") and CapturingLoggerProvider (S4-19)
                                    Integration/Operations/MetricQueryTests = the indicator queries of docs/TECHNICAL.md §8, read from the document (R-MET-02, S6-30)
e2e/                                Playwright + axe (own package.json; eslint.config.mjs lints e2e/ and scripts/*.mjs, S3-26; playwright-image/Dockerfile pins the CI image, S4-01b): support/ (axe helper, suppressions, third-party request checks, global setup, combobox helper S4-14b, XLSX reader S4-22, expired session S5-09, Mailpit reader S5-12b, E2E users S5-14, accounts and organisations through the API S5-17b, a saved list through the API S5-24, the menu „Konto“ and sign-out S5-18, the movable clock of a browser context S5-30, keyboard and waiting helpers and the download bound S7-01), smoke/, identity/ (S01, overlay and sign-out, S5-09, on the seeded user since S5-14; edge cases of S02 and the confirmation page, S5-12b; S03 with the reset mail, S5-13b), journeys/ (J1 on S10, keyboard only, S4-13; to the export and the downloaded file, S4-22; J8 account: register, confirm, sign in, sign out, S5-14; J5 session warning and login overlay on the movable clock, S5-30; J2 workspace up to the assessment, keyboard only, with the Reader view, S6-22b), organization/ (S20 with SEC-10's E2E part, S21 on the seeded organisation, S5-16b; invitations on organisations of their own, S5-17b), configurator/ (S10, S4-10; S15 and S16 with TEN-09, S5-24), workspace/ (S30, S31 and the S32 placeholder, S6-13; S33 and S34 in candidates.spec.ts, S6-16; S37 with two browser contexts in conflict.spec.ts, S6-21a; the saved list there, S6-21b; removing a product and the Reader view in candidates.spec.ts, S6-22a), shell/ (S2-15; route change S2-16; start and error pages S2-17; reflow at 320 px S2-18; appearance S3-09; signed-in header states and organisation switch S5-18); the axe helper checks light and dark (S3-10)
docker-compose.e2e.yml              E2E override of docker-compose.yml (used by e2e/ npm scripts; mounts the test release T.1, S4-08; Mailpit, S5-11; the password of the E2E users, S5-14)
docker-compose.rehearsal.yml        Throwaway stack of the restore rehearsal on top of docker-compose.yml (own project and network, no fixed host port, database in memory; S6-29), used by scripts/rehearse-restore.mjs
docker-compose.dev.yml              Dev override of docker-compose.yml: mounts the test release folders, so the API activates T.1 (S4-08); Mailpit catches every mail (S5-11)
.github/workflows/ci.yml            CI pipeline, stages 1–8 of TESTING §8.2; codeql.yml = stage 9 CodeQL (S3-03)
scripts/backup/                     Shell scripts of the `backup` compose service (S6-29): schedule.sh, backup.sh, restore.sh; self-test scripts/backup-restore.test.mjs, rehearsal scripts/rehearse-restore.mjs
scripts/                            CI scripts without dependencies: check-invariants.mjs (gate, + check-invariants.test.mjs), check-figma-links.mjs (+ check-figma-links.test.mjs), check-i18n.mjs (+ check-i18n.test.mjs); check-release-config.mjs (+ .test.mjs) runs before a production release (S3-21; the legal pages since S6-28); check-icons.mjs (+ check-icons.test.mjs) and the generator subset-material-symbols.sh / .py (S3-25); check-api-notices.mjs (+ .test.mjs; CI stage 2) with the reviewed texts in api-notices/ (S4-27); smoke-dev-server.mjs (+ .test.mjs; CI stage 1, needs a running `ng serve` and e2e/node_modules) and check-chunk-cache.mjs (+ .test.mjs; CI stage 6) (S6-07)
docs/                               Project documents (§2); TECHNICAL.md = operations and onboarding, unversioned (backup and restore S6-29, the rest S6-30)
README.md                           Public entry page of the repository: what, status, quick start, documents, licence (S6-30)
```

## 4. Commands in full

The complete command list of `CLAUDE.md` v3 §5, including the manual checks of the API and the operations commands (key ring, catalog releases, platform roles) that v4 lists only here:

```bash
cp .env.example .env                      # once; fill in the secrets (never commit .env)
docker network create dokploy-network     # once; the compose file uses it as an external network
docker-compose up --build                 # full stack (DB + API + client), reads .env; client http://localhost:4200 calls the API through its nginx (/api/)
docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.dev.yml up --build   # the same plus the active test catalog release T.1 (S4-08; Development only) and Mailpit (S5-11: every mail at http://localhost:8025); docker compose down -v after a change to the T.1 folder
curl http://localhost:5000/health/ready   # 200 Healthy when the API reaches the database; at most 2 checks at a time (429 beyond, #112); /health/live checks nothing else
# Swagger UI: http://localhost:5000/swagger, only when ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT is Development (404 elsewhere, O-S3-2)
curl 'http://localhost:5000/api/v1/catalog/filter-options?useCase=hr&appTypeIds[]=web'   # filter options of the active release (404 until a release is activated; T.1 with docker-compose.dev.yml); add release=<version> for another
curl 'http://localhost:5000/api/v1/catalog/criteria?useCase=hr&appTypeIds[]=web&productPartIds[]=base&testTypeIds[]=softwareTest'   # criteria with resolved priorities; base must be sent
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/v1/configurator/criteria-list-shown   # 204 with the antiforgery token (see below), 400 antiforgery without it (S5-07); counts one CriteriaListShown (anonymous) in analytics."UsageEvents" — only for a manual check, it changes the metric
# Cookie session (S5-07, client since S5-08): the probe issues the antiforgery token; every state-changing request sends it as X-XSRF-TOKEN. The dev and E2E stacks run plain HTTP (cookies Secure only on HTTPS requests there, owner decision 3a), so http://localhost:5000 works for a manual check; staging and production need HTTPS
curl -i -c jar -b jar https://localhost/api/v1/auth/session   # 200 { "user": null } and Set-Cookie XSRF-TOKEN; signed in: { user: { id, email, roles, permissions }, expiresAt, absoluteExpiresAt, warningLeadSeconds }
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"<address>","password":"<password>"}' https://localhost/api/v1/auth/login   # 204 + session cookie, 400 validation (errors.$) on a wrong address or password
curl -i -c jar -b jar -X POST -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" https://localhost/api/v1/auth/logout   # 204, cookie deleted, new token
curl -i -c jar -b jar -X POST -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" https://localhost/api/v1/auth/session/extend   # 204 with X-Session-Expires-At and X-Session-Absolute-Expires-At; every signed-in request except the probe renews the idle limit the same way (S5-29)
# 🔧 Session:IdleTimeout (00:30:00), Session:AbsoluteTimeout (08:00:00), Session:WarningLead (00:05:00, served by the probe) and Session:SecurityStampValidationInterval (00:00:00 = every request) in appsettings.json
# Registration (S5-12a; S02 at http://localhost:4200/registrieren since S5-12b): 204 for new and known addresses; the confirmation link lands in Mailpit (dev stack) as /registrieren/bestaetigen?token=<token>
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"<address>","password":"<12+ characters>","privacyAcknowledged":true}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/register
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"token":"<token>"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/confirm-email   # 204 confirmed, 400 validation at token
# 🔧 Account:Password:MinLength (12) and Account:Tokens:EmailConfirmationLifetime (1.00:00:00) in appsettings.json
# Password reset (S5-13a; S03 at http://localhost:4200/passwort since S5-13b): 200 { validHours } for every address; the mail (dev stack: Mailpit) links /passwort/neu?token=<token>
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"<address>"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/password-reset
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"token":"<token>","newPassword":"<12+ characters>"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/auth/password-reset/complete   # 204, 400 at newPassword or token
# 🔧 Account:Tokens:PasswordResetLifetime (02:00:00), Account:Lockout:MaxFailedAttempts (5), Account:Lockout:Duration (00:15:00) in appsettings.json
# Organisations (S5-16a): signed in (session cookie + X-XSRF-TOKEN as above); creating needs a confirmed address (403 email-unconfirmed otherwise)
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"name":"Stadt Musterhausen","type":"publicBody"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/organizations   # 201 { id }; type publicBody, company or association
curl -i -c jar -b jar http://localhost:5000/api/v1/organizations/<id>/members   # 200 for a member, 404 for anyone else
curl -i -c jar -b jar -OJ http://localhost:5000/api/v1/account/export   # signed in: the personal data export as ProcureAccess_Daten_<YYYY-MM-DD>.json (S5-27)
curl -i -c jar -b jar -X DELETE -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" http://localhost:5000/api/v1/account   # 204: the signed-in account is deleted and signed out; 409 sole-owner with organizations for the only Owner of an organisation with other members (S5-28a)
curl -i -c jar -b jar -X PUT -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"language":"de","appearance":"dark","motion":"reduced","contrast":"system"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/account/preferences   # 200 the stored settings (S5-25a); GET on the same path answers them, the defaults when never saved
curl -i -c jar -b jar -X PATCH -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"role":"editor"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/organizations/<id>/members/<userId>   # 204; 409 last-owner for the last Owner; DELETE on the same path removes or (own id) leaves
# Invitations (S5-17a): Owner only; the mail (dev stack: Mailpit) links /organisation/einladung?token=<token>, the page that accepts it (S5-17b); S21 invites and lists the open ones
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"email":"<address>","role":"editor"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/organizations/<id>/invitations   # 201 { id, validDays }; GET on the same path lists the open ones, DELETE …/invitations/<invitationId> revokes
curl -i -c jar -b jar -H "X-XSRF-TOKEN: $(grep XSRF-TOKEN jar | awk '{print $7}')" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"token":"<token>"}' http://localhost:5000/api/v1/invitations/accept   # 200 { organizationId }; 403 email-unconfirmed or invitation-mismatch; POST /invitations/view reads the link without sign-in
# 🔧 Organizations:Invitations:Lifetime (14.00:00:00) and :CleanupInterval (01:00:00) in appsettings.json
# Rate limits (S4-21a): 30 per 10 minutes per client IP on the export and on criteria-list-shown (🔧 RateLimits:<policy>:PermitLimit / :Window); the 31st answers 429 rate-limit with retryAfterMinutes; registration 5 per hour per client IP, confirmation resend 5 per hour per signed-in user (S5-12a); password reset 5 per hour per client IP and 3 per hour per address (S5-13a); invitations 5 per hour per signed-in user (S5-17a); sign-in 30 per 10 minutes per client IP and 10 per hour per address, organisation creation 5 per day per signed-in user (S6-26)
# Anonymous export (S4-19): the body lists every criterion of the selection once by revisionId (take them from the criteria answer); active release only; CSV (S4-20a) and XLSX (S4-20b)
curl -i -X POST http://localhost:5000/api/v1/exports -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"format":"csv","selection":{"releaseVersion":"T.1","appTypeIds":["web"],"productPartIds":["base"],"testTypeIds":[]},"criteria":[{"criterionRevisionId":<id>,"isIncluded":true}]}'

# API (run from API/API.ProcureAccess). Secrets come only from environment variables:
export ConnectionStrings__ProcureAccess="Host=localhost;Port=5432;Database=ProcureAccessDb;Username=postgres;Password=<from .env>"
# Mail (S5-11): Development may send without credentials or TLS; with the dev stack's Mailpit: export Email__Smtp__Host=localhost Email__Smtp__Port=1025 Email__Smtp__Security=None
# Every other environment needs Email__Smtp__Username / Email__Smtp__Password (compose: SMTP_USERNAME / SMTP_PASSWORD) or the API does not start
dotnet build && dotnet run
dotnet ef migrations add <Name> --project ../../DAL/DAL.ProcureAccess --startup-project . --context ApplicationDBContext
dotnet ef database update     --project ../../DAL/DAL.ProcureAccess --startup-project . --context ApplicationDBContext   # the API applies all contexts on startup anyway
dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes --project ../../DAL/DAL.ProcureAccess --startup-project . --context ApplicationDBContext   # exit 0 = model matches the last migration (OPS-03)
# Module contexts (ADR-014), e.g. Platform: same commands with the module project, the context and the output folder.
# EF generates the migration class `public`: make it `internal` (ARC-03). Moving a table out of the legacy context: ADR-014 rule 4
dotnet ef migrations add <Name> --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform --startup-project . --context PlatformDbContext --output-dir Persistence/Migrations
dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform --startup-project . --context PlatformDbContext
# Catalog (S3-12): the same with --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Catalog --context CatalogDbContext --output-dir Persistence/Migrations
# IdentityAccess (S3-24): the same with --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.IdentityAccess --context IdentityAccessDbContext --output-dir Persistence/Migrations
# Configurator (S5-19): the same with --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Configurator --context ConfiguratorDbContext --output-dir Persistence/Migrations
# Workspace (S6-11): the same with --project ../../src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Workspace --context WorkspaceDbContext --output-dir Persistence/Migrations

# Key-ring certificate (S4-26): required outside Development and E2E, the API does not start without it. Commands in .env.example;
# set the base64 value as DATA_PROTECTION_CERTIFICATE (compose) or DataProtection__Certificate. Keep the PFX apart from the database backups.

# Catalog release import (S3-16): validates catalog/releases/<version>/ and stores it as a published, inactive release.
# Exit code 0 done, 1 refused (database unchanged, every error with file and JSON path), 2 usage. Never migrates: start the API once first.
export Catalog__ReleasesPath=../../catalog/releases   # with dotnet run from API/API.ProcureAccess; the image uses /app/catalog/releases
dotnet run -- catalog import <version>
# Check every release folder without a database, as CI stage 3 does (S4-05; .NET SDK only, no Docker):
dotnet test --project tests/ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit --filter-class "ProcureAccess.Tests.Unit.Catalog.Releases.CatalogReleaseFoldersTests"
# 2026.1 has no priorities yet (O-1): import it only into a throwaway database until they are in its folder (S3-27)
docker compose run --rm api catalog import <version>   # compose stack: the folders ship with the API image (rebuild it after adding one)
# Catalog release activation (S3-17): makes an imported release the active one; the running API serves it from its next request.
# Refuses (exit 1, every gap listed) a release with a criterion without catalog-default priority or a test type without its PROC- criterion.
dotnet run -- catalog activate <version>
docker compose run --rm api catalog activate <version>

# Platform roles (S4-25, SPEC R-ADM-03): PlatformAdmin, CatalogEditor, Moderator; users by e-mail address.
# Exit code 0 done (also when nothing had to change), 1 refused (unknown address, pending migrations), 2 usage. Applies from the user's next sign-in (docs/TECHNICAL.md §5.2).
dotnet run -- roles list [<email>]
dotnet run -- roles grant <email> <role>
dotnet run -- roles revoke <email> <role>
docker compose run --rm api roles grant <email> PlatformAdmin

# Client (run from CLIENT/CLIENT.ProcureAccess)
npm install && npm start                  # http://localhost:4200
npm run build:release
npx ng test                               # Vitest; watch mode in a terminal, single run without one (CI)
npm run test:coverage                     # single run (also in a terminal), plus coverage summary and lcov report in coverage/procure-access/ (models/ folders excluded)
npm run lint                              # ESLint: template rules incl. accessibility + type-aware TypeScript rules (CI stage 1)

# .NET SDK (S5-03): global.json pins it (10.0.404); dotnet refuses another feature band. Install: curl -sSL https://dot.net/v1/dotnet-install.sh | bash -s -- --version 10.0.404 --install-dir ~/.dotnet, then export PATH=$HOME/.dotnet:$PATH DOTNET_ROOT=$HOME/.dotnet
# Backend tests (run from the repository root). Microsoft.Testing.Platform (global.json) needs --solution
dotnet test --solution ProcureAccess.sln   # integration tests need a running Docker (Testcontainers starts postgres:17-alpine)
#   coverage as in CI: add --coverlet --coverlet-output-format cobertura --coverlet-include "[API.ProcureAccess]*" (one per production assembly, see ci.yml)

# Formatting as checked in CI stage 1 (run from the repository root)
dotnet format ProcureAccess.sln --verify-no-changes   # whitespace, style and analyzers on every project (CI runs the three sub-commands)
dotnet build ProcureAccess.sln -warnaserror -warnnotaserror:NU1901,NU1902,NU1903,NU1904   # as in CI stage 2: any build warning fails

# E2E + axe (run from e2e/; Node from .nvmrc). Needs .env like the dev stack; stop the dev stack first (same ports)
npm install && npx playwright install chromium firefox webkit   # once
npm run stack:up                          # E2E stack: fresh in-memory database with the test release T.1 active (S4-08), the E2E users of e2e/support/users.ts (S5-14) and the E2E organisation (S5-16b), waits until the client answers and /health/ready is 200
npx playwright test                       # all browsers; e.g. --project=chromium for one; register.spec.ts reads mails from Mailpit (E2E_MAILPIT_URL, default http://localhost:8025)
npm run stack:down                        # stop and remove the E2E stack
npm run typecheck && npm run lint         # CI stage 1: TypeScript check of e2e/, ESLint on e2e/ and scripts/*.mjs (needs npm install in e2e/)

# Invariant gate and Figma link check (Node, no dependencies; run from anywhere)
node scripts/check-invariants.mjs         # CI stage 7: every `active` invariant of the highest docs/TESTING_v*.md needs a test that runs with INV:<ID> (+ trait in .NET)
node scripts/check-figma-links.mjs        # CI stage 1: built mapped components and pages carry their @figma tags, components only at their mapped path; files not built yet are skipped
node --test scripts/check-figma-links.test.mjs   # self-test of the Figma link check (CI stage 1)
node scripts/check-i18n.mjs               # CI stage 1: the client de.json (AREAS), the Export texts src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Export/Texts/de.json (EXPORT_AREAS) and the e-mail texts src/ProcureAccess.Modules.Platform/Email/Texts/de.json (EMAIL_AREAS, S5-11) match the UX copy; every template key resolves
node scripts/check-i18n.mjs --write       # copy new or changed UX copy texts of those areas into the three files, then check
node --test scripts/check-i18n.test.mjs   # self-test of the i18n check (CI stage 1)
node --test scripts/check-invariants.test.mjs   # self-test of the invariant gate (CI stage 1; the gate itself runs in stage 7)
node scripts/check-release-config.mjs     # before a production release only: fails while a 🔧 placeholder (criteria-suggestion channel, O-6) is set in environment.prod.ts
node --test scripts/check-release-config.test.mjs   # self-test of the release check (CI stage 1)
node scripts/check-icons.mjs              # CI stage 1: the icon list matches the icons the client uses, and the font subset matches the list and the pinned package
node --test scripts/check-icons.test.mjs  # self-test of the icon check (CI stage 1)
node scripts/smoke-dev-server.mjs [url] [paths…]   # CI stage 1 (S6-07): with `ng serve` running, opens each path in Chrome; fails on a 5xx, a page error or no <h1>; CHROME_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium locally
node scripts/check-chunk-cache.mjs [url]   # CI stage 6 (S6-07): main, styles and one lazy chunk of a running client carry Cache-Control immutable
node --test scripts/smoke-dev-server.test.mjs scripts/check-chunk-cache.test.mjs   # their self-tests (CI stage 1)
node scripts/check-api-notices.mjs        # CI stage 2, after dotnet build: API/API.ProcureAccess/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.txt matches the NuGet packages the API image ships, and their licences are allowed
node scripts/check-api-notices.mjs --write   # after a NuGet update (Dependabot too) or an SDK update: regenerate the notices file with the SDK of global.json, then review its diff
node scripts/check-api-notices.mjs --write --deps deps.json   # the same from a built image: docker run --rm --entrypoint cat <api image> /app/API.ProcureAccess.deps.json > deps.json
node --test scripts/check-api-notices.test.mjs   # self-test of the notices check (CI stage 1)
node --test scripts/backup-restore.test.mjs   # self-test of the backup and restore scripts against fake PostgreSQL tools (CI stage 1, S6-29)
node scripts/rehearse-restore.mjs         # restore rehearsal on a throwaway stack (Docker, openssl; not in CI; may run beside the dev or E2E stack; docs/TECHNICAL.md §7.5)
docker compose run --rm backup sh /opt/backup/backup.sh   # one backup now (S6-29; staging and production run it nightly with COMPOSE_PROFILES=backup)
docker compose run --rm backup sh /opt/backup/restore.sh /backups/<dump> <new database>   # only into a new database; then raise CONCURRENCY_ETAG_EPOCH and set DB_NAME (docs/TECHNICAL.md §7.4)
scripts/subset-material-symbols.sh        # after a change to the icon list or a material-symbols update: regenerate the subset (Docker; needs the client's node_modules)
```
